==================== FILE: C:\Users\Dat\OneDrive\Documents\YoloHome\Backend\src\app.js ====================

const express = require('express');
const cors = require('cors');
const helmet = require('helmet');
const morgan = require('morgan');
const config = require('./config');
const { notFound, errorHandler } = require('./middlewares/errorMiddleware');

const authRoutes = require('./routes/auth');
const homeRoutes = require('./routes/home');
const memberRoutes = require('./routes/member');
const deviceRoutes = require('./routes/device');
const doorRoutes = require('./routes/door');
const iotRouter = require('./routes/iot');
const aiRouter = require('./routes/ai');
const notificationRouter = require('./routes/notification');
const activityLogRouter = require('./routes/activityLog');

const app = express();

// 1. Gáº¯n cÃ¡c middleware chung
app.use(helmet());
app.use(cors());
app.use(express.json());
app.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true }));


if (config.nodeEnv !== 'test') {
  app.use(morgan('dev'));
}

// 2. Endpoint kiá»ƒm tra sá»©c khá»e há»‡ thá»‘ng
app.get('/health', (req, res) => {
  res.status(200).json({ status: 'ok' });
});

// Sau nÃ y cÃ¡c route chÃ­nh (/api/auth, /api/homes...) sáº½ Ä‘Æ°á»£c mount táº¡i Ä‘Ã¢y
app.use('/api/auth', authRoutes);
app.use('/api/homes', homeRoutes);
app.use('/api/homes/:homeId/members', memberRoutes);
app.use('/api/homes/:homeId/devices', deviceRoutes);
app.use('/api/homes/:homeId/doors', doorRoutes);
app.use('/api/homes/:homeId/iot', iotRouter);
app.use('/api/homes/:homeId/ai', aiRouter);
app.use('/api/notifications', notificationRouter);
app.use('/api/homes/:homeId/activity-logs', activityLogRouter);
// 3. Xá»­ lÃ½ 404 vÃ  Error Handler (báº¯t buá»™c Ä‘áº·t cuá»‘i cÃ¹ng)
app.use(notFound);
app.use(errorHandler);

module.exports = app;


==================== FILE: C:\Users\Dat\OneDrive\Documents\YoloHome\Backend\src\config.js ====================

require('dotenv').config();

const requiredEnvVars = ['JWT_SECRET', 'DB_PASSWORD', 'MQTT_USERNAME', 'MQTT_KEY'];

for (const envVar of requiredEnvVars) {
  if (!process.env[envVar]) {
    throw new Error(`[CONFIG ERROR] Biáº¿n mÃ´i trÆ°á»ng ${envVar} báº¯t buá»™c pháº£i cÃ³ trong file .env!`);
  }
}

const config = {
  port: parseInt(process.env.PORT, 10) || 3000,
  nodeEnv: process.env.NODE_ENV || 'development',
  jwtSecret: process.env.JWT_SECRET,
  iotApiKey: process.env.IOT_API_KEY || 'default_iot_key',
  db: {
    host: process.env.DB_HOST || 'localhost',
    user: process.env.DB_USER || 'root',
    password: process.env.DB_PASSWORD,
    database: process.env.DB_NAME || 'smart_home',
    port: parseInt(process.env.DB_PORT, 10) || 3306,
    charset: 'utf8mb4',
    timezone: 'Z',
    waitForConnections: true,
    connectionLimit: 10,
    queueLimit: 0,
  },
  mqtt: {
    brokerUrl: process.env.MQTT_BROKER_URL || 'mqtt://io.adafruit.com:1883',
    username: process.env.MQTT_USERNAME.trim(),
    key: process.env.MQTT_KEY.trim(),
  },
};

module.exports = config;

const mysql = require('mysql2/promise');
const config = require('./config');

// Khá»Ÿi táº¡o Connection Pool sá»­ dá»¥ng trá»±c tiáº¿p config.db
const pool = mysql.createPool(config.db);

/**
 * HÃ m há»— trá»£ gá»i Stored Procedure cÃ³ tham sá»‘ IN vÃ  OUT an toÃ n
 * @param {string} procName - TÃªn Stored Procedure
 * @param {Array} inParams - Danh sÃ¡ch tham sá»‘ IN
 * @param {Array} outParamNames - Danh sÃ¡ch tÃªn tham sá»‘ OUT (vÃ­ dá»¥: ['p_decision'])
 */
async function callProc(procName, inParams = [], outParamNames = []) {
  const connection = await pool.getConnection();
  try {
    // 1. Táº¡o placeholder cho IN vÃ  OUT
    const inPlaceholders = inParams.map(() => '?');
    const outPlaceholders = outParamNames.map((name) => `@${name}`);
    const allPlaceholders = [...inPlaceholders, ...outPlaceholders].join(', ');

    // 2. Thá»±c thi CALL procedure
    const callSql = `CALL ${procName}(${allPlaceholders})`;
    const [results] = await connection.query(callSql, inParams);

    // 3. Náº¿u khÃ´ng cÃ³ biáº¿n OUT, tráº£ vá» káº¿t quáº£ truy váº¥n
    if (outParamNames.length === 0) {
      return results;
    }

    // 4. Láº¥y giÃ¡ trá»‹ cÃ¡c biáº¿n OUT (Äáº£m báº£o cÃ³ khoáº£ng tráº¯ng quanh AS)
    const selectOutSql = `SELECT ${outParamNames
      .map((name) => `@${name} AS${name}`)
      .join(', ')}`;

    const [outRows] = await connection.query(selectOutSql);
    const outParams = outRows && outRows.length > 0 ? outRows[0] : {};

    return {
      results,
      outParams,
      ...outParams,
    };
  } finally {
    connection.release();
  }
}

module.exports = {
  pool,
  callProc,
};

const http = require('http');
const { Server } = require('socket.io');
const app = require('./app');
const config = require('./config');
const { initMQTT } = require('./services/mqttService');

// 1. Khá»Ÿi táº¡o HTTP server bá»c Express App
const server = http.createServer(app);

// 2. Cáº¥u hÃ¬nh WebSocket (Socket.IO) vá»›i CORS
const io = new Server(server, {
  cors: {
    origin: '*',
    methods: ['GET', 'POST', 'PATCH', 'DELETE'],
    credentials: true,
  },
});

// 3. Quáº£n lÃ½ káº¿t ná»‘i thá»i gian thá»±c qua Socket.IO
io.on('connection', (socket) => {
  console.log(`[Socket.IO] Client káº¿t ná»‘i thÃ nh cÃ´ng: ${socket.id}`);

  // Client tham gia phÃ²ng theo tá»«ng nhÃ  (vÃ­ dá»¥: client gá»­i sá»± kiá»‡n join_home vá»›i homeId = 1)
  socket.on('join_home', (homeId) => {
    const roomName = `home_${homeId}`;
    socket.join(roomName);
    console.log(`[Socket.IO] Socket ${socket.id} Ä‘Ã£ vÃ o phÃ²ng: ${roomName}`);
  });

  // Client rá»i phÃ²ng khi thoÃ¡t mÃ n hÃ¬nh nhÃ 
  socket.on('leave_home', (homeId) => {
    const roomName = `home_${homeId}`;
    socket.leave(roomName);
    console.log(`[Socket.IO] Socket ${socket.id} Ä‘Ã£ rá»i phÃ²ng: ${roomName}`);
  });

  socket.on('disconnect', (reason) => {
    console.log(`[Socket.IO] Client ngáº¯t káº¿t ná»‘i: ${socket.id} (LÃ½ do: ${reason})`);
  });
});

// 4. Khá»Ÿi Ä‘á»™ng MQTT Service vÃ  truyá»n instance socket.io vÃ o Ä‘á»ƒ broadcast dá»¯ liá»‡u telemetry
initMQTT(io);

// 5. Khá»Ÿi cháº¡y Server
const PORT = config.port || 3000;
server.listen(PORT, () => {
  console.log(`Server YoloHome Ä‘ang cháº¡y táº¡i: http://localhost:${PORT}`);
  console.log(`Health check sáºµn sÃ ng táº¡i: http://localhost:${PORT}/health`);
});

// 6. Xá»­ lÃ½ táº¯t á»©ng dá»¥ng an toÃ n (Graceful Shutdown)
const handleShutdown = () => {
  console.log('\nÄang Ä‘Ã³ng cÃ¡c káº¿t ná»‘i vÃ  dá»«ng server...');
  server.close(() => {
    console.log('Server Ä‘Ã£ dá»«ng an toÃ n.');
    process.exit(0);
  });
};

process.on('SIGINT', handleShutdown);
process.on('SIGTERM', handleShutdown);

==================== FILE: C:\Users\Dat\OneDrive\Documents\YoloHome\Backend\src\middlewares\auth.js ====================

const jwt = require('jsonwebtoken');
const config = require('../config');
const { pool } = require('../db');

async function auth(req, res, next) {
  const authHeader = req.headers.authorization;
  if (!authHeader || !authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
    return res.status(401).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'YÃªu cáº§u token xÃ¡c thá»±c há»£p lá»‡'
    });
  }

  const token = authHeader.split(' ')[1];

  try {
    const decoded = jwt.verify(token, config.jwtSecret);

    // Kiá»ƒm tra tÃ i khoáº£n cÃ³ cÃ²n active trong DB khÃ´ng
    const [users] = await pool.query(
      'SELECT User_ID, Name, Email, Display_name, Account_state FROM `User` WHERE User_ID = ? LIMIT 1',
      [decoded.userId]
    );

    if (users.length === 0) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'TÃ i khoáº£n khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i'
      });
    }

    const user = users[0];
    if (user.Account_state.toLowerCase() !== 'active') {
      return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'TÃ i khoáº£n hiá»‡n Ä‘ang bá»‹ khÃ³a'
      });
    }

    req.user = user;
    next();
  } catch (err) {
    return res.status(401).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Token khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡ hoáº·c Ä‘Ã£ háº¿t háº¡n'
    });
  }
}

module.exports = auth;


==================== FILE: C:\Users\Dat\OneDrive\Documents\YoloHome\Backend\src\middlewares\errorMiddleware.js ====================

// src/middlewares/errorMiddleware.js

function notFound(req, res, next) {
  const error = new Error(`KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y Ä‘Æ°á»ng dáº«n - ${req.originalUrl}`);
  res.status(404);
  next(error);
}

function errorHandler(err, req, res, next) {
  let statusCode = res.statusCode === 200 ? 500 : res.statusCode;
  let message = err.message || 'Lá»—i mÃ¡y chá»§ ná»™i bá»™';

  // 1. Lá»—i logic chá»§ Ä‘á»™ng tá»« MySQL Trigger/Procedure (SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000')
  if (err.sqlState === '45000') {
    statusCode = 400;
    message = err.sqlMessage || message;
  } 
  // 2. Lá»—i trÃ¹ng láº·p dá»¯ liá»‡u (Unique constraint - ER_DUP_ENTRY)
  else if (err.code === 'ER_DUP_ENTRY') {
    statusCode = 409;
    message = 'Dá»¯ liá»‡u Ä‘Ã£ tá»“n táº¡i trong há»‡ thá»‘ng';
  } 
  // 3. Lá»—i vi pháº¡m khÃ³a ngoáº¡i (Foreign key constraint)
  else if (err.code === 'ER_NO_REFERENCED_ROW_2' || err.code === 'ER_ROW_IS_REFERENCED_2') {
    statusCode = 400;
    message = 'Dá»¯ liá»‡u liÃªn káº¿t khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡ hoáº·c Ä‘ang Ä‘Æ°á»£c tham chiáº¿u';
  }

  res.status(statusCode).json({
    success: false,
    message,
    stack: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? null : err.stack
  });
}

module.exports = {
  notFound,
  errorHandler
};


==================== FILE: C:\Users\Dat\OneDrive\Documents\YoloHome\Backend\src\middlewares\permission.js ====================

const { pool } = require('../db');

/**
 * Kiá»ƒm tra quyá»n háº¡n theo permissionCode
 * @param {string} permissionCode - MÃ£ quyá»n (vÃ­ dá»¥: 'HOME_UPDATE', 'DEVICE_CONTROL')
 */
const requirePermission = (permissionCode) => async (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    // Láº¥y homeId tá»« params, query hoáº·c body
    const homeId = req.params.homeId || req.query.homeId || req.body.homeId;

    if (!homeId) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Thiáº¿u Ä‘á»‹nh danh nhÃ  (homeId) Ä‘á»ƒ kiá»ƒm tra quyá»n'
      });
    }

    const userId = req.user.User_ID;

    // Gá»i hÃ m fn_has_permission trong DB
    const [rows] = await pool.query(
      'SELECT fn_has_permission(?, ?, ?) AS hasPerm',
      [userId, homeId, permissionCode]
    );

    const hasPermission = rows[0]?.hasPerm === 1;

    if (!hasPermission) {
      return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message: `Báº¡n khÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n thá»±c hiá»‡n thao tÃ¡c nÃ y (${permissionCode})`
      });
    }

    next();
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
};

module.exports = requirePermission;


==================== FILE: C:\Users\Dat\OneDrive\Documents\YoloHome\Backend\src\middlewares\validate.js ====================

const { ZodError } = require('zod');

const validate = (schema) => (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    const parsed = schema.parse({
      body: req.body,
      query: req.query,
      params: req.params
    });
    req.body = parsed.body || req.body;
    req.query = parsed.query || req.query;
    req.params = parsed.params || req.params;
    next();
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof ZodError || err.errors) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Dá»¯ liá»‡u Ä‘áº§u vÃ o khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡',
        errors: (err.errors || []).map((e) => ({
          path: e.path.join('.'),
          message: e.message
        }))
      });
    }
    next(err);
  }
};

module.exports = validate;


==================== FILE: C:\Users\Dat\OneDrive\Documents\YoloHome\Backend\src\routes\activityLog.js ====================

const express = require('express');
const router = express.Router({ mergeParams: true });
const auth = require('../middlewares/auth');
const { checkPermission } = require('../middlewares/permission');
const asyncHandler = require('../middlewares/asyncHandler');
const { pool } = require('../db');

/**
 * @route   GET /api/homes/:homeId/activity-logs
 * @desc    Xem lá»‹ch sá»­ hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng cá»§a ngÃ´i nhÃ 
 * @access  Owner/Admin xem toÃ n bá»™ (LOG_VIEW_ALL), ThÃ nh viÃªn xem cá»§a mÃ¬nh (LOG_VIEW_OWN)
 */
router.get(
  '/',
  auth,
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
    const userId = req.user.User_ID;
    const homeId = parseInt(req.params.homeId, 10);

    // 1. Kiá»ƒm tra xem user cÃ³ quyá»n xem toÃ n bá»™ hay chá»‰ xem cá»§a mÃ¬nh
    const [permRows] = await pool.query(
      `SELECT 
         fn_has_permission(?, ?, 'LOG_VIEW_ALL') AS can_view_all,
         fn_has_permission(?, ?, 'LOG_VIEW_OWN') AS can_view_own`,
      [userId, homeId, userId, homeId]
    );

    const canViewAll = permRows[0]?.can_view_all === 1;
    const canViewOwn = permRows[0]?.can_view_own === 1;

    if (!canViewAll && !canViewOwn) {
      return res.status(403).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Báº¡n khÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n xem nháº­t kÃ½ hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng cá»§a nhÃ  nÃ y',
      });
    }

    // 2. Láº¥y dá»¯ liá»‡u lá»‹ch sá»­
    let sql = `
      SELECT 
        al.Activity_Log_ID, 
        al.Entity_Type, 
        al.Action, 
        al.Source, 
        al.Result, 
        al.Time, 
        al.User_ID, 
        al.Home_ID,
        u.Name AS UserName
      FROM Activity_Log al
      LEFT JOIN \`User\` u ON al.User_ID = u.User_ID
      WHERE al.Home_ID = ?
    `;
    const params = [homeId];

    // Náº¿u chá»‰ cÃ³ quyá»n xem cá»§a chÃ­nh mÃ¬nh
    if (!canViewAll && canViewOwn) {
      sql += ' AND al.User_ID = ?';
      params.push(userId);
    }

    sql += ' ORDER BY al.Time DESC LIMIT 50';

    const [rows] = await pool.query(sql, params);

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      data: rows,
    });
  })
);

module.exports = router;


==================== FILE: C:\Users\Dat\OneDrive\Documents\YoloHome\Backend\src\routes\ai.js ====================

const express = require('express');
const router = express.Router({ mergeParams: true });
const { z } = require('zod');

const { pool, callProc } = require('../db');
const asyncHandler = require('../middlewares/asyncHandler');
const auth = require('../middlewares/auth');
const checkPermission = require('../middlewares/permission');
const validate = require('../middlewares/validate');
const { publishCommand } = require('../services/mqttService');

// ==========================================
// 0. HELPER FUNCTIONS
// ==========================================

// Giáº£i mÃ£ an toÃ n tÃªn cá»­a tá»« URL params (há»— trá»£ tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t cÃ³ dáº¥u vÃ  URL encoded)
const getDecodedDoorName = (req) => {
  try {
    return decodeURIComponent(req.params.doorName);
  } catch {
    return req.params.doorName;
  }
};

// TrÃ­ch xuáº¥t an toÃ n biáº¿n OUT tá»« outResult
const extractOutValue = (outResult, paramName) => {
  if (!outResult) return undefined;

  const target = outResult.outParams || (Array.isArray(outResult) ? outResult[0] : outResult);
  if (!target || typeof target !== 'object') return undefined;

  return (
    target[paramName] ??
    target[`AS${paramName}`] ??
    target[`@${paramName}`] ??
    target[`@out_${paramName}`] ??
    target[`out_${paramName}`]
  );
};

// HÃ m tÃ­nh khoáº£ng cÃ¡ch Euclidean giá»¯a 2 vector khuÃ´n máº·t
const calculateEuclideanDistance = (vec1, vec2) => {
  if (!Array.isArray(vec1) || !Array.isArray(vec2) || vec1.length !== vec2.length) {
    return Infinity;
  }
  let sum = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < vec1.length; i++) {
    sum += Math.pow(vec1[i] - vec2[i], 2);
  }
  return Math.sqrt(sum);
};

// Bá»™ phÃ¢n tÃ­ch Ã½ Ä‘á»‹nh giá»ng nÃ³i tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t cÆ¡ báº£n (Rule-based NLP)
const parseVietnameseVoiceIntent = (rawText) => {
  const normalized = (rawText || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  let action = 'UNKNOWN';
  let deviceName = '';
  let payload = {};

  if (normalized.startsWith('báº­t') || normalized.includes('má»Ÿ')) {
    action = 'TURN_ON';
    payload = { state: 'ON' };
    deviceName = normalized
      .replace(/^(báº­t|má»Ÿ)\s+/i, '')
      .replace(/^thiáº¿t bá»‹\s+/i, '')
      .trim();
  } else if (normalized.startsWith('táº¯t') || normalized.includes('Ä‘Ã³ng')) {
    action = 'TURN_OFF';
    payload = { state: 'OFF' };
    deviceName = normalized
      .replace(/^(táº¯t|Ä‘Ã³ng)\s+/i, '')
      .replace(/^thiáº¿t bá»‹\s+/i, '')
      .trim();
  }

  if (deviceName) {
    deviceName = deviceName.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + deviceName.slice(1);
  }

  return { action, deviceName, payload };
};

// 1. ZOD SCHEMAS

const registerFaceSchema = z.object({
  body: z
    .object({
      targetUserId: z.number().int().optional(),
      userId: z.number().int().optional(),
      faceName: z.string().trim().optional(),
      faceData: z.array(z.number()).optional().nullable(),
      validFrom: z.string().optional().nullable(),
      validTo: z.string().optional().nullable(),
    })
    .refine((data) => data.targetUserId !== undefined || data.userId !== undefined, {
      message: 'Cáº§n cung cáº¥p targetUserId hoáº·c userId (sá»‘ nguyÃªn)',
      path: ['targetUserId'],
    }),
});

const updateFaceValiditySchema = z.object({
  body: z.object({
    validFrom: z.string().optional().nullable(),
    validTo: z.string().optional().nullable(),
  }),
});

const faceRecognitionSchema = z.object({
  body: z.object({
    faceProfileId: z.number().int().optional().nullable(),
    label: z.string().trim().max(100).default('unknown'),
    reliability: z.number().min(0).max(100, 'Reliability pháº£i tá»« 0 Ä‘áº¿n 100').default(85.0),
    threshold: z.number().min(0).max(100).default(75.0),
    inputFaceVector: z.array(z.number()).optional().nullable(),
  }),
});

const voiceCommandSchema = z.object({
  body: z.object({
    commandDetail: z.string().trim().min(1, 'Chi tiáº¿t lá»‡nh khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘á»ƒ trá»‘ng'),
    deviceName: z.string().trim().optional().nullable(),
    commandData: z.record(z.any()).optional().nullable(),
    language: z.string().trim().default('vi-VN'),
  }),
});

// ==========================================
// 2. ENDPOINTS QUáº¢N LÃ FACE PROFILE

/**
 * @route   POST /api/homes/:homeId/ai/face-profiles
 * @desc    ÄÄƒng kÃ½ FaceProfile cho thÃ nh viÃªn trong nhÃ  (há»— trá»£ lÆ°u vector khuÃ´n máº·t)
 * @access  YÃªu cáº§u quyá»n FACE_MANAGE
 */
router.post(
  '/face-profiles',
  auth,
  checkPermission('FACE_MANAGE'),
  validate(registerFaceSchema),
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
    const actorId = req.user.User_ID;
    const homeId = parseInt(req.params.homeId, 10);
    const targetUserId = req.body.targetUserId ?? req.body.userId;
    const { validFrom = null, validTo = null, faceData } = req.body;

    const outResult = await callProc(
      'sp_register_face_profile',
      [actorId, homeId, targetUserId, validFrom, validTo],
      ['p_profile_id']
    );

    const profileId = extractOutValue(outResult, 'p_profile_id');

    // LÆ°u chuá»—i vector Face_Data náº¿u client cÃ³ gá»­i lÃªn
    if (profileId && faceData && Array.isArray(faceData)) {
      await pool.query(
        'UPDATE Face_Profile SET Face_Data = ? WHERE Face_Profile_ID = ?',
        [JSON.stringify(faceData), profileId]
      );
    }

    res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'ÄÄƒng kÃ½ há»“ sÆ¡ FaceProfile thÃ nh cÃ´ng',
      data: {
        profileId,
        homeId,
        targetUserId,
        validFrom,
        validTo,
        hasFaceVector: Boolean(faceData && Array.isArray(faceData)),
      },
    });
  })
);

/**

router.get(

  checkPermission('FACE_MANAGE'),
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {

    const [profiles] = await pool.execute(
      `SELECT fp.Face_Profile_ID, fp.User_ID, u.Name, u.Display_name, fp.Valid_From, fp.Valid_To,
              (fp.Face_Data IS NOT NULL) AS hasFaceData
       FROM Face_Profile fp
       JOIN \`User\` u ON u.User_ID = fp.User_ID
       WHERE fp.Home_ID = ?`,
      [homeId]
    );

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      data: profiles,
    });
  })
);

/**

router.put(

    );

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Cáº­p nháº­t thá»i háº¡n FaceProfile thÃ nh cÃ´ng',
    });
  })
);

/**

router.delete(

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'XÃ³a FaceProfile thÃ nh cÃ´ng',
    });
  })
);

// ==========================================
// 3. ENDPOINTS NHáº¬N DIá»†N KHUÃ”N Máº¶T (FACE AI RECOGNITION)
// ==========================================

/**
 * @route   POST /api/homes/:homeId/ai/doors/:doorName/face-recognition
 * @desc    Nháº­n káº¿t quáº£ AI tá»« camera Ä‘á»ƒ má»Ÿ cá»­a, Ä‘á»‘i soÃ¡t qua Stored Procedure
 * @access  Public / Camera IoT Device
 */
router.post(
  '/doors/:doorName/face-recognition',
  validate(faceRecognitionSchema),
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
    const doorName = getDecodedDoorName(req);
    const homeId = parseInt(req.params.homeId, 10);
    let { faceProfileId = null, label, reliability, threshold, inputFaceVector } = req.body;

    // So khá»›p vector khi camera gá»­i raw vector lÃªn
    if (inputFaceVector && Array.isArray(inputFaceVector)) {
      const [profiles] = await pool.query(
        `SELECT fp.Face_Profile_ID, fp.Face_Data, u.Name 
         FROM Face_Profile fp
         JOIN \`User\` u ON fp.User_ID = u.User_ID
         WHERE fp.Home_ID = ?`,
        [homeId]
      );

      let bestMatch = null;
      let minDistance = Infinity;

      for (const p of profiles) {
        if (!p.Face_Data) continue;
        try {
          const savedVec = typeof p.Face_Data === 'string' ? JSON.parse(p.Face_Data) : p.Face_Data;
          const dist = calculateEuclideanDistance(inputFaceVector, savedVec);
          if (dist < minDistance) {
            minDistance = dist;
            bestMatch = p;
          }
        } catch {
          // Bá» qua náº¿u dá»¯ liá»‡u JSON vector lÆ°u lá»—i
        }
      }

      // NgÆ°á»¡ng khoáº£ng cÃ¡ch Euclidean (< 0.6 lÃ  khá»›p)
      if (bestMatch && minDistance < 0.6) {
        faceProfileId = bestMatch.Face_Profile_ID;
        label = bestMatch.Name;
        reliability = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Math.round((1 - minDistance) * 100)));
      } else {
        label = 'unknown';
        reliability = 40.0;
      }
    }

    const outResult = await callProc(
      'sp_record_face_recognition',
      [
        doorName,
        faceProfileId ? Number(faceProfileId) : null,
        label,
        Number(reliability),
        Number(threshold),
      ],
      ['p_decision']
    );

    let decision = extractOutValue(outResult, 'p_decision');
    const outParams = outResult?.outParams || outResult;

    if (!decision && outParams && typeof outParams === 'object') {
      const values = Object.values(outParams);
      if (values.includes('allowed')) decision = 'allowed';
      else if (values.includes('denied')) decision = 'denied';
    }

    if (decision === 'allowed') {
      // Gá»­i lá»‡nh MQTT má»Ÿ chá»‘t cá»­a váº­t lÃ½
      publishCommand(homeId, 0, { action: 'UNLOCK_DOOR', doorName });

      return res.status(200).json({
        success: true,
        message: 'KhuÃ´n máº·t há»£p lá»‡, Ä‘Ã£ má»Ÿ cá»­a',
        decision: 'allowed',
        label,
        reliability,
      });
    }

    return res.status(403).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'KhuÃ´n máº·t khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡ hoáº·c khÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n má»Ÿ cá»­a',
      decision: decision || 'denied',
      label,
      reliability,
    });
  })
);

// ==========================================
// 4. ENDPOINTS Lá»†NH GIá»ŒNG NÃ“I (VOICE COMMAND)
// ==========================================

/**
 * @route   POST /api/homes/:homeId/ai/voice-commands
 * @desc    Xá»­ lÃ½ cÃ¢u lá»‡nh giá»ng nÃ³i, phÃ¢n tÃ­ch Intent vÃ  Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn thiáº¿t bá»‹
 * @access  YÃªu cáº§u quyá»n VOICE_USE
 */
router.post(
  '/voice-commands',
  auth,
  checkPermission('VOICE_USE'),
  validate(voiceCommandSchema),
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
    const userId = req.user.User_ID;
    const homeId = parseInt(req.params.homeId, 10);
    let { commandDetail, deviceName, commandData, language } = req.body;

    if (!deviceName || !commandData) {
      const parsed = parseVietnameseVoiceIntent(commandDetail);
      if (!deviceName) deviceName = parsed.deviceName;
      if (!commandData) commandData = parsed.payload;
    }

    const outResult = await callProc(
      'sp_process_voice_command',
      [userId, homeId, commandDetail, deviceName || '', JSON.stringify(commandData || {}), language],
      ['p_voice_id', 'p_state']
    );

    const voiceId = extractOutValue(outResult, 'p_voice_id');
    const state = extractOutValue(outResult, 'p_state');

    if (state === 'unsupported') {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Lá»‡nh khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c há»— trá»£ hoáº·c thiáº¿t bá»‹ khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i/khÃ´ng Ä‘á»§ quyá»n',
        data: { voiceId, state, commandDetail, deviceName },
      });
    }

    if (state === 'sent') {
      const [devRows] = await pool.query(
        'SELECT Device_ID FROM Device WHERE Home_ID = ? AND Device_Name LIKE ? LIMIT 1',
        [homeId, `%${deviceName}%`]
      );

      if (devRows.length > 0) {
        const targetDeviceId = devRows[0].Device_ID;
        publishCommand(homeId, targetDeviceId, commandData);

        const newWorkingState = commandData?.state === 'ON' ? 'on' : 'off';
        await pool.query(
          'UPDATE Device SET Available_working_State = ? WHERE Device_ID = ?',
          [newWorkingState, targetDeviceId]
        );
      }
    }

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Thá»±c thi lá»‡nh giá»ng nÃ³i thÃ nh cÃ´ng',
      data: {
        voiceId,
        state,
        commandDetail,
        deviceName,
        commandData,
      },
    });
  })
);

module.exports = router;

const express = require('express');
const bcrypt = require('bcryptjs');
const jwt = require('jsonwebtoken');
const { z } = require('zod');
const config = require('../config');
const { pool, callProc } = require('../db');
const asyncHandler = require('../middlewares/asyncHandler');
const validate = require('../middlewares/validate');
const auth = require('../middlewares/auth');

const router = express.Router();

// ==========================================
// 1. KHAI BÃO SCHEMA (ZOD)
// ==========================================
const registerSchema = z.object({
  body: z.object({
    name: z.string().min(1, 'TÃªn khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘á»ƒ trá»‘ng'),
    email: z.string().email('Email khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡'),
    password: z.string().min(6, 'Máº­t kháº©u pháº£i tá»« 6 kÃ½ tá»± trá»Ÿ lÃªn'),
    displayName: z.string().optional()
  })
});

const loginSchema = z.object({
  body: z.object({
    email: z.string().email('Email khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡'),
    password: z.string().min(1, 'Máº­t kháº©u khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘á»ƒ trá»‘ng')
  })
});

// ==========================================
// 2. CÃC ÄÆ¯á»œNG DáºªN (ROUTES)
// ==========================================

// ÄÄƒng kÃ½
router.post('/register', validate(registerSchema), asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const { name, email, password, displayName } = req.body;

  // BÄƒm máº­t kháº©u trÆ°á»›c khi lÆ°u
  const salt = await bcrypt.genSalt(10);
  const hashedPassword = await bcrypt.hash(password, salt);

  // Gá»i stored procedure sp_register_user
  const { outParams } = await callProc('sp_register_user', [
    name,
    email,
    hashedPassword,
    displayName || null
  ], ['p_user_id']);

  res.status(201).json({
    success: true,
    message: 'ÄÄƒng kÃ½ tÃ i khoáº£n thÃ nh cÃ´ng',
    data: { userId: outParams.p_user_id }
  });
}));

// ÄÄƒng nháº­p
router.post('/login', validate(loginSchema), asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const { email, password } = req.body;

  // Láº¥y thÃ´ng tin user theo email
  const [users] = await pool.query('SELECT * FROM `User` WHERE Email = ?', [email.toLowerCase().trim()]);
  const user = users[0];

  // Kiá»ƒm tra tá»“n táº¡i vÃ  so khá»›p máº­t kháº©u báº±ng bcrypt
  if (!user || !(await bcrypt.compare(password, user.Password))) {
    return res.status(401).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Email hoáº·c máº­t kháº©u khÃ´ng chÃ­nh xÃ¡c'
    });
  }

  // Kiá»ƒm tra tráº¡ng thÃ¡i tÃ i khoáº£n
  if (user.Account_state.toLowerCase() !== 'active') {
    return res.status(403).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'TÃ i khoáº£n cá»§a báº¡n hiá»‡n Ä‘ang bá»‹ khÃ³a'
    });
  }

  // Táº¡o JWT Token
  const token = jwt.sign(
    { userId: user.User_ID },
    config.jwtSecret,
    { expiresIn: '7d' } // Token cÃ³ háº¡n 7 ngÃ y
  );

  res.status(200).json({
    success: true,
    message: 'ÄÄƒng nháº­p thÃ nh cÃ´ng',
    data: {
      token,
      user: {
        id: user.User_ID,
        name: user.Name,
        email: user.Email,
        displayName: user.Display_name
      }
    }
  });
}));

// Láº¥y thÃ´ng tin cÃ¡ nhÃ¢n (Cáº§n truyá»n token)
router.get('/me', auth, asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  res.status(200).json({
    success: true,
    data: {
      id: req.user.User_ID,
      name: req.user.Name,
      email: req.user.Email,
      displayName: req.user.Display_name,
      accountState: req.user.Account_state
    }
  });
}));

module.exports = router;

const express = require('express');
const router = express.Router({ mergeParams: true });
const auth = require('../middlewares/auth');
const asyncHandler = require('../middlewares/asyncHandler');
const { pool } = require('../db');
const { publishCommand } = require('../services/mqttService');

/**
 * @route   GET /api/homes/:homeId/devices
 * @desc    Láº¥y danh sÃ¡ch táº¥t cáº£ thiáº¿t bá»‹ cá»§a má»™t ngÃ´i nhÃ 
 */
router.get(
  '/',
  auth,
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
    const homeId = parseInt(req.params.homeId, 10);

    const [rows] = await pool.query(
      `SELECT 
        d.Device_ID,
        d.Device_Name,
        d.Hardware_ID,
        d.Serial_No,
        d.Connection_State,
        d.Last_Connection_Time,
        d.Available_working_State,
        d.Home_ID,
        d.Type_Code,
        d.Controller_ID,
        dt.Type_Name,
        dt.Type_Group,
        dt.Manufacturer
      FROM Device d
      LEFT JOIN Device_Type dt ON d.Type_Code = dt.Type_Code
      WHERE d.Home_ID = ?
      ORDER BY d.Device_ID DESC`,
      [homeId]
    );

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      data: rows,
    });
  })
);

/**
 * @route   POST /api/homes/:homeId/devices
 * @desc    Táº¡o má»›i má»™t thiáº¿t bá»‹ trong nhÃ 
 */
router.post(
  '/',
  auth,
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
    const homeId = parseInt(req.params.homeId, 10);
    const {
      deviceName,
      hardwareId,
      serialNo,
      typeCode,
      controllerId,
      connectionState,
      availableWorkingState,
    } = req.body;

    if (!deviceName || !typeCode) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'deviceName vÃ  typeCode lÃ  báº¯t buá»™c',
      });
    }

    const [result] = await pool.query(
      `INSERT INTO Device (
        Device_Name,
        Hardware_ID,
        Serial_No,
        Connection_State,
        Available_working_State,
        Home_ID,
        Type_Code,
        Controller_ID
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
      [
        deviceName,
        hardwareId || null,
        serialNo || null,
        connectionState || 'connected',
        availableWorkingState || 'ready',
        homeId,
        typeCode,
        controllerId || null,
      ]
    );

    res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Táº¡o thiáº¿t bá»‹ thÃ nh cÃ´ng',
      data: {
        deviceId: result.insertId,
        deviceName,
        homeId,
        typeCode,
      },
    });
  })
);

/**
 * @route   PATCH /api/homes/:homeId/devices/:deviceId
 * @desc    Cáº­p nháº­t thÃ´ng tin hoáº·c tráº¡ng thÃ¡i thiáº¿t bá»‹
 */
router.patch(
  '/:deviceId',
  auth,
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
    const homeId = parseInt(req.params.homeId, 10);
    const deviceId = parseInt(req.params.deviceId, 10);
    const { deviceName, connectionState, availableWorkingState, controllerId } = req.body;

    const fields = [];
    const params = [];

    if (deviceName !== undefined) {
      fields.push('Device_Name = ?');
      params.push(deviceName);
    }
    if (connectionState !== undefined) {
      fields.push('Connection_State = ?');
      params.push(connectionState);
    }
    if (availableWorkingState !== undefined) {
      fields.push('Available_working_State = ?');
      params.push(availableWorkingState);
    }
    if (controllerId !== undefined) {
      fields.push('Controller_ID = ?');
      params.push(controllerId);
    }

    if (fields.length === 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ dá»¯ liá»‡u nÃ o Ä‘Æ°á»£c cung cáº¥p Ä‘á»ƒ cáº­p nháº­t',
      });
    }

    params.push(deviceId, homeId);

    const [result] = await pool.query(
      `UPDATE Device SET ${fields.join(', ')} WHERE Device_ID = ? AND Home_ID = ?`,
      params
    );

    if (result.affectedRows === 0) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Thiáº¿t bá»‹ khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i trong nhÃ  nÃ y',
      });
    }

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Cáº­p nháº­t thiáº¿t bá»‹ thÃ nh cÃ´ng',
    });
  })
);

/**
 * @route   DELETE /api/homes/:homeId/devices/:deviceId
 * @desc    XÃ³a thiáº¿t bá»‹ khá»i nhÃ 
 */
router.delete(
  '/:deviceId',
  auth,
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
    const homeId = parseInt(req.params.homeId, 10);
    const deviceId = parseInt(req.params.deviceId, 10);

    const [result] = await pool.query(
      'DELETE FROM Device WHERE Device_ID = ? AND Home_ID = ?',
      [deviceId, homeId]
    );

    if (result.affectedRows === 0) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y thiáº¿t bá»‹ Ä‘á»ƒ xÃ³a',
      });
    }

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'ÄÃ£ xÃ³a thiáº¿t bá»‹ thÃ nh cÃ´ng',
    });
  })
);

/**
 * @route   POST /api/homes/:homeId/devices/:deviceId/commands
 * @desc    Gá»­i lá»‡nh Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn thiáº¿t bá»‹ (Báº­t/Táº¯t, Ä‘iá»u chá»‰nh cÃ´ng suáº¥t)
 */
router.post(
  '/:deviceId/commands',
  auth,
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
    const userId = req.user.User_ID;
    const homeId = parseInt(req.params.homeId, 10);
    const deviceId = parseInt(req.params.deviceId, 10);
    const { action, payload, sourceType } = req.body;

    if (!action) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Thiáº¿u trÆ°á»ng action (vÃ­ dá»¥: TURN_ON, TURN_OFF, SET_BRIGHTNESS)',
      });
    }

    // 1. XÃ¡c thá»±c thiáº¿t bá»‹ thuá»™c nhÃ 
    const [devRows] = await pool.query(
      'SELECT Device_ID, Device_Name FROM Device WHERE Device_ID = ? AND Home_ID = ?',
      [deviceId, homeId]
    );

    if (devRows.length === 0) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Thiáº¿t bá»‹ khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i trong ngÃ´i nhÃ  nÃ y',
      });
    }

    // 2. Ghi nháº­n lá»‡nh vÃ o Device_Command
    const commandPayload = JSON.stringify({ action, ...(payload || {}) });
    const chosenSourceType = ['USER', 'VOICE_COMMAND', 'SCHEDULE', 'SYSTEM'].includes(sourceType)
      ? sourceType
      : 'USER';

    const [cmdResult] = await pool.query(
      `INSERT INTO Device_Command (
        Source_ID,
        Source_Type,
        Data_attached,
        Excute_Time,
        Excute_State,
        Device_ID
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, NOW(), 'EXECUTED', ?)`,
      [userId, chosenSourceType, commandPayload, deviceId]
    );

    publishCommand(homeId, deviceId, { action, payload });
    // 3. Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng Ä‘á»“ng bá»™ tráº¡ng thÃ¡i Available_working_State trong Device
    let newWorkingState = null;
    if (action === 'TURN_ON') newWorkingState = 'on';
    if (action === 'TURN_OFF') newWorkingState = 'off';

    if (newWorkingState) {
      await pool.query(
        'UPDATE Device SET Available_working_State = ? WHERE Device_ID = ?',
        [newWorkingState, deviceId]
      );
    }

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Gá»­i lá»‡nh Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn thÃ nh cÃ´ng',
      data: {
        commandId: cmdResult.insertId,
        deviceId,
        action,
        workingState: newWorkingState,
        executeState: 'EXECUTED',
      },
    });
  })
);

module.exports = router;

const express = require('express');
const router = express.Router({ mergeParams: true });
const { z } = require('zod');

const { pool, callProc } = require('../db');
const asyncHandler = require('../middlewares/asyncHandler');
const auth = require('../middlewares/auth');
const checkPermission = require('../middlewares/permission');
const validate = require('../middlewares/validate');

// Helper giáº£i mÃ£ an toÃ n tÃªn cá»­a (há»— trá»£ tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t cÃ³ dáº¥u vÃ  URL encoded)
const getDecodedDoorName = (req) => {
  try {
    return decodeURIComponent(req.params.doorName);
  } catch {
    return req.params.doorName;
  }
};

// ==========================================
// 1. ZOD SCHEMAS
// ==========================================

const createDoorSchema = z.object({
  body: z.object({
    doorName: z.string().trim().min(1, 'TÃªn cá»­a khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘á»ƒ trá»‘ng').max(100),
    workingState: z.string().trim().max(30).optional().default('enabled'),
  }),
});

const createPinSchema = z.object({
  body: z.object({
    targetUserId: z.number().int({ message: 'User ID pháº£i lÃ  sá»‘ nguyÃªn' }),
    pin: z.string().regex(/^[0-9]{4,8}$/, 'MÃ£ PIN pháº£i gá»“m tá»« 4 Ä‘áº¿n 8 chá»¯ sá»‘'),
    validFrom: z.string().datetime().optional().nullable(),
    validTo: z.string().datetime().optional().nullable(),
  }),
});

const verifyPinSchema = z.object({
  body: z.object({
    pin: z.string().min(1, 'MÃ£ PIN khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘á»ƒ trá»‘ng'),
  }),
});

const changePinSchema = z.object({
  body: z.object({
    credentialId: z.number().int(),
    currentPin: z.string().min(1, 'Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p mÃ£ PIN hiá»‡n táº¡i'),
    newPin: z.string().regex(/^[0-9]{4,8}$/, 'MÃ£ PIN má»›i pháº£i gá»“m 4 Ä‘áº¿n 8 chá»¯ sá»‘'),
    confirmPin: z.string().regex(/^[0-9]{4,8}$/, 'XÃ¡c nháº­n mÃ£ PIN má»›i pháº£i gá»“m 4 Ä‘áº¿n 8 chá»¯ sá»‘'),
  }),
});

const setDoorStateSchema = z.object({
  body: z.object({
    state: z.enum(['locked', 'unlocked'], {
      errorMap: () => ({ message: 'Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i pháº£i lÃ  "locked" hoáº·c "unlocked"' }),
    }),
  }),
});

// ==========================================
// 2. ENDPOINTS
// ==========================================

/**
 * @route   POST /api/homes/:homeId/doors
 * @desc    Táº¡o cá»­a thÃ´ng minh má»›i (báº£ng Smart_Door)
 * @access  YÃªu cáº§u quyá»n DEVICE_MANAGE
 */
router.post(
  '/',
  auth,
  checkPermission('DEVICE_MANAGE'),
  validate(createDoorSchema),
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
    const homeId = parseInt(req.params.homeId, 10);
    const { doorName, workingState } = req.body;

    await pool.execute(
      'INSERT INTO Smart_Door (Smart_Door_Name, Working_State, Lock_Unlock_State, Home_ID) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
      [doorName, workingState, 'Locked', homeId]
    );

    res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Khá»Ÿi táº¡o cá»­a thÃ´ng minh thÃ nh cÃ´ng',
      data: {
        doorName,
        homeId,
        lockState: 'Locked',
        workingState,
      },
    });
  })
);

/**
 * @route   GET /api/homes/:homeId/doors
 * @desc    Láº¥y danh sÃ¡ch cÃ¡c cá»­a thÃ´ng minh trong nhÃ 
 * @access  YÃªu cáº§u quyá»n DEVICE_VIEW
 */
router.get(
  '/',
  auth,
  checkPermission('DEVICE_VIEW'),
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
    const homeId = parseInt(req.params.homeId, 10);

    const [doors] = await pool.execute(
      'SELECT Smart_Door_Name, Working_State, Lock_Unlock_State, Home_ID FROM Smart_Door WHERE Home_ID = ?',
      [homeId]
    );

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      data: doors,
    });
  })
);

/**
 * @route   POST /api/homes/:homeId/doors/:doorName/pins
 * @desc    Cáº¥p mÃ£ PIN má»Ÿ cá»­a cho thÃ nh viÃªn / khÃ¡ch
 * @access  YÃªu cáº§u quyá»n DOOR_MANAGE
 */
router.post(
  '/:doorName/pins',
  auth,
  checkPermission('DOOR_MANAGE'),
  validate(createPinSchema),
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
    const actorId = req.user.User_ID;
    const doorName = getDecodedDoorName(req);
    const { targetUserId, pin, validFrom = null, validTo = null } = req.body;

    // sp_create_access_credential(p_actor_id, p_door, p_target_user_id, p_pin, p_valid_from, p_valid_to, OUT p_credential_id)
    const outResult = await callProc(
      'sp_create_access_credential',
      [actorId, doorName, targetUserId, pin, validFrom, validTo],
      ['p_credential_id']
    );

    const credentialId = outResult?.p_credential_id ?? outResult?.['@p_credential_id'];

    res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Cáº¥p mÃ£ PIN má»Ÿ cá»­a thÃ nh cÃ´ng',
      data: {
        credentialId,
        doorName,
        targetUserId,
      },
    });
  })
);

/**
 * @route   POST /api/homes/:homeId/doors/:doorName/verify-pin
 * @desc    XÃ¡c thá»±c mÃ£ PIN má»Ÿ cá»­a (táº¡i bÃ n phÃ­m / remote cá»­a)
 * @access  Public / Device
 */
router.post(
  '/:doorName/verify-pin',
  validate(verifyPinSchema),
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
    const doorName = getDecodedDoorName(req);
    const { pin } = req.body;

    // sp_verify_door_pin(IN p_door, IN p_pin, OUT p_result, OUT p_user_id)
    const outResult = await callProc(
      'sp_verify_door_pin',
      [doorName, pin],
      ['p_result', 'p_user_id']
    );

    const result = outResult?.p_result ?? outResult?.['@p_result'] ?? outResult?.result;
    const userId = outResult?.p_user_id ?? outResult?.['@p_user_id'] ?? outResult?.user_id;

    if (result === 'locked_out') {
      return res.status(429).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Cá»­a Ä‘ang bá»‹ khÃ³a táº¡m thá»i do nháº­p sai quÃ¡ 5 láº§n trong 10 phÃºt',
        result,
      });
    }

    if (result !== 'success') {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'MÃ£ PIN khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡, Ä‘Ã£ háº¿t háº¡n hoáº·c ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng khÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n má»Ÿ cá»­a',
        result: result || 'failed',
      });
    }

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Má»Ÿ cá»­a thÃ nh cÃ´ng',
      data: {
        result,
        userId,
      },
    });
  })
);

/**
 * @route   PUT /api/homes/:homeId/doors/:doorName/change-pin
 * @desc    Äá»•i mÃ£ PIN má»Ÿ cá»­a
 * @access  YÃªu cáº§u quyá»n DOOR_MANAGE
 */
router.put(
  '/:doorName/change-pin',
  auth,
  checkPermission('DOOR_MANAGE'),
  validate(changePinSchema),
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
    const actorId = req.user.User_ID;
    const { credentialId, currentPin, newPin, confirmPin } = req.body;

    // sp_change_door_pin(IN p_actor_id, IN p_credential_id, IN p_current_pin, IN p_new_pin, IN p_confirm_pin)
    await callProc(
      'sp_change_door_pin',
      [actorId, credentialId, currentPin, newPin, confirmPin],
      []
    );

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Äá»•i mÃ£ PIN má»Ÿ cá»­a thÃ nh cÃ´ng',
    });
  })
);

router.delete(

    await callProc('sp_revoke_credential', [actorId, credentialId], []);

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,

    });

    const { state } = req.body;

    // sp_set_door_state(IN p_actor_id, IN p_door, IN p_state)

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: `ÄÃ£ ${state === 'unlocked' ? 'má»Ÿ khÃ³a' : 'khÃ³a'} cá»­a tá»« xa thÃ nh cÃ´ng`,
      data: {
        doorName,
        state,
      },
    });
  })
);

    const [attempts] = await pool.execute(
      'SELECT Access_ID, Verify_Method, Access_Result, Failure_Reason, User_ID, `Time` FROM Access_Attempt WHERE Smart_Door_Name = ? ORDER BY `Time` DESC LIMIT ?',
      [doorName, limit]
    );

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      data: attempts,
    });
  })
);

const express = require('express');
const { z } = require('zod');
const { pool, callProc } = require('../db');
const asyncHandler = require('../middlewares/asyncHandler');
const validate = require('../middlewares/validate');
const auth = require('../middlewares/auth');
const requirePermission = require('../middlewares/permission');

const router = express.Router();

// Táº¥t cáº£ route cá»§a Home Ä‘á»u yÃªu cáº§u Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p
router.use(auth);

// ==========================================
// 1. KHAI BÃO SCHEMA (ZOD)
// ==========================================
const createHomeSchema = z.object({
  body: z.object({
    homeName: z.string().min(1, 'TÃªn nhÃ  khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘á»ƒ trá»‘ng'),
    address: z.string().optional()
  })
});

const updateHomeSchema = z.object({
  params: z.object({
    homeId: z.string().regex(/^\d+$/, 'homeId pháº£i lÃ  sá»‘ nguyÃªn')
  }),
  body: z.object({
    homeName: z.string().min(1).optional(),
    address: z.string().optional(),
    state: z.string().optional()
  })
});

const homeIdParamSchema = z.object({
  params: z.object({
    homeId: z.string().regex(/^\d+$/, 'homeId pháº£i lÃ  sá»‘ nguyÃªn')
  })
});

// ==========================================
// 2. CÃC ROUTES
// ==========================================

// Táº¡o nhÃ  má»›i
router.post('/', validate(createHomeSchema), asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const { homeName, address } = req.body;
  const ownerId = req.user.User_ID;

  // Gá»i procedure sp_create_home
  const { outParams } = await callProc(
    'sp_create_home',
    [ownerId, homeName, address || null],
    ['p_home_id']
  );

  res.status(201).json({
    success: true,
    message: 'Táº¡o nhÃ  thÃ nh cÃ´ng',
    data: { homeId: outParams.p_home_id }
  });
}));

// Xem danh sÃ¡ch nhÃ  cá»§a ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng Ä‘ang Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p
router.get('/', asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const userId = req.user.User_ID;

  const [homes] = await pool.query(
    `SELECT h.Home_ID, h.Home_Name, h.Address, h.State, h.Time_Home_Created, 
            r.Role_Name, hm.Status AS Member_Status
     FROM Home h
     JOIN Home_Member hm ON h.Home_ID = hm.Home_ID
     JOIN Role r ON hm.Role_ID = r.Role_ID
     WHERE hm.User_ID = ? AND LOWER(hm.Status) = 'active'
       AND (hm.Expired IS NULL OR hm.Expired > NOW())`,
    [userId]
  );

  res.status(200).json({
    success: true,
    data: homes
  });
}));

// Xem chi tiáº¿t má»™t nhÃ 
router.get('/:homeId', validate(homeIdParamSchema), asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const homeId = parseInt(req.params.homeId, 10);
  const userId = req.user.User_ID;

  // Kiá»ƒm tra ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng cÃ³ pháº£i thÃ nh viÃªn cá»§a nhÃ  nÃ y khÃ´ng
  const [memberCheck] = await pool.query(
    'SELECT fn_is_home_member(?, ?) AS isMember',
    [userId, homeId]
  );

  if (!memberCheck[0]?.isMember) {
    return res.status(403).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Báº¡n khÃ´ng pháº£i thÃ nh viÃªn cá»§a ngÃ´i nhÃ  nÃ y'
    });
  }

  const [homes] = await pool.query(
    'SELECT * FROM Home WHERE Home_ID = ? LIMIT 1',
    [homeId]
  );

  if (homes.length === 0) {
    return res.status(404).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y ngÃ´i nhÃ '
    });
  }

  res.status(200).json({
    success: true,
    data: homes[0]
  });
}));

// Cáº­p nháº­t thÃ´ng tin nhÃ  (YÃªu cáº§u quyá»n HOME_UPDATE)
router.put('/:homeId', validate(updateHomeSchema), requirePermission('HOME_UPDATE'), asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const homeId = parseInt(req.params.homeId, 10);
  const { homeName, address, state } = req.body;
  const actorId = req.user.User_ID;

  await callProc('sp_update_home', [
    actorId,
    homeId,
    homeName || null,
    address || null,
    state || null
  ]);

  res.status(200).json({
    success: true,
    message: 'Cáº­p nháº­t thÃ´ng tin nhÃ  thÃ nh cÃ´ng'
  });
}));

// XÃ³a nhÃ  (Chá»‰ chá»§ nhÃ  má»›i Ä‘Æ°á»£c xÃ³a)
router.delete('/:homeId', validate(homeIdParamSchema), asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const homeId = parseInt(req.params.homeId, 10);
  const actorId = req.user.User_ID;

  await callProc('sp_delete_home', [actorId, homeId]);

  res.status(200).json({
    success: true,
    message: 'XÃ³a nhÃ  thÃ nh cÃ´ng'
  });
}));

module.exports = router;

const express = require('express');
const router = express.Router({ mergeParams: true });
const auth = require('../middlewares/auth');
const asyncHandler = require('../middlewares/asyncHandler');
const { pool } = require('../db');

/**
 * @route   GET /api/homes/:homeId/iot/sensors
 * @desc    Láº¥y danh sÃ¡ch toÃ n bá»™ cáº£m biáº¿n trong nhÃ  kÃ¨m thÃ´ng tin thiáº¿t bá»‹ cha
 */
router.get(
  '/sensors',
  auth,
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
    const homeId = parseInt(req.params.homeId, 10);

    const [rows] = await pool.query(
      `SELECT 
        s.Sensor_ID,
        s.Sampling_Cycle,
        s.Working_State,
        s.Data_Type,
        s.Unit,
        s.Device_ID,
        d.Device_Name,
        d.Home_ID
      FROM Sensor s
      INNER JOIN Device d ON s.Device_ID = d.Device_ID
      WHERE d.Home_ID = ?
      ORDER BY s.Sensor_ID DESC`,
      [homeId]
    );

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      data: rows,
    });
  })
);

/**
 * @route   POST /api/homes/:homeId/iot/sensors
 * @desc    Gáº¯n thÃªm má»™t cáº£m biáº¿n (Sensor) vÃ o má»™t Device trong nhÃ 
 */
router.post(
  '/sensors',
  auth,
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
    const homeId = parseInt(req.params.homeId, 10);
    const { deviceId, samplingCycle, dataType, unit, workingState } = req.body;

    if (!deviceId || !dataType || !unit) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'deviceId, dataType vÃ  unit lÃ  báº¯t buá»™c',
      });
    }

    // XÃ¡c thá»±c thiáº¿t bá»‹ thuá»™c nhÃ 
    const [devCheck] = await pool.query(
      'SELECT Device_ID FROM Device WHERE Device_ID = ? AND Home_ID = ?',
      [deviceId, homeId]
    );

    if (devCheck.length === 0) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Thiáº¿t bá»‹ khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i trong ngÃ´i nhÃ  nÃ y',
      });
    }

    const [result] = await pool.query(
      `INSERT INTO Sensor (Sampling_Cycle, Working_State, Data_Type, Unit, Device_ID)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
      [samplingCycle || 10, workingState || 'active', dataType, unit, deviceId]
    );

    res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Táº¡o cáº£m biáº¿n thÃ nh cÃ´ng',
      data: {
        sensorId: result.insertId,
        deviceId,
        dataType,
        unit,
      },
    });
  })
);

/**
 * @route   POST /api/homes/:homeId/iot/sensors/:sensorId/telemetry
 * @desc    Gá»­i dá»¯ liá»‡u Ä‘o Ä‘áº¡c (Telemetry) tá»« cáº£m biáº¿n lÃªn server vÃ  lÆ°u vÃ o Sensor_Record
 */
router.post(
  '/sensors/:sensorId/telemetry',
  auth,
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
    const sensorId = parseInt(req.params.sensorId, 10);
    const { value, dataQuality } = req.body;

    if (value === undefined || value === null) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'Thiáº¿u giÃ¡ trá»‹ Ä‘o Ä‘áº¡c (value)',
      });
    }

    // 1. Kiá»ƒm tra cáº£m biáº¿n cÃ³ tá»“n táº¡i khÃ´ng
    const [sensorRows] = await pool.query(
      'SELECT Sensor_ID, Unit, Data_Type FROM Sensor WHERE Sensor_ID = ?',
      [sensorId]
    );

    if (sensorRows.length === 0) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y cáº£m biáº¿n vá»›i ID tÆ°Æ¡ng á»©ng',
      });
    }

    // 2. ChÃ¨n báº£n ghi Ä‘o Ä‘áº¡c vÃ o Sensor_Record
    const numericValue = parseFloat(value);
    const quality = dataQuality || 'GOOD';

    const [insertResult] = await pool.query(
      `INSERT INTO Sensor_Record (\`Value\`, Data_quality, Recorded_At, Sensor_ID)
       VALUES (?, ?, NOW(), ?)`,
      [numericValue, quality, sensorId]
    );

    res.status(201).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Ghi nháº­n sá»‘ liá»‡u cáº£m biáº¿n thÃ nh cÃ´ng',
      data: {
        recordId: insertResult.insertId,
        sensorId,
        value: numericValue,
        unit: sensorRows[0].Unit,
        dataType: sensorRows[0].Data_Type,
        quality,
      },
    });
  })
);

/**
 * @route   GET /api/homes/:homeId/iot/sensors/:sensorId/records
 * @desc    Láº¥y lá»‹ch sá»­ dá»¯ liá»‡u cáº£m biáº¿n gáº§n nháº¥t
 */
router.get(
  '/sensors/:sensorId/records',
  auth,
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
    const sensorId = parseInt(req.params.sensorId, 10);
    const limit = parseInt(req.query.limit, 10) || 50;

    const [rows] = await pool.query(
      `SELECT Record_ID, \`Value\`, Data_quality, Recorded_At, Sensor_ID
       FROM Sensor_Record
       WHERE Sensor_ID = ?
       ORDER BY Recorded_At DESC
       LIMIT ?`,
      [sensorId, limit]
    );

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      data: rows,
    });
  })
);

module.exports = router;

// src/routes/member.js
const express = require('express');
const { z } = require('zod');
const { pool, callProc } = require('../db');
const asyncHandler = require('../middlewares/asyncHandler');
const validate = require('../middlewares/validate');
const auth = require('../middlewares/auth');
const requirePermission = require('../middlewares/permission');

const router = express.Router({ mergeParams: true });

router.use(auth);

// ==========================================
// 1. SCHEMA KIá»‚M TRA Dá»® LIá»†U (ZOD)
// ==========================================
const homeIdParamSchema = z.object({
  params: z.object({
    homeId: z.string().regex(/^\d+$/, 'homeId pháº£i lÃ  sá»‘ nguyÃªn')
  })
});

const addMemberSchema = z.object({
  params: z.object({
    homeId: z.string().regex(/^\d+$/, 'homeId pháº£i lÃ  sá»‘ nguyÃªn')
  }),
  body: z.object({
    email: z.string().email('Email thÃ nh viÃªn khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡'),
    roleName: z.enum(['Member', 'Guest'], {
      errorMap: () => ({ message: 'Vai trÃ² chá»‰ cÃ³ thá»ƒ lÃ  Member hoáº·c Guest' })
    }),
    expired: z.string().datetime().optional()
  })
});

const changeRoleSchema = z.object({
  params: z.object({
    homeId: z.string().regex(/^\d+$/, 'homeId pháº£i lÃ  sá»‘ nguyÃªn'),
    userId: z.string().regex(/^\d+$/, 'userId pháº£i lÃ  sá»‘ nguyÃªn')
  }),
  body: z.object({
    roleName: z.enum(['Member', 'Guest'], {
      errorMap: () => ({ message: 'Vai trÃ² chá»‰ cÃ³ thá»ƒ lÃ  Member hoáº·c Guest' })
    }),
    expired: z.string().datetime().optional()
  })
});

const memberParamSchema = z.object({
  params: z.object({
    homeId: z.string().regex(/^\d+$/, 'homeId pháº£i lÃ  sá»‘ nguyÃªn'),
    userId: z.string().regex(/^\d+$/, 'userId pháº£i lÃ  sá»‘ nguyÃªn')
  })
});

// ==========================================
// 2. CÃC ROUTES
// ==========================================

// Láº¥y danh sÃ¡ch thÃ nh viÃªn trong nhÃ 
router.get('/', validate(homeIdParamSchema), asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const homeId = parseInt(req.params.homeId, 10);
  const userId = req.user.User_ID;

  // Kiá»ƒm tra ngÆ°á»i gá»i cÃ³ thuá»™c nhÃ  nÃ y khÃ´ng
  const [memberCheck] = await pool.query(
    'SELECT fn_is_home_member(?, ?) AS isMember',
    [userId, homeId]
  );

  if (!memberCheck[0]?.isMember) {
    return res.status(403).json({
      success: false,
      message: 'Báº¡n khÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n xem thÃ nh viÃªn cá»§a ngÃ´i nhÃ  nÃ y'
    });
  }

  const [members] = await pool.query(
    `SELECT hm.Home_Member_ID, u.User_ID, u.Name, u.Email, u.Display_name,
            r.Role_Name, hm.Status, hm.Joined, hm.Expired
     FROM Home_Member hm
     JOIN \`User\` u ON hm.User_ID = u.User_ID
     JOIN Role r ON hm.Role_ID = r.Role_ID
     WHERE hm.Home_ID = ?`,
    [homeId]
  );

  res.status(200).json({
    success: true,
    data: members
  });
}));

// ThÃªm thÃ nh viÃªn má»›i vÃ o nhÃ  (Cáº§n quyá»n MEMBER_MANAGE)
router.post('/', validate(addMemberSchema), requirePermission('MEMBER_MANAGE'), asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const homeId = parseInt(req.params.homeId, 10);
  const { email, roleName, expired } = req.body;
  const actorId = req.user.User_ID;

  // Chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng ngÃ y giá» sang Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng MySQL (YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS) náº¿u cÃ³
  const formattedExpired = expired ? new Date(expired).toISOString().slice(0, 19).replace('T', ' ') : null;

  await callProc('sp_add_member', [
    actorId,
    homeId,
    email,
    roleName,
    formattedExpired
  ]);

  res.status(201).json({
    success: true,
    message: 'ThÃªm thÃ nh viÃªn thÃ nh cÃ´ng'
  });
}));

// Thay Ä‘á»•i vai trÃ² / thá»i háº¡n thÃ nh viÃªn
router.put('/:userId', validate(changeRoleSchema), requirePermission('MEMBER_MANAGE'), asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const homeId = parseInt(req.params.homeId, 10);
  const targetUserId = parseInt(req.params.userId, 10);
  const { roleName, expired } = req.body;
  const actorId = req.user.User_ID;

  const formattedExpired = expired ? new Date(expired).toISOString().slice(0, 19).replace('T', ' ') : null;

  await callProc('sp_change_member_role', [
    actorId,
    homeId,
    targetUserId,
    roleName,
    formattedExpired
  ]);

  res.status(200).json({
    success: true,
    message: 'Cáº­p nháº­t vai trÃ² thÃ nh viÃªn thÃ nh cÃ´ng'
  });
}));

// XÃ³a thÃ nh viÃªn khá»i nhÃ 
router.delete('/:userId', validate(memberParamSchema), requirePermission('MEMBER_MANAGE'), asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
  const homeId = parseInt(req.params.homeId, 10);
  const targetUserId = parseInt(req.params.userId, 10);
  const actorId = req.user.User_ID;

  await callProc('sp_remove_member', [actorId, homeId, targetUserId]);

  res.status(200).json({
    success: true,
    message: 'XÃ³a thÃ nh viÃªn khá»i nhÃ  thÃ nh cÃ´ng'
  });
}));

module.exports = router;


==================== FILE: C:\Users\Dat\OneDrive\Documents\YoloHome\Backend\src\routes\notification.js ====================

const express = require('express');
const router = express.Router();
const auth = require('../middlewares/auth');
const asyncHandler = require('../middlewares/asyncHandler');
const { pool } = require('../db');

/**
 * @route   GET /api/notifications
 * @desc    Láº¥y danh sÃ¡ch thÃ´ng bÃ¡o cá»§a ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng hiá»‡n táº¡i
 */
router.get(
  '/',
  auth,
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
    const userId = req.user.User_ID;
    const { status } = req.query; // 'Unread', 'Read' hoáº·c undefined (táº¥t cáº£)

    let sql = `
      SELECT Notification_ID, Title, Type, Content, Level, Unread_Read, User_ID
      FROM Notification
      WHERE User_ID = ?
    `;
    const params = [userId];

    if (status && ['Unread', 'Read'].includes(status)) {
      sql += ' AND Unread_Read = ?';
      params.push(status);
    }

    sql += ' ORDER BY Notification_ID DESC LIMIT 50';

    const [rows] = await pool.query(sql, params);

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      data: rows,
    });
  })
);

/**
 * @route   PATCH /api/notifications/:id/read
 * @desc    ÄÃ¡nh dáº¥u má»™t thÃ´ng bÃ¡o lÃ  Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘á»c
 */
router.patch(
  '/:id/read',
  auth,
  asyncHandler(async (req, res) => {
    const userId = req.user.User_ID;
    const notificationId = parseInt(req.params.id, 10);

    const [result] = await pool.query(
      `UPDATE Notification 
       SET Unread_Read = 'Read' 
       WHERE Notification_ID = ? AND User_ID = ?`,
      [notificationId, userId]
    );

    if (result.affectedRows === 0) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        success: false,
        message: 'KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y thÃ´ng bÃ¡o hoáº·c báº¡n khÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n sá»­a thÃ´ng bÃ¡o nÃ y',
      });
    }

    res.status(200).json({
      success: true,
      message: 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘Ã¡nh dáº¥u thÃ´ng bÃ¡o lÃ  Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘á»c',
    });
  })
);

module.exports = router;


==================== FILE: C:\Users\Dat\OneDrive\Documents\YoloHome\Backend\src\services\mqttService.js ====================

const mqtt = require('mqtt');
const config = require('../config');
const { pool } = require('../db');

let mqttClient = null;
let ioInstance = null;

const initMQTT = (io) => {
  ioInstance = io;

  const { brokerUrl, username, key } = config.mqtt;

  // Cáº¥u hÃ¬nh káº¿t ná»‘i Adafruit IO báº¯t buá»™c pháº£i truyá»n username vÃ  key vÃ o password
  const options = {
    clientId: `yolohome_backend_${Math.random().toString(16).slice(3)}`,
    username: username,
    password: key, // Adafruit IO sá»­ dá»¥ng Active Key lÃ m password
    clean: true,
    connectTimeout: 4000,
    reconnectPeriod: 2000,
  };

  mqttClient = mqtt.connect(brokerUrl, options);

  mqttClient.on('connect', () => {
    console.log(`[MQTT] ÄÃ£ káº¿t ná»‘i thÃ nh cÃ´ng tá»›i Adafruit IO: ${brokerUrl}`);

    // Subscribe cÃ¡c feeds trÃªn Adafruit IO cá»§a báº¡n (cáº¥u trÃºc: <username>/feeds/<feed_key>)
    const feedsToSubscribe = [
      `${username}/feeds/sensor-temperature`,
      `${username}/feeds/sensor-humidity`,
      `${username}/feeds/sensor-light`,
      `${username}/feeds/door-status`,
    ];

    feedsToSubscribe.forEach((feed) => {
      mqttClient.subscribe(feed, (err) => {
        if (!err) {
          console.log(`[MQTT] ÄÃ£ subscribe feed: ${feed}`);
        }
      });
    });
  });

  mqttClient.on('message', async (topic, message) => {
    try {
      const rawData = message.toString();
      console.log(`[MQTT IN] Feed: ${topic} - Data: ${rawData}`);

      // BÃ³c tÃ¡ch tÃªn feed: phandat13/feeds/<feed_name>
      const feedName = topic.split('/')[2];

      // Xá»­ lÃ½ dá»¯ liá»‡u cáº£m biáº¿n nhiá»‡t Ä‘á»™ / Ä‘á»™ áº©m tá»« DHT20
      if (feedName === 'sensor-temperature' || feedName === 'sensor-humidity') {
        const val = parseFloat(rawData);
        if (!isNaN(val)) {
          // Mapping Sensor_ID theo cáº¥u hÃ¬nh há»‡ thá»‘ng cá»§a báº¡n (vÃ­ dá»¥ ID 1 lÃ  nhiá»‡t Ä‘á»™, ID 2 lÃ  Ä‘á»™ áº©m)
          const sensorId = feedName === 'sensor-temperature' ? 1 : 2;

          // 1. LÆ°u báº£n ghi vÃ o báº£ng Sensor_Record trong MySQL
          await pool.query(
            'INSERT INTO Sensor_Record (`Value`, Data_quality, Recorded_At, Sensor_ID) VALUES (?, ?, NOW(), ?)',
            [val, 'GOOD', sensorId]
          );

          // 2. Broadcast sá»± kiá»‡n thá»i gian thá»±c tá»›i Socket.IO cho Frontend
          if (ioInstance) {
            ioInstance.to('home_1').emit('sensor_update', {
              feed: feedName,
              sensorId,
              value: val,
              quality: 'GOOD',
              timestamp: new Date(),
            });
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[MQTT Error] Lá»—i xá»­ lÃ½ message MQTT:', err.message);
    }
  });

  mqttClient.on('error', (err) => {
    console.error('[MQTT Error] Káº¿t ná»‘i tháº¥t báº¡i:', err.message);
  });
};

// HÃ m gá»­i lá»‡nh tá»« Backend xuá»‘ng thiáº¿t bá»‹ / cÆ¡ cáº¥u cháº¥p hÃ nh qua Adafruit IO
const publishCommand = (feedKey, commandPayload) => {
  if (!mqttClient || !mqttClient.connected) {
    console.error('[MQTT Error] Client chÆ°a káº¿t ná»‘i, khÃ´ng thá»ƒ gá»­i lá»‡nh');
    return false;
  }

  const topic = `${config.mqtt.username}/feeds/${feedKey}`;
  const payloadString = typeof commandPayload === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(commandPayload) : String(commandPayload);

  mqttClient.publish(topic, payloadString);
  console.log(`[MQTT OUT] ÄÃ£ gá»­i lá»‡nh tá»›i feed ${topic}:`, payloadString);
  return true;
};

module.exports = {
  initMQTT,
  publishCommand,
};

# Aetherium AIoT Dashboard

Cháº¡y qua web server (ES Modules khÃ´ng cháº¡y vá»›i file://):

    python3 -m http.server 8000     # má»Ÿ http://localhost:8000

Trang (hash router `#/monitor`, `#/control`, `#/ai`, `#/history`, `#/settings`):
- `js/pages/monitoring.js` â€” GiÃ¡m sÃ¡t mÃ´i trÆ°á»ng (UC 1.1/2.1)
- `js/pages/control.js`    â€” ÄÃ¨n/quáº¡t/cá»­a, cá»­a máº­t mÃ£ + Ä‘á»•i máº­t mÃ£ (UC 3.1, 3.2)
- `js/pages/ai.js`         â€” Giá»ng nÃ³i (UC 3.3) vÃ  FaceAI (UC 3.4)
- `js/pages/history.js`    â€” Cáº£nh bÃ¡o & lá»‹ch sá»­ theo ngÃ y/tuáº§n/thÃ¡ng
- `js/pages/settings.js`   â€” CÃ i Ä‘áº·t ngÆ°á»¡ng

Logic nghiá»‡p vá»¥ + tráº¡ng thÃ¡i chung: `js/store.js`. Cáº¥u hÃ¬nh: `js/config.js`.
Ná»‘i MQTT tháº­t: thay pháº§n giáº£ láº­p trong `tick()` cá»§a `js/store.js`.
NgÆ°á»¡ng, máº­t mÃ£, lá»‹ch sá»­ Ä‘Æ°á»£c lÆ°u trong localStorage.

// Lá»›p gá»i HTTP API theo "TÃ€I LIá»†U TÃCH Há»¢P FRONTEND - Há»† THá»NG API YOLOHOME".
// Má»i API cÃ³ ðŸ”’ tá»± Ä‘á»™ng gáº¯n header Authorization: Bearer <token>.
import { CONFIG } from './config.js';

const TOKEN_KEY = 'yolohome.token';
export const getToken = () => { try { return localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY); } catch { return null; } };
export const setToken = (t) => {
  try { if (t) localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, t); else localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY); } catch {}
};

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(status, message, body) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'ApiError';
    this.status = status;
    this.body = body;
  }
}

const STATUS_TEXT = {
  400: 'Dá»¯ liá»‡u gá»­i lÃªn khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡',
  401: 'ChÆ°a Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p hoáº·c phiÃªn Ä‘Ã£ háº¿t háº¡n',
  403: 'Báº¡n khÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n thá»±c hiá»‡n thao tÃ¡c nÃ y',
  404: 'KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y dá»¯ liá»‡u',
  409: 'Dá»¯ liá»‡u Ä‘Ã£ tá»“n táº¡i',
  422: 'Dá»¯ liá»‡u khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡',
  429: 'Thao tÃ¡c quÃ¡ nhiá»u láº§n, vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i sau',
  500: 'MÃ¡y chá»§ gáº·p lá»—i',
};

let onUnauthorized = () => {};
export const setUnauthorizedHandler = (fn) => { onUnauthorized = fn; };

// 401 á»Ÿ má»™t API nghiá»‡p vá»¥ (vd. sai mÃ£ PIN cÅ©) chÆ°a cháº¯c lÃ  háº¿t phiÃªn â†’ há»i láº¡i /auth/me.
let tokenCheck = null;
function verifySession() {
  tokenCheck ??= fetch(`${CONFIG.API_BASE_URL}/auth/me`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${getToken()}` } })
    .then((r) => { if (r.status === 401) onUnauthorized(); })
    .catch(() => {})
    .finally(() => { tokenCheck = null; });
}

function messageOf(body, status) {
  if (body && typeof body === 'object') {
    const m = body.message ?? body.error ?? body.msg ?? body.errors;
    if (typeof m === 'string' && m) return m;
    if (Array.isArray(m) && m.length) {
      return m.map((x) => (typeof x === 'string' ? x : x?.msg || x?.message || JSON.stringify(x))).join('; ');
    }
    if (m && typeof m === 'object') return m.message || JSON.stringify(m);
  }
  if (typeof body === 'string' && body && body.length < 200 && !body.trim().startsWith('<')) return body;
  return STATUS_TEXT[status] || `Lá»—i HTTP ${status}`;
}

async function request(method, path, { body, query, auth = true } = {}) {
  const url = new URL(CONFIG.API_BASE_URL.replace(/\/$/, '') + path, location.href);
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(query ?? {})) {
    if (v !== undefined && v !== null && v !== '') url.searchParams.set(k, v);
  }
  const headers = { Accept: 'application/json' };
  if (body !== undefined) headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
  const token = getToken();
  if (auth && token) headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;

  let res;
  try {
    res = await fetch(url, { method, headers, body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body) });
  } catch {
    throw new ApiError(0, `KhÃ´ng káº¿t ná»‘i Ä‘Æ°á»£c tá»›i Backend (${CONFIG.API_BASE_URL})`);
  }
  const text = await res.text();
  let data = null;
  if (text) { try { data = JSON.parse(text); } catch { data = text; } }

  if (!res.ok) {
    if (res.status === 401 && auth && token) {
      if (path === '/auth/me') onUnauthorized(); else verifySession();
    }
    throw new ApiError(res.status, messageOf(data, res.status), data);
  }
  // Má»™t sá»‘ BE tráº£ HTTP 200 kÃ¨m { success: false } â†’ coi nhÆ° bá»‹ tá»« chá»‘i.
  if (data && typeof data === 'object' && data.success === false) {
    throw new ApiError(400, messageOf(data, 400), data);
  }
  return data;
}

const enc = encodeURIComponent;                       // "Cá»­a chÃ­nh" â†’ "C%E1%BB%ADa%20ch%C3%ADnh"
const H = (homeId) => `/homes/${enc(homeId)}`;
const DOOR = (homeId, doorName) => `${H(homeId)}/doors/${enc(doorName)}`;

export const api = {
  // 3.1 Auth
  auth: {
    register: (body) => request('POST', '/auth/register', { body, auth: false }),
    login: (body) => request('POST', '/auth/login', { body, auth: false }),
    me: () => request('GET', '/auth/me'),
  },
  // 3.2 Homes
  homes: {
    list: () => request('GET', '/homes'),
    create: (body) => request('POST', '/homes', { body }),
    get: (homeId) => request('GET', H(homeId)),
    update: (homeId, body) => request('PUT', H(homeId), { body }),
    remove: (homeId) => request('DELETE', H(homeId)),
  },
  // 3.3 Members
  members: {
    list: (homeId) => request('GET', `${H(homeId)}/members`),
    add: (homeId, body) => request('POST', `${H(homeId)}/members`, { body }),
    update: (homeId, userId, body) => request('PUT', `${H(homeId)}/members/${enc(userId)}`, { body }),
    remove: (homeId, userId) => request('DELETE', `${H(homeId)}/members/${enc(userId)}`),
  },
  // 3.4 Devices
  devices: {
    list: (homeId) => request('GET', `${H(homeId)}/devices`),
    create: (homeId, body) => request('POST', `${H(homeId)}/devices`, { body }),
    update: (homeId, deviceId, body) => request('PATCH', `${H(homeId)}/devices/${enc(deviceId)}`, { body }),
    remove: (homeId, deviceId) => request('DELETE', `${H(homeId)}/devices/${enc(deviceId)}`),
    command: (homeId, deviceId, action, payload = {}) => request('POST', `${H(homeId)}/devices/${enc(deviceId)}/commands`,
      { body: { action, payload, sourceType: CONFIG.COMMAND_SOURCE } }),
  },
  // 3.5 Smart door
  doors: {
    list: (homeId) => request('GET', `${H(homeId)}/doors`),
    create: (homeId, doorName) => request('POST', `${H(homeId)}/doors`, { body: { doorName } }),
    issuePin: (homeId, doorName, pin) => request('POST', `${DOOR(homeId, doorName)}/pins`, { body: { pin } }),
    changePin: (homeId, doorName, oldPin, newPin) => request('PUT', `${DOOR(homeId, doorName)}/change-pin`, { body: { oldPin, newPin } }),
    revoke: (homeId, doorName, credentialId) => request('DELETE', `${DOOR(homeId, doorName)}/credentials/${enc(credentialId)}`),
    unlock: (homeId, doorName, state = 'unlocked') => request('POST', `${DOOR(homeId, doorName)}/unlock`, { body: { state } }),
    attempts: (homeId, doorName) => request('GET', `${DOOR(homeId, doorName)}/attempts`),
    verifyPin: (homeId, doorName, pin) => request('POST', `${DOOR(homeId, doorName)}/verify-pin`, { body: { pin }, auth: false }),
  },
  // 3.6 AI
  ai: {
    listFaces: (homeId) => request('GET', `${H(homeId)}/ai/face-profiles`),
    addFace: (homeId, body) => request('POST', `${H(homeId)}/ai/face-profiles`, { body }),
    recognize: (homeId, doorName, inputFaceVector, threshold) => request('POST',
      `${H(homeId)}/ai/doors/${enc(doorName)}/face-recognition`, { body: { inputFaceVector, threshold }, auth: false }),
    voice: (homeId, commandDetail) => request('POST', `${H(homeId)}/ai/voice-commands`, { body: { commandDetail } }),
  },
  // 3.7 IoT telemetry
  iot: {
    sensors: (homeId) => request('GET', `${H(homeId)}/iot/sensors`),
    addSensor: (homeId, body) => request('POST', `${H(homeId)}/iot/sensors`, { body }),
    pushTelemetry: (homeId, sensorId, value, dataQuality = 'GOOD') => request('POST',
      `${H(homeId)}/iot/sensors/${enc(sensorId)}/telemetry`, { body: { value, dataQuality } }),
    records: (homeId, sensorId) => request('GET', `${H(homeId)}/iot/sensors/${enc(sensorId)}/records`),
  },
  // 3.8 Logs & notifications
  logs: {
    activity: (homeId) => request('GET', `${H(homeId)}/activity-logs`),
  },
  notifications: {
    list: (status) => request('GET', '/notifications', { query: { status } }),   // 'Unread' | 'Read' | undefined
    markRead: (id) => request('PATCH', `/notifications/${enc(id)}/read`),
  },
};

import { state, on, initStore, selectHome, logout, refreshHomeData, refreshNotifications } from './store.js';
import { startRouter } from './router.js';
import { startSyncService } from './services/syncService.js';
import { renderTopbar } from './components/topbar.js';
import { toast } from './ui.js';

const topbar = document.getElementById('topbar');
const paintTopbar = () => renderTopbar(topbar);
['auth', 'home', 'homes', 'socket', 'notifications', 'alerts'].forEach((e) => on(e, paintTopbar));

// Sá»± kiá»‡n topbar gáº¯n má»™t láº§n (topbar Ä‘Æ°á»£c váº½ láº¡i nhiá»u láº§n).
topbar.addEventListener('change', async (e) => {

// Chuáº©n hoÃ¡ dá»¯ liá»‡u Backend vá» má»™t dáº¡ng thá»‘ng nháº¥t cho giao diá»‡n.
import { pick, label, toTs, clamp, normText, asList } from './utils/fields.js';

// ---- PhÃ¢n loáº¡i ----
export function sensorKind(dataType = '', unit = '') {
  const s = normText(`${dataType} ${unit}`);
  if (/temp|nhiet|Â°c|celsius/.test(s)) return 'temperature';
  if (/hum|do am|\bam\b|%/.test(s)) return 'humidity';
  if (/light|lux|anh sang|ldr|illum/.test(s)) return 'light';
  if (/motion|pir|chuyen dong/.test(s)) return 'motion';
  return null;
}

const KIND_RE = {
  sensor: /sensor|dht|ldr|pir|cam bien/,
  door: /door|servo|lock|\bcua\b/,
  light: /light|lamp|led|\bden\b/,
  fan: /fan|\bquat\b/,
};
// typeCode Ä‘Æ°á»£c Æ°u tiÃªn (LIGHT_SENSOR â†’ cáº£m biáº¿n); náº¿u khÃ´ng rÃµ má»›i xÃ©t tÃªn (Quáº¡t cá»­a sá»• â†’ quáº¡t).
export function deviceKind(typeCode = '', name = '') {
  const t = normText(typeCode);
  for (const k of ['sensor', 'door', 'light', 'fan']) if (t && KIND_RE[k].test(t)) return k;
  const n = normText(name);
  for (const k of ['light', 'fan', 'sensor', 'door']) if (n && KIND_RE[k].test(n)) return k;
  return 'other';
}

// Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng tá»« BE â†’ { on, speed } (null náº¿u khÃ´ng suy ra cháº¯c cháº¯n Ä‘Æ°á»£c).
export function parseWorking(v) {
  if (v === undefined || v === null || v === '') return null;
  if (typeof v === 'boolean') return { on: v, speed: v ? 100 : 0 };
  if (typeof v === 'number') return { on: v > 0, speed: clamp(v, 0, 100) };
  if (typeof v === 'object') {
    const st = parseWorking(pick(v, 'state', 'status', 'power'));
    const sp = Number(pick(v, 'speed', 'level'));
    if (Number.isFinite(sp)) return { on: sp > 0, speed: clamp(sp, 0, 100) };
    return st;
  }
  const s = String(v).trim();
  if (s.startsWith('{')) { try { return parseWorking(JSON.parse(s)); } catch { return null; } }
  if (/[,;/|]/.test(s)) return null;                         // giá»‘ng danh sÃ¡ch tráº¡ng thÃ¡i â†’ bá» qua
  if (/^-?\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(s)) { const n = Number(s); return { on: n > 0, speed: clamp(n, 0, 100) }; }
  if (/^(on|true|bat|báº­t|open|opened|unlocked|running|active|1)$/i.test(s)) return { on: true, speed: 100 };
  if (/^(off|false|tat|táº¯t|closed|locked|idle|stopped|inactive|0)$/i.test(s)) return { on: false, speed: 0 };
  return null;
}

export function parseOnline(v) {
  if (v === undefined || v === null || v === '') return null;
  if (typeof v === 'boolean') return v;
  const s = String(v).toLowerCase();
  if (/off|disconn|lost|inactive|error|fail|down/.test(s)) return false;
  if (/on|connect|active|ok|alive|up/.test(s)) return true;
  return null;
}

export function parseMotion(v) {
  if (typeof v === 'boolean') return v;
  const n = Number(v);
  if (Number.isFinite(n)) return n > 0;
  return /true|yes|detect|on|co/.test(normText(v));
}

// ---- Thá»±c thá»ƒ ----
export const normUser = (u) => ({
  id: pick(u, 'userId', 'id'),
  name: label(pick(u, 'displayName', 'name', 'userName', 'fullName')) || label(pick(u, 'email')) || 'NgÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng',
  email: label(pick(u, 'email')),
  raw: u,
});

export const normHome = (h) => ({
  id: pick(h, 'homeId', 'id'),
  name: label(pick(h, 'homeName', 'name')) || 'NhÃ  chÆ°a Ä‘áº·t tÃªn',
  address: label(pick(h, 'address')),
  state: label(pick(h, 'state', 'status', 'homeState')),
  role: label(pick(h, 'roleName', 'role', 'memberRole')),
  raw: h,
});

export function normDevice(d, cache = {}) {
  const id = pick(d, 'deviceId', 'id');
  const typeCode = label(pick(d, 'typeCode', 'deviceType', 'type', 'typeName')).toUpperCase();
  const name = label(pick(d, 'deviceName', 'name')) || `Thiáº¿t bá»‹ #${id}`;
  const kind = deviceKind(typeCode, name);
  const workingRaw = pick(d, 'availableWorkingState', 'workingState', 'currentState', 'state');
  const connRaw = pick(d, 'connectionState', 'connectionStatus', 'connection');
  // Æ¯u tiÃªn tráº¡ng thÃ¡i lá»‡nh gáº§n nháº¥t do trÃ¬nh duyá»‡t gá»­i (BE cÃ³ thá»ƒ chÆ°a cáº­p nháº­t availableWorkingState).
  const st = cache[String(id)] ?? parseWorking(workingRaw) ?? { on: false, speed: 0 };
  const speed = kind === 'fan' ? clamp(Number(st.speed ?? (st.on ? 100 : 0)) || 0, 0, 100) : (st.on ? 100 : 0);
  return {
    id, name, typeCode, kind,
    on: kind === 'fan' ? speed > 0 : !!st.on,
    speed,
    online: parseOnline(connRaw),
    connection: label(connRaw),
    working: label(workingRaw),
    controllerId: label(pick(d, 'controllerId', 'controller')),
    raw: d,
  };
}

export function normSensor(s) {
  const dataType = label(pick(s, 'dataType', 'sensorType', 'type'));
  const unit = label(pick(s, 'unit'));
  return {
    id: pick(s, 'sensorId', 'id'),
    deviceId: pick(s, 'deviceId'),
    dataType, unit,
    kind: sensorKind(dataType, unit) ?? sensorKind(label(pick(s, 'feed', 'feedKey', 'sensorName', 'name'))),
    raw: s,
  };
}

export const normRecord = (r) => ({
  ts: toTs(pick(r, 'recordedAt', 'recordTime', 'timestamp', 'createdAt', 'measuredAt', 'time', 'date')),
  value: pick(r, 'value', 'recordValue', 'dataValue', 'reading'),
  quality: label(pick(r, 'dataQuality', 'quality')),
});

export const normCredential = (c) => ({
  id: pick(c, 'credentialId', 'id'),
  type: label(pick(c, 'credentialType', 'type', 'method')) || 'PIN',
  status: label(pick(c, 'status', 'state')),
  ts: toTs(pick(c, 'createdAt', 'issuedAt', 'created')),
  expired: pick(c, 'expired', 'expiresAt', 'expiredAt'),
});

export const normDoor = (d) => ({
  id: pick(d, 'doorId', 'id'),
  name: label(pick(d, 'doorName', 'name')),
  state: label(pick(d, 'state', 'status', 'lockState', 'doorState')),
  credentials: asList(pick(d, 'credentials', 'pins', 'accessCredentials') ?? []).map(normCredential),
  raw: d,
});

export function normMember(m) {
  const u = pick(m, 'user');
  const src = u && typeof u === 'object' ? { ...u, ...m } : m;
  return {
    userId: pick(src, 'userId', 'id'),

    expired: pick(src, 'expired', 'expiredAt', 'expiresAt', 'expiry'),
