    raw: m,
  };
}

const detailText = (v) => (v === undefined ? '' : typeof v === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(v) : String(v));

export function normLog(l) {
  const user = pick(l, 'user', 'actor');
  const uid = pick(l, 'userId', 'actorId');
  return {
    ts: toTs(pick(l, 'createdAt', 'timestamp', 'logTime', 'actionTime', 'time', 'date')),
    actor: label(pick(l, 'userName', 'displayName', 'actorName', 'email'))
      || (user && typeof user === 'object' ? label(user) : label(user))
      || (uid != null ? `NgÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng #${uid}` : 'Há»‡ thá»‘ng'),
    action: label(pick(l, 'action', 'actionType', 'activityType', 'event', 'type')),
    target: label(pick(l, 'targetName', 'deviceName', 'doorName', 'targetType', 'entity')),
    detail: detailText(pick(l, 'detail', 'details', 'description', 'message', 'content', 'metadata')),
    raw: l,
  };
}

export function normAttempt(a) {
  const result = pick(a, 'result', 'status', 'success', 'isSuccess', 'granted');
  const r = normText(label(result));
  return {
    ts: toTs(pick(a, 'attemptTime', 'attemptedAt', 'createdAt', 'timestamp', 'time')),
    ok: result === true || /success|ok|granted|thanh cong|true|unlock|allow/.test(r) ? true
      : result === false || /fail|denied|lock|sai|false|reject|block/.test(r) ? false : null,
    result: label(result),
    method: label(pick(a, 'method', 'accessMethod', 'credentialType', 'type')),
    actor: label(pick(a, 'userName', 'displayName', 'userId')),
    detail: detailText(pick(a, 'detail', 'reason', 'message', 'note')),
    raw: a,
  };
}

export function normNotification(n) {
  const status = label(pick(n, 'status', 'state'));
  return {
    id: pick(n, 'notificationId', 'id'),
    title: label(pick(n, 'title', 'subject', 'notificationType', 'type')) || 'ThÃ´ng bÃ¡o',
    content: detailText(pick(n, 'content', 'message', 'body', 'detail')),
    status,
    read: status ? /^(read|da doc|seen)$/i.test(normText(status)) : !!pick(n, 'isRead', 'read'),
    ts: toTs(pick(n, 'createdAt', 'timestamp', 'sentAt', 'time')),
    raw: n,
  };
}

export const normFaceProfile = (f) => {
  const data = pick(f, 'faceData', 'vector', 'faceVector');
  let dims = null;
  if (Array.isArray(data)) dims = data.length;
  else if (typeof data === 'string' && data.startsWith('[')) { try { dims = JSON.parse(data).length; } catch {} }
  return {
    id: pick(f, 'faceProfileId', 'profileId', 'faceId', 'id'),
    userId: pick(f, 'userId'),
    name: label(pick(f, 'displayName', 'userName', 'name')),
    dims,
    expired: pick(f, 'expired', 'expiresAt', 'expiredAt'),
    ts: toTs(pick(f, 'createdAt', 'registeredAt')),
    raw: f,
  };
};

// ---- Giá»ng nÃ³i: suy ra thay Ä‘á»•i Ä‘á»ƒ pháº£n Ã¡nh lÃªn giao diá»‡n sau khi BE xá»­ lÃ½ xong ----
export function parseVoiceCommand(text) {
  const t = normText(text);
  const on = /\b(bat|mo)\b/.test(t);
  const off = /\b(tat|dung)\b/.test(t);
  if (!on && !off) return null;
  const pct = t.match(/(\d{1,3})\s*(%|phan tram)/);
  if (/\bden\b/.test(t)) return { kind: 'light', on: on && !off };
  if (/\bquat\b/.test(t)) return { kind: 'fan', speed: off && !on ? 0 : pct ? clamp(+pct[1], 0, 100) : 100 };
  return null;
}

import { state, on } from './store.js';
import { getToken } from './api.js';
import { renderSidebar } from './components/sidebar.js';
import { pageTitle, emptyState, linkButton, loadingBlock } from './ui.js';
import { loginPage } from './pages/login.js';
import { homesPage } from './pages/homes.js';
import { membersPage } from './pages/members.js';
import { monitoringPage } from './pages/monitoring.js';
import { controlPage } from './pages/control.js';
import { doorPage } from './pages/door.js';
import { aiPage } from './pages/ai.js';
import { historyPage } from './pages/history.js';
import { notificationsPage } from './pages/notifications.js';
import { settingsPage } from './pages/settings.js';

const ROUTES = {
  login: loginPage, homes: homesPage, members: membersPage, monitor: monitoringPage, control: controlPage,
  door: doorPage, ai: aiPage, history: historyPage, notifications: notificationsPage, settings: settingsPage,
};
const NEEDS_HOME = new Set(['monitor', 'control', 'door', 'ai', 'history', 'members']);
let cleanup = null;
let current = 'monitor';

const noHomeView = () => pageTitle('ChÆ°a chá»n ngÃ´i nhÃ ', 'CÃ¡c chá»©c nÄƒng giÃ¡m sÃ¡t vÃ  Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng theo tá»«ng nhÃ ') + `
  <div class="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200/80 shadow-sm">
    ${emptyState('home_work', 'Báº¡n chÆ°a cÃ³ ngÃ´i nhÃ  nÃ o', 'Táº¡o nhÃ  má»›i (báº¡n sáº½ lÃ  Owner) hoáº·c nhá» chá»§ nhÃ  má»i báº¡n báº±ng email.', linkButton('#/homes', 'Äi tá»›i NhÃ  cá»§a tÃ´i'))}
  </div>`;

const paintSidebar = () => renderSidebar(document.getElementById('sidebar'), current);

function go() {
  const key = location.hash.replace(/^#\/?/, '').split('?')[0] || 'monitor';
  const authed = !!getToken();
  if (!authed && key !== 'login') { location.hash = '#/login'; return; }
  if (authed && key === 'login') { location.hash = '#/monitor'; return; }
  current = ROUTES[key] ? key : 'monitor';

  cleanup?.();
  cleanup = null;
  // Thay #page-root báº±ng pháº§n tá»­ má»›i Ä‘á»ƒ bá» toÃ n bá»™ listener cá»§a trang trÆ°á»›c.
  const old = document.getElementById('page-root');
  const root = old.cloneNode(false);
  old.replaceWith(root);
  document.body.classList.toggle('auth-mode', current === 'login');

  if (current !== 'login' && !state.ready) root.innerHTML = loadingBlock('Äang táº£i phiÃªn lÃ m viá»‡câ€¦');
  else if (NEEDS_HOME.has(current) && state.homeId == null) root.innerHTML = noHomeView();
  else cleanup = ROUTES[current].mount(root) ?? null;

  if (current !== 'login') paintSidebar();
  window.scrollTo(0, 0);
}

export function startRouter() {
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', go);
  on('auth', go);
  on('home', go);
  ['devices', 'sensors', 'notifications', 'socket', 'homes'].forEach((e) => on(e, () => { if (current !== 'login') paintSidebar(); }));
  go();
}

// Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i dÃ¹ng chung + phiÃªn Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p + káº¿t ná»‘i Socket.IO + logic ngÆ°á»¡ng/tá»± Ä‘á»™ng hoÃ¡.
import { api, getToken, setToken, setUnauthorizedHandler } from './api.js';
import { CONFIG, THRESHOLDS, DEFAULT_THRESHOLDS, TIMING } from './config.js';

  normUser, normHome, normDevice, normSensor, normDoor, normRecord, normNotification,

// ---------------- Event bus ----------------
const listeners = {};
export const on = (evt, fn) => { (listeners[evt] ??= new Set()).add(fn); return () => listeners[evt].delete(fn); };
export const emit = (evt) => listeners[evt]?.forEach((fn) => { try { fn(); } catch (e) { console.error(e); } });
// Chá»‰ gá»i fn khi "chá»¯ kÃ½" dá»¯ liá»‡u thay Ä‘á»•i (trÃ¡nh váº½ láº¡i lÃ m máº¥t ná»™i dung Ä‘ang nháº­p trong form).
export function onChange(events, signature, fn) {
  let last = signature();
  const handler = () => { const s = signature(); if (s !== last) { last = s; fn(); } };
  const offs = [].concat(events).map((e) => on(e, handler));
  return () => offs.forEach((off) => off());
}

// ---------------- localStorage ----------------
const LS = {
  home: 'yolohome.homeId',
  thresholds: 'yolohome.thresholds',
  logs: (h) => `yolohome.logs.${h}`,
  mode: (h) => `yolohome.mode.${h}`,
  dev: (h) => `yolohome.devstate.${h}`,
};
const lsGet = (k, fallback) => { try { const v = localStorage.getItem(k); return v == null ? fallback : JSON.parse(v); } catch { return fallback; } };
const lsSet = (k, v) => { try { localStorage.setItem(k, JSON.stringify(v)); } catch {} };

// ---------------- State ----------------
export const AUTO = 'tá»± Ä‘á»™ng';
const emptySensor = () => ({ temperature: null, humidity: null, light: null, motion: null, quality: {}, at: {}, updatedAt: null });
const emptyHistory = () => ({ temperature: [], humidity: [], light: [] });

export const state = {
  ready: false,                 // Ä‘Ã£ khá»Ÿi táº¡o phiÃªn xong
  user: null,
  homes: [],
  homeId: null,

  devices: [],                  // [{ id, name, typeCode, kind, on, speed, online, ... }]
  doors: [],                    // [{ id, name, state, credentials }]
  sensor: emptySensor(),        // giÃ¡ trá»‹ má»›i nháº¥t theo loáº¡i
  history: emptyHistory(),      // chuá»—i {ts, value} Ä‘á»ƒ váº½ biá»ƒu Ä‘á»“
  mode: 'auto',
  buzzer: false,

  alerts: [],                   // cáº£nh bÃ¡o ngÆ°á»¡ng Ä‘ang hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng
  logs: [],                     // nháº­t kÃ½ cá»¥c bá»™ (cáº£nh bÃ¡o, tá»± Ä‘á»™ng hoÃ¡, thao tÃ¡c)
  unread: 0,
  socketConnected: false,
  loading: false,
  errors: {},
};

let socket = null;
let doorTimer = null;
const autoBackoff = {};

const sameId = (a, b) => a != null && b != null && String(a) === String(b);

// ---------------- Nháº­t kÃ½ cá»¥c bá»™ ----------------
function saveLogs() { if (state.homeId != null) lsSet(LS.logs(state.homeId), state.logs.slice(0, 300)); }
export function addLog(type, message) {
  state.logs.unshift({ ts: Date.now(), type, message });
  if (state.logs.length > 300) state.logs.length = 300;
  saveLogs();
  emit('logs');
}
export function clearLogs() { state.logs = []; saveLogs(); emit('logs'); }

// ---------------- PhiÃªn Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p ----------------
setUnauthorizedHandler(() => {
  if (!getToken()) return;
  toast('PhiÃªn Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p Ä‘Ã£ háº¿t háº¡n, vui lÃ²ng Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p láº¡i', 'amber');
  logout();
});

export async function login(email, password) {
  const res = await api.auth.login({ email, password });
  const token = deepFind(res, ['token', 'accessToken', 'jwt']);
  if (!token) throw new Error('MÃ¡y chá»§ khÃ´ng tráº£ vá» token Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p');
  setToken(token);
  await bootstrap();
}

export const register = (body) => api.auth.register(body);

export function logout() {
  if (socket) {
    if (state.homeId != null) socket.emit('leave_home', state.homeId);
    socket.disconnect();
    socket = null;
  }
  setToken(null);
  resetHomeState();
  Object.assign(state, { user: null, homes: [], homeId: null, home: null, logs: [], unread: 0, socketConnected: false, errors: {} });
  emit('auth'); emit('home'); emit('notifications'); emit('socket');
  if (location.hash !== '#/login') location.hash = '#/login';
}

export async function bootstrap() {
  if (!getToken()) { state.ready = true; emit('auth'); return; }
  try {
    state.user = normUser(asObject(await api.auth.me(), 'user'));
  } catch (e) {
    if (e.status === 401) return;           // handler Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Äƒng xuáº¥t
    console.warn('[auth/me]', e.message);
  }
  if (!getToken()) return;
  await loadHomes();
  connectSocket();
  const saved = localStorage.getItem(LS.home);
  const target = state.homes.find((h) => sameId(h.id, saved)) ?? state.homes[0];
  const loadingHome = selectHome(target ? target.id : null);   // pháº§n Ä‘á»“ng bá»™ Ä‘áº·t homeId ngay
  state.ready = true;
  emit('auth');
  refreshNotifications();
  await loadingHome;
}

// ---------------- NhÃ  ----------------
export async function loadHomes() {
  try {
    state.homes = asList(await api.homes.list(), 'homes').map(normHome);
    state.errors.homes = null;
  } catch (e) {
    state.errors.homes = e.message;
  }
  state.home = state.homes.find((h) => sameId(h.id, state.homeId)) ?? null;
  emit('homes');
}

function resetHomeState() {
  Object.assign(state, {
    sensors: [], devices: [], doors: [], sensor: emptySensor(), history: emptyHistory(),
    alerts: [], buzzer: false, doorOpen: false, loading: false, errors: { homes: state.errors.homes },
  });
}

export async function selectHome(id) {
  const prev = state.homeId;
  if (prev != null && socket?.connected) socket.emit('leave_home', prev);
  const home = state.homes.find((h) => sameId(h.id, id));
  state.homeId = home ? home.id : (id ?? null);
  state.home = home ?? null;
  resetHomeState();
  if (state.homeId == null) {
    localStorage.removeItem(LS.home);
    state.logs = [];
    emit('home');
    return;
  }
  localStorage.setItem(LS.home, String(state.homeId));
  state.mode = lsGet(LS.mode(state.homeId), 'auto');
  state.logs = lsGet(LS.logs(state.homeId), []);
  devCache = lsGet(LS.dev(state.homeId), {});
  if (socket?.connected) socket.emit('join_home', state.homeId);
  state.loading = true;
  emit('home');

  const hid = state.homeId;
  api.homes.get(hid).then((res) => {           // GET /homes/:homeId â€“ cáº­p nháº­t chi tiáº¿t
    const item = state.homes.find((h) => sameId(h.id, hid));
    if (!item) return;
    const detail = normHome(asObject(res, 'home'));
    for (const k of ['name', 'address', 'state', 'role']) {
      if (detail[k] && !(k === 'name' && detail[k] === 'NhÃ  chÆ°a Ä‘áº·t tÃªn')) item[k] = detail[k];
    }
    emit('homes');
  }).catch(() => {});
  await refreshHomeData();
}

// Táº£i thiáº¿t bá»‹, cáº£m biáº¿n, cá»­a vÃ  sá»‘ liá»‡u má»›i nháº¥t cá»§a nhÃ  Ä‘ang chá»n.
export async function refreshHomeData({ silent = false } = {}) {
  const hid = state.homeId;
  if (hid == null || !getToken()) return;
  if (!silent) { state.loading = true; emit('loading'); }
  const [devs, sens, doors] = await Promise.allSettled([api.devices.list(hid), api.iot.sensors(hid), api.doors.list(hid)]);
  if (!sameId(hid, state.homeId)) return;
  const take = (r, key, norm) => {
    if (r.status === 'fulfilled') { state.errors[key] = null; return asList(r.value, key).map(norm); }
    state.errors[key] = r.reason?.message || 'Lá»—i táº£i dá»¯ liá»‡u';
    return null;
  };
  const d = take(devs, 'devices', (x) => normDevice(x, devCache));
  if (d) state.devices = d;
  const s = take(sens, 'sensors', normSensor);
  if (s) state.sensors = s;
  const dr = take(doors, 'doors', normDoor);
  if (dr) state.doors = dr.filter((x) => x.name);
  await loadReadings(hid);
  if (!sameId(hid, state.homeId)) return;
  state.loading = false;
  evaluate();
  ['devices', 'sensors', 'doors', 'sensor', 'history', 'loading'].forEach(emit);
}

// GET /iot/sensors/:sensorId/records â†’ giÃ¡ trá»‹ má»›i nháº¥t + chuá»—i lá»‹ch sá»­ cho biá»ƒu Ä‘á»“.
async function loadReadings(hid) {
  const jobs = ['temperature', 'humidity', 'light', 'motion'].map(async (kind) => {
    const sensor = state.sensors.find((x) => x.kind === kind);
    if (!sensor) return [kind, null];
    try { return [kind, asList(await api.iot.records(hid, sensor.id), 'records').map(normRecord)]; }
    catch { return [kind, null]; }
  });
  for (const [kind, recs] of await Promise.all(jobs)) {
    if (!recs?.length || !sameId(hid, state.homeId)) continue;
    const timed = recs.filter((r) => r.ts != null).sort((a, b) => a.ts - b.ts);
    const latest = timed.length ? timed[timed.length - 1] : recs[recs.length - 1];
    if (kind in state.history && timed.length) {
      state.history[kind] = timed
        .map((r) => ({ ts: r.ts, value: Number(r.value) }))
        .filter((p) => Number.isFinite(p.value))
        .slice(-3000);
    }
    const ts = latest.ts ?? Date.now();
    if (!state.sensor.at[kind] || ts >= state.sensor.at[kind]) setReading(kind, latest.value, latest.quality, latest.ts);
  }
}

function setReading(kind, raw, quality, ts) {
  let v;
  if (kind === 'motion') v = parseMotion(raw);
  else {
    v = Number(raw);
    if (!Number.isFinite(v)) return false;
    v = kind === 'light' ? Math.round(v) : Math.round(v * 10) / 10;
  }
  state.sensor[kind] = v;
  state.sensor.quality[kind] = quality || 'GOOD';
  state.sensor.at[kind] = ts ?? null;
  state.sensor.updatedAt = Math.max(state.sensor.updatedAt ?? 0, ts ?? 0) || null;
  return true;
}

// ---------------- Socket.IO ----------------
export const getSocket = () => socket;

function connectSocket() {
  if (socket) return;
  if (typeof window.io !== 'function') { console.warn('[Socket.IO] KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y thÆ° viá»‡n socket.io client.'); return; }
  socket = window.io(CONFIG.SOCKET_URL, { auth: { token: getToken() } });
  socket.on('connect', () => {
    state.socketConnected = true;
    if (state.homeId != null) socket.emit('join_home', state.homeId);   // tá»± vÃ o láº¡i phÃ²ng sau khi reconnect
    emit('socket');
  });
  socket.on('disconnect', () => { state.socketConnected = false; emit('socket'); });
  socket.on('connect_error', () => { state.socketConnected = false; emit('socket'); });
  socket.on('sensor_update', onSensorUpdate);
}

// Payload: { feed: "sensor-temperature", sensorId, value, quality, timestamp }
function onSensorUpdate(data) {
  if (!data || state.homeId == null) return;
  const dataHome = pick(data, 'homeId');
  if (dataHome != null && !sameId(dataHome, state.homeId)) return;
  const sensor = state.sensors.find((s) => sameId(s.id, pick(data, 'sensorId')));
  const kind = sensor?.kind ?? sensorKind(label(pick(data, 'feed')));
  if (!kind) return;
  const ts = toTs(pick(data, 'timestamp')) ?? Date.now();
  if (!setReading(kind, pick(data, 'value'), label(pick(data, 'quality', 'dataQuality')), ts)) return;
  const series = state.history[kind];
  if (series) {
    series.push({ ts, value: Number(state.sensor[kind]) });
    if (series.length > 3000) series.splice(0, series.length - 3000);
  }
  evaluate();
  emit('sensor'); emit('history');
}

// ---------------- Thiáº¿t bá»‹ ----------------
const devLabel = (d) => (d.kind === 'fan' ? `${d.name} ${d.speed}%` : `${d.name} ${d.on ? 'Báº¬T' : 'Táº®T'}`);

function rememberDevice(d) {
  devCache[String(d.id)] = { on: d.on, speed: d.speed };
  if (state.homeId != null) lsSet(LS.dev(state.homeId), devCache);
}

// Gá»­i lá»‡nh qua POST /homes/:homeId/devices/:deviceId/commands
export async function controlDevice(dev, change, source = 'web') {
  if (state.homeId == null || !dev) return;
  if (source !== AUTO && state.mode === 'auto') setMode('manual', 'thao tÃ¡c thá»§ cÃ´ng');
  const prev = { on: dev.on, speed: dev.speed };
  let action;
  let payload;
  if (dev.kind === 'fan') {
    const speed = clamp(Math.round(change.speed ?? (change.on ? (dev.speed || 100) : 0)), 0, 100);
    Object.assign(dev, { speed, on: speed > 0 });
    [action, payload] = speed > 0 ? ['SET_SPEED', { state: 'ON', speed }] : ['TURN_OFF', { state: 'OFF', speed: 0 }];
  } else {
    const onv = !!change.on;
    Object.assign(dev, { on: onv, speed: onv ? 100 : 0 });
    [action, payload] = onv ? ['TURN_ON', { state: 'ON' }] : ['TURN_OFF', { state: 'OFF' }];
  }
  emit('devices');
  try {
    await api.devices.command(state.homeId, dev.id, action, payload);
    rememberDevice(dev);
    addLog('device', `${devLabel(dev)} (${source})`);
  } catch (e) {
    Object.assign(dev, prev);
    emit('devices');
    addLog('device', `Lá»—i gá»­i lá»‡nh ${action} tá»›i ${dev.name}: ${e.message}`);
    throw e;
  }
}

// Sau khi BE xá»­ lÃ½ lá»‡nh giá»ng nÃ³i thÃ nh cÃ´ng: pháº£n Ã¡nh thay Ä‘á»•i lÃªn giao diá»‡n (khÃ´ng gá»­i lá»‡nh láº§n 2).
export function reflectVoiceCommand(text) {
  const cmd = parseVoiceCommand(text);
  if (!cmd) return null;
  const t = normText(text);
  const candidates = state.devices.filter((d) => d.kind === cmd.kind);
  const dev = candidates.find((d) => t.includes(normText(d.name))) ?? candidates[0];
  if (!dev) return null;
  if (state.mode === 'auto') setMode('manual', 'lá»‡nh giá»ng nÃ³i');
  if (cmd.kind === 'fan') Object.assign(dev, { speed: cmd.speed, on: cmd.speed > 0 });
  else Object.assign(dev, { on: cmd.on, speed: cmd.on ? 100 : 0 });
  rememberDevice(dev);
  addLog('device', `${devLabel(dev)} (giá»ng nÃ³i)`);
  emit('devices');
  return dev;
}

export function setMode(mode, reason = '') {
  if (state.mode === mode) return;
  state.mode = mode;
  if (state.homeId != null) lsSet(LS.mode(state.homeId), mode);
  addLog('device', `Chuyá»ƒn sang cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ ${mode === 'auto' ? 'tá»± Ä‘á»™ng' : 'thá»§ cÃ´ng'}${reason ? ` (${reason})` : ''}`);
  emit('devices');
  applyAuto();
}

// ---------------- Cá»­a ----------------
export function markDoorOpened(source, doorName = '') {
  state.doorOpen = true;
  addLog('door', `Cá»­a${doorName ? ` "${doorName}"` : ''} má»Ÿ lÃºc ${hhmm(Date.now())} báº±ng ${source}`);
  emit('door');
  clearTimeout(doorTimer);
  doorTimer = setTimeout(() => { state.doorOpen = false; emit('door'); }, TIMING.DOOR_OPEN_MS);
}

// ---------------- NgÆ°á»¡ng + tá»± Ä‘á»™ng hoÃ¡ ----------------
const fresh = (k) => state.sensor[k] != null && state.sensor.at[k] != null && Date.now() - state.sensor.at[k] < TIMING.FRESH_DATA_MS;

function autoSend(dev, change) {
  if ((autoBackoff[dev.id] ?? 0) > Date.now()) return;
  controlDevice(dev, change, AUTO).catch(() => { autoBackoff[dev.id] = Date.now() + 60e3; });
}

// Cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ tá»± Ä‘á»™ng: Ä‘Ã¨n theo PIR/Ã¡nh sÃ¡ng, quáº¡t theo nhiá»‡t Ä‘á»™. Lá»‡nh Ä‘Æ°á»£c gá»­i tháº­t xuá»‘ng BE.
function applyAuto() {
  if (state.mode !== 'auto' || state.homeId == null) return;
  const s = state.sensor;
  const light = state.devices.find((d) => d.kind === 'light');
  if (light && (fresh('light') || fresh('motion'))) {
    const want = (fresh('motion') && !!s.motion) || (fresh('light') && s.light < THRESHOLDS.LIGHT_MIN);
    if (light.on !== want) autoSend(light, { on: want });
  }
  const fan = state.devices.find((d) => d.kind === 'fan');
  if (fan && fresh('temperature')) {
    const want = s.temperature > THRESHOLDS.TEMP_MAX ? 100 : 0;
    if (fan.speed !== want) autoSend(fan, { speed: want });
  }
}

export function evaluate() {
  const s = state.sensor;
  const t = THRESHOLDS;
  const issues = [];
  if (s.temperature != null && s.temperature > t.TEMP_MAX) {
    issues.push({ key: 'temp', message: `Nhiá»‡t Ä‘á»™ ${s.temperature}Â°C vÆ°á»£t ngÆ°á»¡ng ${t.TEMP_MAX}Â°C` });
  }
  if (s.humidity != null && (s.humidity < t.HUMIDITY_MIN || s.humidity > t.HUMIDITY_MAX)) {
    issues.push({ key: 'hum', message: `Äá»™ áº©m ${s.humidity}% ngoÃ i khoáº£ng ${t.HUMIDITY_MIN}â€“${t.HUMIDITY_MAX}%` });
  }
  const before = new Set(state.alerts.map((a) => a.key));
  issues.forEach((i) => { if (!before.has(i.key)) addLog('alert', `Cáº£nh bÃ¡o: ${i.message} lÃºc ${hhmm(Date.now())}`); });
  state.alerts = issues;
  state.buzzer = issues.some((i) => i.key === 'temp');
  applyAuto();
  emit('alerts');
}

export function applyThresholds(values) {
  Object.assign(THRESHOLDS, values);
  lsSet(LS.thresholds, { ...THRESHOLDS });
  addLog('device', 'Cáº­p nháº­t ngÆ°á»¡ng cáº£nh bÃ¡o');
  evaluate();
  emit('sensor');
}
export const resetThresholds = () => applyThresholds({ ...DEFAULT_THRESHOLDS });

// ---------------- ThÃ´ng bÃ¡o ----------------
export async function refreshNotifications() {
  if (!getToken()) return;
  try {
    const list = asList(await api.notifications.list('Unread'), 'notifications').map(normNotification);
    state.unread = list.filter((n) => !n.read).length;
    emit('notifications');
  } catch { /* khÃ´ng cháº·n giao diá»‡n */ }
}

// ---------------- Khá»Ÿi táº¡o ----------------
export async function initStore() {
  Object.assign(THRESHOLDS, lsGet(LS.thresholds, {}));
  await bootstrap();
}

export async function busy(btn, fn) {
  if (btn) btn.disabled = true;
  try { return await fn(); } finally { if (btn?.isConnected) btn.disabled = false; }
}

const TOAST = { sky: 'bg-sky-600', emerald: 'bg-emerald-600', rose: 'bg-rose-600', amber: 'bg-amber-500', slate: 'bg-slate-700' };
export function toast(msg, tone = 'sky') {
  let box = document.getElementById('toast-box');
  if (!box) {
    box = document.createElement('div');
    box.id = 'toast-box';
    box.className = 'fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-[100] flex flex-col gap-2 max-w-sm';
    document.body.appendChild(box);
  }
  const t = document.createElement('div');
  t.className = `px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg shadow-lg text-sm font-medium text-white ${TOAST[tone] ?? TOAST.sky}`;
  t.textContent = msg;
  box.appendChild(t);
  setTimeout(() => t.remove(), tone === 'rose' ? 5000 : 3000);
}

export const fail = (e, prefix = '') => toast(prefix ? `${prefix}: ${e?.message ?? e}` : String(e?.message ?? e), 'rose');

import { THRESHOLDS, TIMING } from '../config.js';
import { TONES, getTempStatus, getHumidityStatus, getLightStatus } from '../utils/status.js';
import { esc, hhmm } from '../utils/fields.js';

function card({ icon, iconBg, label, badge, value, unit, footLeft, footRight, footClass }) {
  return `
  <div class="bg-white rounded-xl p-5 border border-slate-200/80 shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between hover:border-slate-300 transition-all">
    <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
      <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
        <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg ${iconBg} flex items-center justify-center">
          <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">${icon}</span>
        </div>
        <span class="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500">${label}</span>
      </div>
      ${badge}
    </div>
    <div class="my-4">
      <div class="flex items-baseline gap-1">
        ${unit
          ? `<span class="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 font-['Space_Grotesk'] font-mono-num">${value}</span>
             <span class="text-lg font-medium text-slate-400">${unit}</span>`
          : `<span class="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 font-['Space_Grotesk']">${value}</span>`}
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 border-t border-slate-100 pt-2.5">
      <span class="truncate">${footLeft}</span><span class="${footClass} font-medium whitespace-nowrap">${footRight}</span>
    </div>
  </div>`;
}

const pill = (text, tone) =>
  `<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md ${TONES[tone].badge} text-[11px] font-semibold">${text}</span>`;

const num = (v, digits = 1) => (v == null ? '--' : Number.isInteger(v) ? String(v) : v.toFixed(digits));

// ChÃ¢n tháº»: ID cáº£m biáº¿n trÃªn BE + cháº¥t lÆ°á»£ng dá»¯ liá»‡u + thá»i Ä‘iá»ƒm cáº­p nháº­t.
function sensorFoot(kind, s, sensors) {
  const sensor = sensors.find((x) => x.kind === kind);
  if (!sensor) return 'ChÆ°a Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ cáº£m biáº¿n';
  const q = s.quality[kind];
  const at = s.at[kind] ? ` â€¢ ${hhmm(s.at[kind])}` : '';
  const qText = q && q !== 'GOOD' ? ` â€¢ <span class="text-amber-600 font-semibold">${esc(q)}</span>` : '';
  return `Sensor #${esc(sensor.id)}${at}${qText}`;
}

    : `<span class="inline-flex rounded-full h-2.5 w-2.5 bg-slate-300"></span>`;

      badge: pill(`â‰¤ ${THRESHOLDS.TEMP_MAX}Â°C`, temp.tone),

    }),
  ].join('');
}

==================== FILE: C:\Users\Dat\OneDrive\Documents\YoloHome\Frontend\js\components\trendChart.js ====================

import { THRESHOLDS } from '../config.js';
import { hhmm } from '../utils/fields.js';
import { emptyState } from '../ui.js';

// Biá»ƒu Ä‘á»“ 24 giá» váº½ tá»« GET /iot/sensors/:sensorId/records + Ä‘iá»ƒm má»›i tá»« sensor_update.
const W = 500, TOP = 30, BOTTOM = 165, SPAN = 864e5, BUCKETS = 96;   // 96 nhÃ³m Ã— 15 phÃºt

// Gom Ä‘iá»ƒm theo khoáº£ng 15 phÃºt (láº¥y trung bÃ¬nh) Ä‘á»ƒ biá»ƒu Ä‘á»“ gá»n khi BE tráº£ nhiá»u báº£n ghi.
function bucketize(points, start, end) {
  const size = (end - start) / BUCKETS;
  const sum = new Array(BUCKETS).fill(0), cnt = new Array(BUCKETS).fill(0);
  for (const p of points) {
    if (p.ts < start || p.ts > end || !Number.isFinite(p.value)) continue;
    const i = Math.min(BUCKETS - 1, Math.floor((p.ts - start) / size));
    sum[i] += p.value; cnt[i]++;
  }
  return sum.map((s, i) => (cnt[i] ? { ts: start + (i + 0.5) * size, value: s / cnt[i] } : null)).filter(Boolean);
}

function rangeOf(points, [lo, hi]) {
  if (!points.length) return [lo, hi];
  const vals = points.map((p) => p.value);
  return [Math.min(lo, Math.floor(Math.min(...vals) - 2)), Math.max(hi, Math.ceil(Math.max(...vals) + 2))];
}

const yOf = (v, [min, max]) => BOTTOM - ((v - min) / (max - min)) * (BOTTOM - TOP);

// LÃ m mÆ°á»£t Ä‘Æ°á»ng báº±ng quadratic BÃ©zier qua trung Ä‘iá»ƒm
function smoothPath(pts) {
  let d = `M${pts[0].x.toFixed(1)},${pts[0].y.toFixed(1)}`;
  for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) {
    const p = pts[i - 1], c = pts[i];
    d += ` Q${p.x.toFixed(1)},${p.y.toFixed(1)} ${((p.x + c.x) / 2).toFixed(1)},${((p.y + c.y) / 2).toFixed(1)}`;
  }
  const last = pts[pts.length - 1];
  return d + ` L${last.x.toFixed(1)},${last.y.toFixed(1)}`;
}

function series(pts, color, width) {
  if (!pts.length) return '';
  if (pts.length === 1) return `<circle cx="${pts[0].x}" cy="${pts[0].y}" r="3" fill="${color}"/>`;
  return `<path d="${smoothPath(pts)}" fill="none" stroke="${color}" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-width="${width}" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke"/>`;
}

const legend = (dot, text, cls = 'text-slate-600') =>
  `<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">${dot}<span class="${cls}">${text}</span></div>`;

const header = () => `
  <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 pb-3">
    <div>
      <h2 class="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 font-['Space_Grotesk']">Xu hÆ°á»›ng 24 Giá»</h2>
      <p class="text-xs text-slate-400">Lá»‹ch sá»­ tá»« API records, cáº­p nháº­t trá»±c tiáº¿p qua Socket.IO</p>
    </div>
    <div class="flex items-center gap-4 text-xs font-medium">
      ${legend('<span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-sky-500"></span>', 'Nhiá»‡t Ä‘á»™ (Â°C)')}
      ${legend('<span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-teal-500"></span>', 'Äá»™ áº©m (%)')}
      ${legend('<span class="w-3 border-b-2 border-dashed border-rose-400"></span>', `NgÆ°á»¡ng ${THRESHOLDS.TEMP_MAX}Â°C`, 'text-rose-600 font-semibold')}
    </div>
  </div>`;

export function renderTrendChart(el, history) {
  const all = [...history.temperature, ...history.humidity];
  if (!all.length) {
    el.innerHTML = header() + emptyState('show_chart', 'ChÆ°a cÃ³ dá»¯ liá»‡u lá»‹ch sá»­',
      'Biá»ƒu Ä‘á»“ sáº½ hiá»ƒn thá»‹ khi cáº£m biáº¿n nhiá»‡t Ä‘á»™/Ä‘á»™ áº©m cÃ³ báº£n ghi (records) hoáº·c khi nháº­n Ä‘Æ°á»£c sensor_update.');
    return;
  }
  const now = Date.now();
  const latest = all.reduce((m, p) => Math.max(m, p.ts), 0);
  const end = latest < now - SPAN ? latest : now;          // khÃ´ng cÃ³ dá»¯ liá»‡u 24h gáº§n nháº¥t â†’ lÃ¹i vá» má»‘c cuá»‘i
  const start = end - SPAN;
  const temp = bucketize(history.temperature, start, end);
  const hum = bucketize(history.humidity, start, end);
  const tRange = rangeOf(temp, [20, 40]);
  const hRange = rangeOf(hum, [40, 100]);
  const xOf = (ts) => ((ts - start) / SPAN) * W;
  const tempPts = temp.map((p) => ({ x: xOf(p.ts), y: yOf(p.value, tRange) }));
  const humPts = hum.map((p) => ({ x: xOf(p.ts), y: yOf(p.value, hRange) }));

  let peak = '';
  if (temp.length) {
    const i = temp.reduce((best, p, idx) => (p.value > temp[best].value ? idx : best), 0);
    const pt = tempPts[i];
    peak = `<circle cx="${pt.x}" cy="${pt.y}" r="4" fill="#0284c7"/>
      <circle cx="${pt.x}" cy="${pt.y}" r="8" fill="#0284c7" opacity="0.3"/>
      <text fill="#0f172a" font-family="Space Grotesk" font-size="10" font-weight="700" text-anchor="middle" x="${Math.min(W - 20, Math.max(20, pt.x))}" y="${pt.y - 10}">${temp[i].value.toFixed(1)}Â°C</text>`;
  }
  const yThreshold = yOf(THRESHOLDS.TEMP_MAX, tRange);
  const grid = [30, 75, 120].map((y) => `<line stroke="#f1f5f9" stroke-width="1" x1="0" x2="${W}" y1="${y}" y2="${y}"/>`).join('');
  const isNow = end === now;
  const ticks = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => {
    const ts = start + (i * SPAN) / 6;
    return i === 6 && isNow ? '<span class="text-sky-600 font-semibold">Hiá»‡n táº¡i</span>' : `<span>${hhmm(ts)}</span>`;
  }).join('');

  el.innerHTML = `${header()}
    <div class="relative w-full h-56 my-2">
      <svg class="w-full h-full" preserveAspectRatio="none" viewBox="0 0 ${W} 180">
        ${grid}
        <line stroke="#e2e8f0" stroke-width="1" x1="0" x2="${W}" y1="${BOTTOM}" y2="${BOTTOM}"/>
        ${yThreshold >= TOP && yThreshold <= BOTTOM ? `
          <line stroke="#f87171" stroke-dasharray="4 4" stroke-width="1.2" x1="0" x2="${W}" y1="${yThreshold}" y2="${yThreshold}"/>
          <text fill="#ef4444" font-family="Plus Jakarta Sans" font-size="9" font-weight="600" text-anchor="end" x="${W - 5}" y="${yThreshold - 5}">NgÆ°á»¡ng ${THRESHOLDS.TEMP_MAX}Â°C</text>` : ''}
        ${series(humPts, '#14b8a6', 2)}
        ${series(tempPts, '#0284c7', 2.5)}
        ${peak}
      </svg>
    </div>
    <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400 font-mono text-[11px] pt-1">${ticks}</div>

}

  <pre class="mt-1 text-[11px] bg-slate-900 text-slate-100 rounded p-2 overflow-x-auto max-h-48">${esc(JSON.stringify(res, null, 2))}</pre></details>`);

        ${panel({ title: 'ÄÄƒng kÃ½ há»“ sÆ¡ khuÃ´n máº·t', icon: 'person_add', body: `
          <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div id="face-user-field"></div>
            ${field('Háº¿t háº¡n', `<input id="face-expired" type="date" class="${inputCls}">`, 'Äá»ƒ trá»‘ng náº¿u khÃ´ng giá»›i háº¡n')}
          </div>
          <div class="mt-4">${button('LÆ°u há»“ sÆ¡ khuÃ´n máº·t', { act: 'register-face', icon: 'save' })}</div>
          <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-3">DÃ¹ng vector hiá»‡n táº¡i á»Ÿ khung "Vector khuÃ´n máº·t". Cáº§n quyá»n FACE_MANAGE.</p>` })}
      </div>
      <div id="profiles"></div>`;
    const $ = (s) => root.querySelector(s);

    // ----- Giá»ng nÃ³i -----
    const showVoice = (heard, text, tone, res) => {
      $('#voice-result').innerHTML = `<div class="text-xs text-slate-400">Nháº­n Ä‘Æ°á»£c</div>
        <div class="font-semibold text-slate-800">â€œ${esc(heard || 'â€¦')}â€</div>
        <div class="mt-2 font-bold ${tone}">${esc(text)}</div>${rawJson(res)}`;
    };
    const runVoice = async (text) => {
      const t = text.trim();
      if (!t) return showVoice('', 'KHÃ”NG HIá»‚U â€” cÃ¢u lá»‡nh trá»‘ng', 'text-amber-600');
      showVoice(t, 'Äang gá»­i tá»›i Backendâ€¦', 'text-slate-500');
      try {
        const res = await api.ai.voice(homeId, t);
        if (!alive) return;
        const message = label(pickAny(res, 'message', 'reply', 'responseText')) || label(pickAny(res, 'result', 'detail')) || 'Backend Ä‘Ã£ xá»­ lÃ½ lá»‡nh';
        showVoice(t, message, 'text-emerald-600', res);
        toast(message, 'emerald');
        const dev = reflectVoiceCommand(t);
        if (!dev) addLog('device', `Giá»ng nÃ³i "${t}" â†’ ${message}`);
        setTimeout(() => refreshHomeData({ silent: true }), 800);
      } catch (e) {
        if (!alive) return;
        const text403 = e.status === 403 ? 'Báº¡n khÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n VOICE_USE' : e.message;
        showVoice(t, `KHÃ”NG THá»°C HIá»†N ÄÆ¯á»¢C â€” ${text403}`, 'text-rose-600', e.body);
        addLog('device', `Giá»ng nÃ³i "${t}" â†’ lá»—i: ${text403}`);
      }
    };

    const SR = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
    $('#mic-btn').onclick = () => {
      if (!SR) return showVoice('', 'TrÃ¬nh duyá»‡t khÃ´ng há»— trá»£ nháº­n giá»ng nÃ³i â€” hÃ£y dÃ¹ng Ã´ nháº­p lá»‡nh', 'text-amber-600');
      if (recog) { recog.stop(); return; }
      recog = new SR();
      recog.lang = 'vi-VN';
      recog.onresult = (e) => runVoice(e.results[0][0].transcript);
      recog.onerror = (e) => {
        const map = { network: 'Lá»–I: máº¥t káº¿t ná»‘i Internet', 'not-allowed': 'ChÆ°a cáº¥p quyá»n micro cho trang', 'no-speech': 'KhÃ´ng nghe tháº¥y giá»ng nÃ³i' };
        showVoice('', map[e.error] ?? 'KHÃ”NG HIá»‚U', e.error === 'no-speech' ? 'text-amber-600' : 'text-rose-600');
      };
      recog.onend = () => { recog = null; if (alive) $('#mic-label').textContent = 'Báº¥m Ä‘á»ƒ nÃ³i'; };
      recog.start();
      $('#mic-label').textContent = 'Äang ngheâ€¦ (báº¥m Ä‘á»ƒ dá»«ng)';
    };

    // ----- Vector -----
    const paintVector = () => {
      $('#vec-status').innerHTML = vector
        ? `<div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">${badge(`Vector ${vector.length} chiá»u`, 'emerald')}
             <span class="font-mono text-slate-500 truncate">[${vector.slice(0, 4).map((x) => x.toFixed(3)).join(', ')}, â€¦]</span></div>`
        : `<div class="text-xs text-slate-400">${badge('ChÆ°a cÃ³ vector', 'slate')}</div>`;
    };

    // ----- Danh sÃ¡ch cá»­a / thÃ nh viÃªn -----
    const paintDoors = () => {
      const sel = $('#face-door');
      const keep = sel.value;
      sel.innerHTML = state.doors.length
        ? state.doors.map((d) => `<option value="${esc(d.name)}">${esc(d.name)}</option>`).join('')
        : '<option value="">(chÆ°a cÃ³ cá»­a)</option>';
      if (state.doors.some((d) => d.name === keep)) sel.value = keep;
    };
    const paintUserField = () => {
      $('#face-user-field').innerHTML = members?.length
        ? field('ThÃ nh viÃªn', `<select id="face-user" class="w-full rounded-lg border-slate-200 text-sm">${members.map((m) =>
            `<option value="${esc(m.userId)}" ${state.user && String(state.user.id) === String(m.userId) ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(m.name || m.email)}${m.role ? ` (${esc(m.role)})` : ''}</option>`).join('')}</select>`)
        : field('User ID', `<input id="face-user" type="number" value="${esc(state.user?.id ?? '')}" class="${inputCls} font-mono">`);
    };

    const memberName = (uid) => {
      const m = members?.find((x) => String(x.userId) === String(uid));
      return m ? (m.name || m.email) : null;
    };

    const paintProfiles = () => {
      let body;
      if (profiles === null) body = loadingBlock();
      else if (profilesErr) body = errorBlock(profilesErr);
      else if (!profiles.length) body = emptyState('face', 'ChÆ°a cÃ³ há»“ sÆ¡ khuÃ´n máº·t nÃ o', 'Láº¥y vector rá»“i báº¥m "LÆ°u há»“ sÆ¡ khuÃ´n máº·t".');
      else {
        body = `<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-sm">
          <thead><tr class="text-left text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 border-b border-slate-100">
            <th class="py-2 pr-4 font-semibold">ID</th><th class="py-2 pr-4 font-semibold">NgÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng</th><th class="py-2 pr-4 font-semibold">Vector</th>
            <th class="py-2 pr-4 font-semibold">Háº¿t háº¡n</th><th class="py-2 font-semibold">NgÃ y táº¡o</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>${profiles.map((p) => `<tr class="border-b border-slate-50">
            <td class="py-2 pr-4 font-mono text-xs text-slate-500">${esc(p.id ?? 'â€”')}</td>
            <td class="py-2 pr-4 font-semibold text-slate-800">${esc(p.name || memberName(p.userId) || (p.userId != null ? `User #${p.userId}` : 'â€”'))}</td>
            <td class="py-2 pr-4 text-xs text-slate-500">${p.dims ? `${p.dims} chiá»u` : 'â€”'}</td>
            <td class="py-2 pr-4 text-xs text-slate-600">${p.expired ? fmtDate(p.expired) : 'KhÃ´ng giá»›i háº¡n'}</td>
            <td class="py-2 font-mono text-xs text-slate-500">${p.ts ? fullTime(p.ts) : 'â€”'}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`;
      }
      $('#profiles').innerHTML = panel({
        title: `Há»“ sÆ¡ khuÃ´n máº·t Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½${profiles?.length ? ` (${profiles.length})` : ''}`, icon: 'badge',
        right: button('Táº£i láº¡i', { act: 'reload-profiles', tone: 'secondary', size: 'sm', icon: 'refresh' }), body,
      });
    };

    const loadProfiles = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.ai.listFaces(homeId);
        if (!alive) return;
        profiles = asList(res, 'profiles', 'faceProfiles').map(normFaceProfile);
        profilesErr = null;
      } catch (e) {
        if (!alive) return;
        profiles = [];
        profilesErr = e.status === 403 ? 'Cáº§n quyá»n FACE_MANAGE Ä‘á»ƒ xem há»“ sÆ¡ khuÃ´n máº·t' : `KhÃ´ng táº£i Ä‘Æ°á»£c há»“ sÆ¡: ${e.message}`;
      }
      paintProfiles();
    };
    const loadMembers = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.members.list(homeId);
        if (!alive) return;
        members = asList(res, 'members').map(normMember).filter((m) => m.userId != null);
      } catch { members = []; }
      paintUserField();
      paintProfiles();
    };

    const faceMsg = (html, tone = 'text-slate-600') => { $('#face-result').innerHTML = `<div class="font-semibold ${tone}">${html}</div>`; };

    root.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
      const cmd = e.target.closest('[data-cmd]');
      if (cmd) return runVoice(cmd.dataset.cmd);
      const b = e.target.closest('[data-act]');
      if (!b) return;
      const video = $('#cam');
      switch (b.dataset.act) {
        case 'cam-on':
          if (stream) return;
          try { stream = await startCamera(video); $('#cam-off').classList.add('hidden'); }
          catch (err) { fail(err, 'KhÃ´ng má»Ÿ Ä‘Æ°á»£c camera'); }
          return;
        case 'cam-off':
          stopCamera(stream, video); stream = null; $('#cam-off').classList.remove('hidden');
          return;
        case 'capture':
          if (!stream) return toast('HÃ£y báº­t camera trÆ°á»›c', 'amber');
          await busy(b, async () => {
            toast('Äang phÃ¢n tÃ­ch khuÃ´n máº·tâ€¦ (láº§n Ä‘áº§u cáº§n táº£i mÃ´ hÃ¬nh)', 'sky');
            try {
              const v = await extractDescriptor(video);
              if (!alive) return;
              if (!v) return toast('KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y khuÃ´n máº·t trong khung hÃ¬nh', 'amber');
              vector = v; paintVector(); toast('ÄÃ£ láº¥y vector khuÃ´n máº·t', 'emerald');
            } catch (err) { fail(err, 'KhÃ´ng táº£i Ä‘Æ°á»£c mÃ´ hÃ¬nh nháº­n diá»‡n (kiá»ƒm tra Internet)'); }
          });
          return;
        case 'paste': {
          const v = parseVector($('#vec-input').value);
          if (!v) return toast('Vector khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡ â€” cáº§n máº£ng sá»‘, VD [0.1, -0.2, ...]', 'rose');
          vector = v; paintVector(); toast(`ÄÃ£ nháº­n vector ${v.length} chiá»u`, 'emerald');
          return;
        }
        case 'recognize': {
          const doorName = $('#face-door').value;
          const threshold = parseFloat($('#face-threshold').value);
          if (!vector) return toast('ChÆ°a cÃ³ vector khuÃ´n máº·t', 'amber');
          if (!doorName) return toast('NhÃ  chÆ°a cÃ³ cá»­a â€” táº¡o cá»­a á»Ÿ trang Cá»­a thÃ´ng minh', 'amber');
          await busy(b, async () => {
            faceMsg('Äang so khá»›pâ€¦', 'text-sky-600');
            try {
              const res = await api.ai.recognize(homeId, doorName, vector, Number.isFinite(threshold) ? threshold : FACE.DEFAULT_THRESHOLD);
              if (!alive) return;
              const matched = pickAny(res, 'matched', 'isMatch', 'recognized', 'granted', 'unlocked', 'success');
              const dist = Number(pickAny(res, 'distance', 'minDistance', 'euclideanDistance'));
              const who = label(pickAny(res, 'userName', 'displayName', 'name', 'matchedUser', 'userId', 'matchedUserId'));
              const message = label(pickAny(res, 'message', 'result'));
              const ok = matched !== false && matched !== 'false';
              const info = [who && `NgÆ°á»i: ${esc(memberName(who) ?? who)}`, Number.isFinite(dist) && `Khoáº£ng cÃ¡ch: ${dist.toFixed(4)}`, message && esc(message)].filter(Boolean).join(' Â· ');
              if (ok) {
                markDoorOpened('FaceAI', doorName);
                faceMsg(`KHá»šP â€” Ä‘Ã£ má»Ÿ "${esc(doorName)}"${info ? `<div class="text-xs font-normal text-slate-500 mt-1">${info}</div>` : ''}${rawJson(res)}`, 'text-emerald-600');
              } else {
                faceMsg(`KHÃ”NG KHá»šP${info ? `<div class="text-xs font-normal text-slate-500 mt-1">${info}</div>` : ''}${rawJson(res)}`, 'text-rose-600');
              }
            } catch (err) {
              if (!alive) return;
              faceMsg(`KHÃ”NG Má»ž Cá»¬A â€” ${esc(err.message)}${rawJson(err.body)}`, 'text-rose-600');
              addLog('door', `FaceAI khÃ´ng khá»›p táº¡i "${doorName}": ${err.message}`);
            }
          });
          return;
        }
        case 'register-face': {
          const userId = idValue($('#face-user')?.value);
          const expired = $('#face-expired').value;
          if (!vector) return toast('ChÆ°a cÃ³ vector khuÃ´n máº·t', 'amber');
          if (userId === '' || userId == null) return toast('Chá»n thÃ nh viÃªn', 'amber');
          await busy(b, async () => {
            try {
              const body = { userId, faceData: vector };
              if (expired) body.expired = expired;
              await api.ai.addFace(homeId, body);
              toast('ÄÃ£ lÆ°u há»“ sÆ¡ khuÃ´n máº·t', 'emerald');
              addLog('device', `ÄÄƒng kÃ½ há»“ sÆ¡ khuÃ´n máº·t cho ${memberName(userId) ?? `user #${userId}`}`);
              loadProfiles();
            } catch (err) { fail(err, 'KhÃ´ng lÆ°u Ä‘Æ°á»£c há»“ sÆ¡'); }
          });
          return;
        }
        case 'reload-profiles':
          profiles = null; paintProfiles(); loadProfiles();
          return;
        default:
      }
    });
    root.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
      if (e.target.id !== 'voice-form') return;
      e.preventDefault();
      const input = $('#voice-text');
      runVoice(input.value);
      input.value = '';
    });

    paintVector();
    paintDoors();
    paintUserField();
    paintProfiles();
    loadMembers();
    loadProfiles();
    const off = onChange('doors', () => state.doors.map((d) => d.name).join('|'), paintDoors);
    return () => {
      alive = false;
      off();
      recog?.abort?.();
      stopCamera(stream, $('#cam'));
    };
  },
};

// Äiá»u khiá»ƒn thiáº¿t bá»‹ (UC 3.1): POST /devices/:deviceId/commands (TURN_ON / TURN_OFF / SET_SPEED)

const KIND_LABEL = { light: 'ÄÃ¨n', fan: 'Quáº¡t', other: 'Thiáº¿t bá»‹', sensor: 'Cáº£m biáº¿n', door: 'Cá»­a' };
const connBadge = (d) => (d.online === true ? badge(d.connection || 'Online', 'emerald')
  : d.online === false ? badge(d.connection || 'Offline', 'rose') : badge(d.connection || 'KhÃ´ng rÃµ', 'slate'));

      </div>`;

           <b class="w-11 text-right font-mono text-sm text-slate-700" data-fan-val="${esc(d.id)}">${d.speed}%</b></div>`
      : toggle(d.on, 'toggle', `data-id="${esc(d.id)}"`));

      const list = state.devices.filter((d) => ['light', 'fan', 'other'].includes(d.kind));
      let body;
      if (state.errors.devices && !list.length) body = errorBlock(`KhÃ´ng táº£i Ä‘Æ°á»£c thiáº¿t bá»‹: ${state.errors.devices}`);
      else if (state.loading && !list.length) body = loadingBlock();
      else if (!list.length) body = emptyState('devices_other', 'ChÆ°a cÃ³ thiáº¿t bá»‹ Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn', 'ThÃªm Ä‘Ã¨n (LIGHT) hoáº·c quáº¡t (FAN) á»Ÿ khung "ThÃªm thiáº¿t bá»‹".');
      else body = list.map((d) => row(ICON[d.kind], esc(d.name), `<span>${KIND_LABEL[d.kind]} Â· ${esc(d.typeCode || 'â€”')} Â· ID ${esc(d.id)}</span>${connBadge(d)}`, control(d))).join('');

        body: `
          ${state.mode === 'auto' ? '<p class="text-xs text-sky-700 bg-sky-50 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 mb-2">Cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ tá»± Ä‘á»™ng: Ä‘Ã¨n theo PIR/Ã¡nh sÃ¡ng, quáº¡t theo nhiá»‡t Ä‘á»™ (lá»‡nh gá»­i tháº­t xuá»‘ng Backend). Thao tÃ¡c tay bÃªn dÆ°á»›i sáº½ chuyá»ƒn sang thá»§ cÃ´ng.</p>' : ''}
          ${body}
          ${row('notifications_active', 'CÃ²i cáº£nh bÃ¡o', '<span>Máº¡ch tá»± kÃªu khi nhiá»‡t Ä‘á»™ vÆ°á»£t ngÆ°á»¡ng â€” hiá»ƒn thá»‹ theo cáº£nh bÃ¡o</span>', badge(state.buzzer ? 'ÄANG KÃŠU' : 'Táº¯t', state.buzzer ? 'rose' : 'slate'))}
          ${row('door_front', 'Cá»­a', `<span>${state.doors.length} cá»­a Â· má»Ÿ báº±ng mÃ£ PIN, FaceAI hoáº·c tá»« xa</span>`,
            `<div class="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">${badge(state.doorOpen ? 'ÄANG Má»ž' : 'ÄÃ³ng', state.doorOpen ? 'emerald' : 'slate')}<a href="#/door" class="text-xs font-semibold text-sky-600 hover:text-sky-700">Má»Ÿ trang cá»­a â†’</a></div>`)}`,
      });
    };

    $('#add-panel').innerHTML = panel({ title: 'ThÃªm thiáº¿t bá»‹', icon: 'add_circle', body: `
      <form id="add-device" class="space-y-3">
        ${field('TÃªn thiáº¿t bá»‹ *', `<input name="deviceName" required class="${inputCls}" placeholder="VD: ÄÃ¨n phÃ²ng khÃ¡ch">`)}
        ${field('MÃ£ loáº¡i (typeCode) *', `<input name="typeCode" required list="type-codes" class="${inputCls} uppercase" placeholder="LIGHT">`, 'Gá»£i Ã½: LIGHT (Ä‘Ã¨n), FAN (quáº¡t), SENSOR (cá»¥m cáº£m biáº¿n)â€¦')}

        ${button('ÄÄƒng kÃ½ thiáº¿t bá»‹', { type: 'submit', icon: 'add' })}
      </form>` });

    const paintManage = () => {

      const rows = list.map((d) => (String(editing) === String(d.id)

            </td>
          </tr>`)).join('');
      $('#manage-panel').innerHTML = panel({

        body: list.length ? `

    root.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
      const b = e.target.closest('[data-act]');
      if (!b) return;
      const { act, val } = b.dataset;
      const d = find(b.dataset.id ?? val);
      if (act === 'reload') return busy(b, () => refreshHomeData());
      if (act === 'mode') return setMode(val);
      if (act === 'toggle' && d) return controlDevice(d, { on: !d.on }).catch((err) => fail(err, `KhÃ´ng gá»­i Ä‘Æ°á»£c lá»‡nh tá»›i ${d.name}`));
      if (act === 'edit') { editing = val; return paintManage(); }
      if (act === 'cancel') { editing = null; return paintManage(); }
      if (act === 'delete' && d) {
        if (!confirm(`XoÃ¡ thiáº¿t bá»‹ "${d.name}" khá»i nhÃ ?`)) return;
        await busy(b, async () => {
          try { await api.devices.remove(state.homeId, d.id); toast('ÄÃ£ xoÃ¡ thiáº¿t bá»‹', 'emerald'); await refreshHomeData({ silent: true }); }
          catch (err) { fail(err, 'KhÃ´ng xoÃ¡ Ä‘Æ°á»£c thiáº¿t bá»‹'); }
        });
      }
    });
    root.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
      if (e.target.dataset.act !== 'fan') return;
      const lab = root.querySelector(`[data-fan-val="${CSS.escape(e.target.dataset.id)}"]`);
      if (lab) lab.textContent = `${e.target.value}%`;
    });
    root.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      if (e.target.dataset.act !== 'fan') return;
      const d = find(e.target.dataset.id);
      if (d) controlDevice(d, { speed: +e.target.value }).catch((err) => fail(err, `KhÃ´ng gá»­i Ä‘Æ°á»£c lá»‡nh tá»›i ${d.name}`));
    });
    root.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const f = e.target;
      const v = formValues(f);
      if (f.id === 'add-device') {
        if (!v.deviceName || !v.typeCode) return toast('Nháº­p Ä‘á»§ tÃªn vÃ  mÃ£ loáº¡i thiáº¿t bá»‹', 'rose');
        await busy(f.querySelector('[type=submit]'), async () => {
          try {
            await api.devices.create(state.homeId, { deviceName: v.deviceName, typeCode: v.typeCode.toUpperCase() });
            f.reset();
            toast('ÄÃ£ Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ thiáº¿t bá»‹', 'emerald');
            await refreshHomeData({ silent: true });
          } catch (err) { fail(err, 'KhÃ´ng Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ Ä‘Æ°á»£c thiáº¿t bá»‹'); }
        });
      } else if (f.id === 'edit-device') {
        const body = {};
        for (const k of ['deviceName', 'connectionState', 'availableWorkingState', 'controllerId']) if (v[k]) body[k] = v[k];
        await busy(root.querySelector('[form="edit-device"][type=submit]'), async () => {
          try {
            await api.devices.update(state.homeId, f.dataset.id, body);
            toast('ÄÃ£ cáº­p nháº­t thiáº¿t bá»‹', 'emerald');
            editing = null;
            await refreshHomeData({ silent: true });
          } catch (err) { fail(err, 'KhÃ´ng cáº­p nháº­t Ä‘Æ°á»£c'); }
        });
      }
    });

    paintDev();
    paintManage();
    const offs = [
      ...['devices', 'loading', 'alerts', 'door', 'doors'].map((e) => on(e, paintDev)),
      on('devices', () => { if (editing == null) paintManage(); }),
    ];
    return () => offs.forEach((off) => off());
  },
};

          ${creds.map((c) => `<div class="flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 text-sm">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px] text-slate-400">password</span>
              <span class="font-mono text-slate-700">#${esc(c.id)}</span>${badge(c.type, 'sky')}${c.status ? badge(c.status, 'slate') : ''}
              <span class="text-xs text-slate-400">${c.ts ? fullTime(c.ts) : ''}</span></div>
            ${button('Thu há»“i', { act: 'revoke', val: c.id, tone: 'danger', size: 'sm' })}
          </div>`).join('')}
        </div>` : '<p class="text-xs text-slate-400">Backend khÃ´ng tráº£ danh sÃ¡ch credential trong thÃ´ng tin cá»­a â€” cÃ³ thá»ƒ thu há»“i theo ID bÃªn dÆ°á»›i.</p>';
    };

    const paintAttempts = () => {
      const d = door();
      if (!d) return;
      let body;
      if (attempts === null) body = loadingBlock();
      else if (attemptsErr) body = errorBlock(`KhÃ´ng táº£i Ä‘Æ°á»£c lá»‹ch sá»­ truy cáº­p: ${attemptsErr}`);
      else if (!attempts.length) body = emptyState('history', 'ChÆ°a cÃ³ lÆ°á»£t truy cáº­p nÃ o');
      else {
        body = `<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-sm">
          <thead><tr class="text-left text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 border-b border-slate-100">
            <th class="py-2 pr-4 font-semibold">Thá»i gian</th><th class="py-2 pr-4 font-semibold">Káº¿t quáº£</th><th class="py-2 pr-4 font-semibold">PhÆ°Æ¡ng thá»©c</th>
            <th class="py-2 pr-4 font-semibold">NgÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng</th><th class="py-2 font-semibold">Chi tiáº¿t</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>${attempts.slice(0, 100).map((a) => `<tr class="border-b border-slate-50">
            <td class="py-2 pr-4 font-mono text-xs text-slate-500 whitespace-nowrap">${fullTime(a.ts)}</td>
            <td class="py-2 pr-4">${badge(a.result || (a.ok ? 'ThÃ nh cÃ´ng' : a.ok === false ? 'Tháº¥t báº¡i' : 'â€”'), a.ok ? 'emerald' : a.ok === false ? 'rose' : 'slate')}</td>
            <td class="py-2 pr-4 text-slate-600">${esc(a.method || 'â€”')}</td>
            <td class="py-2 pr-4 text-slate-600">${esc(a.actor || 'â€”')}</td>
            <td class="py-2 text-slate-500 text-xs">${esc(a.detail || '')}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`;
      }
      $('#door-attempts').innerHTML = panel({
        title: `Lá»‹ch sá»­ truy cáº­p Â· ${esc(d.name)}${attempts?.length ? ` (${attempts.length})` : ''}`, icon: 'manage_history',
        right: button('Táº£i láº¡i', { act: 'reload-attempts', tone: 'secondary', size: 'sm', icon: 'refresh' }), body,
      });
    };

    const loadAttempts = async () => {
      const d = door();
      if (!d) return;
      try {
        const res = await api.doors.attempts(state.homeId, d.name);
        if (!alive || d.name !== selected) return;
        attempts = asList(res, 'attempts').map(normAttempt).sort((a, b) => (b.ts ?? 0) - (a.ts ?? 0));
        attemptsErr = null;
      } catch (e) {
        if (!alive || d.name !== selected) return;
        attempts = [];
        attemptsErr = e.message;
      }
      paintAttempts();
    };

    const paintAll = () => {
      ensureSelected();
      paintPicker(); paintEmpty(); paintKeypad(); paintCtrl(); paintCreds(); paintAttempts();
    };

    const resetKeypad = () => { buf = ''; step = 0; oldPin = ''; newPin = ''; msg = null; };

    // PhÃ­m F
    const confirmKey = async () => {
      const d = door();
      if (!buf || !d) return;
      const pin = buf;
      buf = '';
      if (!PIN_PATTERN.test(pin)) { msg = { ok: false, text: 'MÃ£ PIN gá»“m 4â€“8 chá»¯ sá»‘' }; return paintKeypad(); }

      if (mode === 'open') {
        msg = { ok: null, text: 'Äang xÃ¡c thá»±câ€¦' };
        paintKeypad();
        try {
          await api.doors.verifyPin(state.homeId, d.name, pin);
          msg = { ok: true, text: 'ÄÃšNG MÃƒ PIN â€” cá»­a Ä‘ang má»Ÿ' };
          markDoorOpened('mÃ£ PIN', d.name);
          setTimeout(loadAttempts, 500);
        } catch (e) {
          if (e.status === 429) msg = { ok: false, text: 'Táº M KHOÃ: nháº­p sai quÃ¡ 5 láº§n trong 10 phÃºt, vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i sau' };
          else if ([400, 401, 403].includes(e.status)) msg = { ok: false, text: `SAI MÃƒ PIN â€” ${e.message}` };
          else msg = { ok: false, text: e.message };
          addLog('door', `Nháº­p mÃ£ PIN tháº¥t báº¡i táº¡i "${d.name}": ${msg.text}`);
          setTimeout(loadAttempts, 500);
        }
        if (alive) paintKeypad();
        return;
      }

      if (step === 0) { oldPin = pin; step = 1; msg = null; }
      else if (step === 1) { newPin = pin; step = 2; msg = null; }
      else if (pin !== newPin) { resetKeypad(); msg = { ok: false, text: 'MÃ£ PIN nháº­p láº¡i khÃ´ng khá»›p â€” lÃ m láº¡i tá»« Ä‘áº§u' }; }
      else {
        msg = { ok: null, text: 'Äang Ä‘á»•i mÃ£ PINâ€¦' };
        paintKeypad();
        try {
          await api.doors.changePin(state.homeId, d.name, oldPin, newPin);
          resetKeypad();
          mode = 'open';
          msg = { ok: true, text: 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘á»•i mÃ£ PIN thÃ nh cÃ´ng' };
          toast('ÄÃ£ Ä‘á»•i mÃ£ PIN', 'emerald');
          addLog('door', `Äá»•i mÃ£ PIN cá»­a "${d.name}"`);
        } catch (e) {
          resetKeypad();
          msg = { ok: false, text: `KhÃ´ng Ä‘á»•i Ä‘Æ°á»£c mÃ£ PIN â€” ${e.message}` };
        }
      }
      if (alive) paintKeypad();
    };

    root.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
      const b = e.target.closest('[data-act]');
      if (!b) return;
      const { act, val } = b.dataset;
      const d = door();
      if (act === 'refresh') return busy(b, () => refreshHomeData());
      if (act === 'kptab') { mode = val; resetKeypad(); return paintKeypad(); }
      if (act === 'key') {
        if (val === 'C') { buf = ''; msg = null; }
        else if (val === 'F') return confirmKey();
        else if (buf.length < 8) { buf += val; msg = null; }
        return paintKeypad();
      }
      if (act === 'reload-attempts') { attempts = null; paintAttempts(); return loadAttempts(); }
      if (act === 'unlock' && d) {
        await busy(b, async () => {
          try {
            await api.doors.unlock(state.homeId, d.name, val);
            if (val === 'unlocked') { markDoorOpened('má»Ÿ tá»« xa', d.name); toast(`ÄÃ£ gá»­i lá»‡nh má»Ÿ khoÃ¡ "${d.name}"`, 'emerald'); }
            else { addLog('door', `KhoÃ¡ cá»­a "${d.name}" tá»« xa`); toast(`ÄÃ£ gá»­i lá»‡nh khoÃ¡ "${d.name}"`, 'sky'); }
            setTimeout(loadAttempts, 500);
          } catch (err) { fail(err, 'KhÃ´ng gá»­i Ä‘Æ°á»£c lá»‡nh'); }
        });
      }
      if (act === 'revoke' && d) {
        if (!confirm(`Thu há»“i credential #${val} cá»§a "${d.name}"?`)) return;
        await busy(b, () => revoke(d, val));
      }
    });

    const revoke = async (d, credentialId) => {
      try {
        await api.doors.revoke(state.homeId, d.name, credentialId);
        issued[d.name] = (issued[d.name] ?? []).filter((c) => String(c.id) !== String(credentialId));
        toast(`ÄÃ£ thu há»“i credential #${credentialId}`, 'emerald');
        addLog('door', `Thu há»“i credential #${credentialId} cá»§a cá»­a "${d.name}"`);
        await refreshHomeData({ silent: true });
        paintCreds();
      } catch (err) { fail(err, 'KhÃ´ng thu há»“i Ä‘Æ°á»£c'); }
    };

    root.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      if (e.target.id !== 'door-select') return;
      selected = e.target.value;
      resetKeypad(); mode = 'open'; attempts = null;
      paintAll();
      loadAttempts();
    });

    root.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const f = e.target;
      const v = formValues(f);
      const btn = f.querySelector('[type=submit]');
      const d = door();
      if (f.id === 'issue-pin' && d) {
        if (!PIN_PATTERN.test(v.pin)) return toast('MÃ£ PIN pháº£i gá»“m 4â€“8 chá»¯ sá»‘', 'rose');
        await busy(btn, async () => {
          try {
            const res = await api.doors.issuePin(state.homeId, d.name, v.pin);
            const id = deepFind(res, ['credentialId', 'id']);
            if (id != null) (issued[d.name] ??= []).push({ id, ts: Date.now() });
            f.reset();
            toast(`ÄÃ£ cáº¥p mÃ£ PIN má»›i${id != null ? ` (credential #${id})` : ''}`, 'emerald');
            addLog('door', `Cáº¥p mÃ£ PIN má»›i cho cá»­a "${d.name}"`);
            await refreshHomeData({ silent: true });
            paintCreds();
          } catch (err) { fail(err, 'KhÃ´ng cáº¥p Ä‘Æ°á»£c mÃ£ PIN'); }
        });
      } else if (f.id === 'revoke-cred' && d) {
        if (!v.credentialId) return toast('Nháº­p Credential ID cáº§n thu há»“i', 'rose');
        await busy(btn, async () => { await revoke(d, v.credentialId); f.reset(); });
      } else if (f.dataset.form === 'create-door') {
        if (!v.doorName) return toast('Nháº­p tÃªn cá»­a', 'rose');
        await busy(btn, async () => {
          try {
            const res = await api.doors.create(state.homeId, v.doorName);
            f.reset();
            toast(`ÄÃ£ táº¡o cá»­a "${v.doorName}"`, 'emerald');
            selected = pick(asObject(res, 'door'), 'doorName', 'name') ?? v.doorName;
            await refreshHomeData({ silent: true });
            attempts = null;
            paintAll();
            loadAttempts();
          } catch (err) { fail(err, 'KhÃ´ng táº¡o Ä‘Æ°á»£c cá»­a'); }
        });
      }
    });

    paintAll();
    loadAttempts();
    const offs = [
      onChange(['doors', 'loading'], () => JSON.stringify([state.loading, state.errors.doors, state.doors.map((d) => [d.name, d.state, d.credentials.map((c) => c.id)])]), () => {
        const prev = selected;
        ensureSelected();
        paintPicker(); paintEmpty(); paintCtrl(); paintCreds();
        if (selected !== prev) { resetKeypad(); attempts = null; paintKeypad(); paintAttempts(); loadAttempts(); }
      }),
      on('door', paintCtrl),
    ];
    return () => { alive = false; offs.forEach((off) => off()); };
  },
};

import { state, on, clearLogs } from '../store.js';
import { api } from '../api.js';
import { asList, esc, fullTime, normText } from '../utils/fields.js';
import { normLog } from '../models.js';
import { panel, pageTitle, badge, segmented, button, inputCls, emptyState, loadingBlock, errorBlock, busy } from '../ui.js';

const TYPES = [['all', 'Táº¥t cáº£'], ['sensor', 'Cáº£m biáº¿n'], ['device', 'Thiáº¿t bá»‹'], ['door', 'Cá»­a'], ['alert', 'Cáº£nh bÃ¡o']];
const TYPE_META = { sensor: ['Cáº£m biáº¿n', 'sky'], device: ['Thiáº¿t bá»‹', 'slate'], door: ['Cá»­a', 'emerald'], alert: ['Cáº£nh bÃ¡o', 'rose'] };
const PERIODS = { day: ['24 giá»', 864e5], week: ['7 ngÃ y', 6048e5], month: ['30 ngÃ y', 2592e6], all: ['Táº¥t cáº£', Infinity] };
const LIMIT = 200;
let tab = 'system', period = 'week', localType = 'all', query = '';   // giá»¯ lá»±a chá»n khi chuyá»ƒn trang

const actionTone = (a) => {
  const s = normText(a);
  if (/delete|remove|revoke|fail|deny|lock|xoa|thu hoi/.test(s)) return 'rose';
  if (/unlock|open|login|create|add|mo|tao|them/.test(s)) return 'emerald';
  if (/update|change|edit|patch|doi|sua/.test(s)) return 'amber';
  return 'slate';
};

export const historyPage = {
  mount(root) {
    let logs = null, logsErr = null, alive = true;
    const homeId = state.homeId;
    root.innerHTML = pageTitle('Cáº£nh bÃ¡o & Lá»‹ch sá»­', 'Cáº£nh bÃ¡o ngÆ°á»¡ng Ä‘ang hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng, nháº­t kÃ½ hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng cá»§a há»‡ thá»‘ng vÃ  sá»± kiá»‡n trÃªn trÃ¬nh duyá»‡t') + `
      <div id="alert-box"></div>
      <div id="tab-box"></div>
      <div id="log-box"></div>`;
    const $ = (s) => root.querySelector(s);

    const paintAlerts = () => {
      $('#alert-box').innerHTML = state.alerts.length
        ? `<div class="grid gap-3">${state.alerts.map((a) => `
            <div class="p-4 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 border border-rose-100 flex items-start gap-3">
              <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-rose-500">warning</span>
              <div><div class="text-sm font-semibold text-rose-800">Äang cáº£nh bÃ¡o</div><div class="text-sm text-rose-700">${esc(a.message)}</div></div>
            </div>`).join('')}</div>`
        : `<div class="p-4 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-100 flex items-center gap-3 text-sm text-emerald-800 font-semibold">
             <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-emerald-500">check_circle</span>Táº¥t cáº£ thÃ´ng sá»‘ an toÃ n</div>`;
    };

    const paintTabs = () => {
      $('#tab-box').innerHTML = segmented([['system', 'Nháº­t kÃ½ há»‡ thá»‘ng (Backend)'], ['local', 'Sá»± kiá»‡n trÃªn trÃ¬nh duyá»‡t']], tab, 'tab');
    };

    const systemRows = () => {
      const since = Date.now() - PERIODS[period][1];
      const q = normText(query);
      return logs.filter((l) => (l.ts == null || l.ts >= since)
        && (!q || normText(`${l.actor} ${l.action} ${l.target} ${l.detail}`).includes(q)));
    };

    const paintSystemTable = () => {
      const el = $('#log-table');
      if (!el) return;
      if (logs === null) { el.innerHTML = loadingBlock(); return; }
      if (logsErr) { el.innerHTML = errorBlock(logsErr); return; }
      const rows = systemRows();
      $('#log-count').textContent = `(${rows.length})`;
      el.innerHTML = rows.length ? `
        <div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-sm">
          <thead><tr class="text-left text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 border-b border-slate-100">
            <th class="py-2 pr-4 font-semibold">Thá»i gian</th><th class="py-2 pr-4 font-semibold">NgÆ°á»i thá»±c hiá»‡n</th>
            <th class="py-2 pr-4 font-semibold">HÃ nh Ä‘á»™ng</th><th class="py-2 font-semibold">Chi tiáº¿t</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>${rows.slice(0, LIMIT).map((l) => `<tr class="border-b border-slate-50 align-top">
            <td class="py-2 pr-4 font-mono text-xs text-slate-500 whitespace-nowrap">${fullTime(l.ts)}</td>
            <td class="py-2 pr-4 text-slate-700 whitespace-nowrap">${esc(l.actor)}</td>
            <td class="py-2 pr-4">${l.action ? badge(l.action, actionTone(l.action)) : 'â€”'}${l.target ? `<div class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">${esc(l.target)}</div>` : ''}</td>
            <td class="py-2 text-slate-600 text-xs break-all">${esc(l.detail)}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>
        ${rows.length > LIMIT ? `<p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-3">Hiá»ƒn thá»‹ ${LIMIT} sá»± kiá»‡n má»›i nháº¥t.</p>` : ''}`
        : emptyState('history', 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ sá»± kiá»‡n trong khoáº£ng nÃ y');
    };

    const paintLocal = () => {
      const since = Date.now() - PERIODS[period][1];
      const rows = state.logs.filter((l) => l.ts >= since && (localType === 'all' || l.type === localType));
      $('#log-box').innerHTML = panel({
        title: `Sá»± kiá»‡n trÃªn trÃ¬nh duyá»‡t (${rows.length})`, icon: 'devices',
        right: '<button type="button" data-act="clear" class="text-xs font-semibold text-rose-600 hover:text-rose-700">XoÃ¡ lá»‹ch sá»­ cá»¥c bá»™</button>',
        body: `
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mb-3">Cáº£nh bÃ¡o ngÆ°á»¡ng, lá»‡nh tá»± Ä‘á»™ng/thá»§ cÃ´ng vÃ  thao tÃ¡c cá»­a ghi nháº­n trÃªn trÃ¬nh duyá»‡t nÃ y (lÆ°u trong localStorage theo tá»«ng nhÃ ).</p>
          <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 mb-4">
            ${segmented(Object.entries(PERIODS).map(([k, v]) => [k, v[0]]), period, 'period')}
            ${segmented(TYPES, localType, 'type')}
          </div>
          <div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-sm">
            <thead><tr class="text-left text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 border-b border-slate-100"><th class="py-2 pr-4 font-semibold">Thá»i gian</th><th class="py-2 pr-4 font-semibold">Loáº¡i</th><th class="py-2 font-semibold">Ná»™i dung</th></tr></thead>

              <td class="py-2 pr-4">${badge(...(TYPE_META[l.type] ?? ['KhÃ¡c', 'slate']))}</td><td class="py-2 text-slate-700">${esc(l.message)}</td></tr>`).join('')
              || '<tr><td colspan="3" class="py-8 text-center text-slate-400">KhÃ´ng cÃ³ sá»± kiá»‡n trong khoáº£ng nÃ y</td></tr>'}</tbody>
          </table></div>`,
      });
    };

    const paintSystem = () => {
      $('#log-box').innerHTML = panel({
        title: 'Nháº­t kÃ½ hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng <span id="log-count" class="text-slate-400 font-medium"></span>', icon: 'history',
        right: button('Táº£i láº¡i', { act: 'reload', tone: 'secondary', size: 'sm', icon: 'refresh' }),
        body: `
          <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 mb-4">
            ${segmented(Object.entries(PERIODS).map(([k, v]) => [k, v[0]]), period, 'period')}
            <input id="log-q" type="search" value="${esc(query)}" placeholder="TÃ¬m theo ngÆ°á»i, hÃ nh Ä‘á»™ng, chi tiáº¿tâ€¦" class="${inputCls} max-w-xs">
          </div>
          <div id="log-table"></div>`,
      });
      paintSystemTable();
    };

    const paintLogs = () => (tab === 'system' ? paintSystem() : paintLocal());

    const load = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.logs.activity(homeId);
        if (!alive) return;
        logs = asList(res, 'logs', 'activityLogs').map(normLog).sort((a, b) => (b.ts ?? 0) - (a.ts ?? 0));
        logsErr = null;
      } catch (e) {
        if (!alive) return;
        logs = [];
        logsErr = e.status === 403 ? 'Báº¡n cáº§n quyá»n LOG_VIEW_ALL hoáº·c LOG_VIEW_OWN Ä‘á»ƒ xem nháº­t kÃ½' : `KhÃ´ng táº£i Ä‘Æ°á»£c nháº­t kÃ½: ${e.message}`;

      if (e.target.id !== 'log-q') return;
      query = e.target.value;
      paintSystemTable();
    });

    paintAlerts(); paintTabs(); paintLogs();
    load();
    const offs = [on('alerts', paintAlerts), on('logs', () => { if (tab === 'local') paintLocal(); })];
    return () => { alive = false; offs.forEach((off) => off()); };
  },
};

import { state, on, loadHomes, selectHome } from '../store.js';

import { asObject, esc } from '../utils/fields.js';
import { normHome } from '../models.js';
import { panel, pageTitle, badge, button, field, inputCls, emptyState, errorBlock, toast, busy, formValues, fail } from '../ui.js';

export const homesPage = {
  mount(root) {
    let editing = null;
    root.innerHTML = pageTitle('NhÃ  cá»§a tÃ´i', 'CÃ¡c ngÃ´i nhÃ  báº¡n lÃ  thÃ nh viÃªn (Ä‘ang hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng, chÆ°a háº¿t háº¡n)',

              </div>
              <div class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">${esc(h.address || 'ChÆ°a cÃ³ Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰')} Â· ID ${esc(h.id)}</div>

          </div>
          <div class="flex gap-2 shrink-0">
            ${current ? '' : button('Chá»n', { act: 'select', val: h.id, size: 'sm' })}
            ${button('', { act: 'edit', val: h.id, tone: 'ghost', icon: 'edit', size: 'sm', attrs: 'title="Sá»­a (cáº§n quyá»n HOME_UPDATE)"' })}

          </div>

      </div>`;

    const paint = () => {
      if (state.errors.homes && !state.homes.length) { listEl.innerHTML = errorBlock(`KhÃ´ng táº£i Ä‘Æ°á»£c danh sÃ¡ch nhÃ : ${state.errors.homes}`); return; }

        listEl.innerHTML = `<div class="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200/80 shadow-sm">${emptyState('home_work', 'Báº¡n chÆ°a thuá»™c ngÃ´i nhÃ  nÃ o', 'Táº¡o nhÃ  má»›i á»Ÿ khung bÃªn pháº£i hoáº·c nhá» chá»§ nhÃ  má»i báº¡n qua email.')}</div>`;
        return;

      listEl.innerHTML = state.homes.map(card).join('');

      const { act, val } = b.dataset;
      const home = find(val);
      if (act === 'reload') return busy(b, loadHomes);
      if (act === 'select' && home) return selectHome(home.id).then(() => toast(`ÄÃ£ chuyá»ƒn sang "${home.name}"`, 'sky'));
      if (act === 'edit') { editing = val; return paint(); }
      if (act === 'cancel') { editing = null; return paint(); }
      if (act === 'delete' && home) {
        if (!confirm(`XoÃ¡ nhÃ  "${home.name}"? ToÃ n bá»™ dá»¯ liá»‡u cá»§a nhÃ  sáº½ bá»‹ xoÃ¡ vÃ  khÃ´ng thá»ƒ hoÃ n tÃ¡c.`)) return;

        });
      }
    });

    root.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const f = e.target;
      const v = formValues(f);
      const btn = f.querySelector('[type=submit]');
      if (f.id === 'create-home') {
        if (!v.homeName) return toast('Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p tÃªn nhÃ ', 'rose');
        await busy(btn, async () => {
          try {
            const res = await api.homes.create({ homeName: v.homeName, address: v.address || undefined });
            f.reset();

            const created = normHome(asObject(res, 'home') ?? {});

              const target = state.homes.find((h) => created.id != null && String(h.id) === String(created.id)) ?? state.homes.at(-1);
              if (target) await selectHome(target.id);
            }
          } catch (err) { fail(err, 'KhÃ´ng táº¡o Ä‘Æ°á»£c nhÃ '); }
        });
      } else if (f.dataset.form === 'edit-home') {
        if (!v.homeName) return toast('TÃªn nhÃ  khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘á»ƒ trá»‘ng', 'rose');

          try {
            const body = { homeName: v.homeName, address: v.address };
            if (v.state) body.state = v.state;
            await api.homes.update(f.dataset.id, body);
            toast('ÄÃ£ cáº­p nháº­t thÃ´ng tin nhÃ ', 'emerald');
            editing = null;
            await loadHomes();
          } catch (err) { fail(err, 'KhÃ´ng cáº­p nháº­t Ä‘Æ°á»£c'); }
        });
      }
    });

    paint();
    const off = on('homes', () => { if (editing == null) paint(); });
    return () => off();
  },
};

// ÄÄƒng nháº­p / Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½: POST /auth/login, POST /auth/register (public).
import { login, register } from '../store.js';
import { DEVICE } from '../config.js';
import { esc } from '../utils/fields.js';
import { inputCls, field, button, segmented, errorBlock, formValues, busy, toast } from '../ui.js';

let lastEmail = '';

export const loginPage = {
  mount(root) {
    let tab = 'login';

    const paint = () => {
      const reg = tab === 'register';
      root.innerHTML = `
        <div class="min-h-[calc(100vh-4rem)] flex items-center justify-center">
          <div class="w-full max-w-md">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-3 justify-center mb-6">
              <img src="${DEVICE.logo}" alt="" class="h-11 w-11 rounded-xl ring-1 ring-slate-200 object-cover">
              <div>
                <div class="font-bold text-slate-900 text-xl font-['Space_Grotesk'] leading-none">${DEVICE.brand}</div>
                <div class="text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-wider text-sky-600 uppercase mt-1">${DEVICE.platform} Â· YoloHome</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <section class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-7 border border-slate-200/80 shadow-sm">
              <div class="flex justify-center">${segmented([['login', 'ÄÄƒng nháº­p'], ['register', 'ÄÄƒng kÃ½']], tab, 'tab')}</div>
              <form id="auth-form" class="space-y-4 mt-6" novalidate>
                ${reg ? field('Há» vÃ  tÃªn *', `<input name="name" required autocomplete="name" class="${inputCls}">`) : ''}
                ${reg ? field('TÃªn hiá»ƒn thá»‹', `<input name="displayName" class="${inputCls}" placeholder="Máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh dÃ¹ng há» tÃªn">`) : ''}
                ${field('Email *', `<input name="email" type="email" required autocomplete="email" value="${esc(lastEmail)}" class="${inputCls}">`)}
                ${field('Máº­t kháº©u *', `<input name="password" type="password" required autocomplete="${reg ? 'new-password' : 'current-password'}" class="${inputCls}">`, reg ? 'Tá»‘i thiá»ƒu 6 kÃ½ tá»±' : '')}
                ${reg ? field('Nháº­p láº¡i máº­t kháº©u *', `<input name="confirm" type="password" autocomplete="new-password" class="${inputCls}">`) : ''}
                <div id="auth-error"></div>
                ${button(reg ? 'Táº¡o tÃ i khoáº£n' : 'ÄÄƒng nháº­p', { type: 'submit', icon: reg ? 'person_add' : 'login', cls: 'w-full', attrs: 'id="auth-submit"' })}
              </form>
            </section>
            <p class="text-center text-xs text-slate-400 mt-4">PhiÃªn Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p (JWT) cÃ³ hiá»‡u lá»±c 7 ngÃ y.</p>
          </div>
        </div>`;
      root.querySelector(reg ? '[name=name]' : (lastEmail ? '[name=password]' : '[name=email]'))?.focus();
    };

    const showError = (msg) => { root.querySelector('#auth-error').innerHTML = msg ? errorBlock(msg) : ''; };

    const validate = (v) => {
      if (tab === 'register' && !v.name) return 'Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p há» tÃªn';
      if (!/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(v.email)) return 'Email khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡';
      if (!v.password) return 'Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p máº­t kháº©u';
      if (tab === 'register' && v.password.length < 6) return 'Máº­t kháº©u tá»‘i thiá»ƒu 6 kÃ½ tá»±';
      if (tab === 'register' && v.password !== v.confirm) return 'Máº­t kháº©u nháº­p láº¡i khÃ´ng khá»›p';
      return '';
    };

    root.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      const b = e.target.closest('[data-act="tab"]');
      if (b) { tab = b.dataset.val; paint(); }
    });

    root.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const v = formValues(e.target);
      const err = validate(v);
      showError(err);
      if (err) return;
      lastEmail = v.email;
      await busy(root.querySelector('#auth-submit'), async () => {
        try {
          if (tab === 'register') {
            await register({ name: v.name, email: v.email, password: v.password, displayName: v.displayName || v.name });
            toast('ÄÄƒng kÃ½ thÃ nh cÃ´ng', 'emerald');
          }
          await login(v.email, v.password);
        } catch (ex) {
          if (root.isConnected) showError(ex.status === 401 ? 'Email hoáº·c máº­t kháº©u khÃ´ng Ä‘Ãºng' : ex.message);
        }
      });
    });

    paint();
  },
};
