// ThÃ nh viÃªn: GET/POST /homes/:homeId/members, PUT/DELETE /homes/:homeId/members/:userId
import { state } from '../store.js';
import { api } from '../api.js';
import { MEMBER_ROLES } from '../config.js';
import { asList, esc, fmtDate, toDateInput, toTs } from '../utils/fields.js';
import { normMember } from '../models.js';
import { panel, pageTitle, badge, button, field, inputCls, emptyState, loadingBlock, errorBlock, toast, busy, formValues, fail } from '../ui.js';

const isOwner = (role) => /owner|chu nha|chá»§ nhÃ /i.test(role);
const roleTone = (role) => (isOwner(role) ? 'amber' : /guest|khach/i.test(role) ? 'slate' : 'sky');
const initials = (s = '') => s.trim().split(/\s+/).slice(-2).map((w) => w[0]).join('').toUpperCase() || '?';

export const membersPage = {
  mount(root) {
    let members = null, error = null, editing = null, alive = true;
    const homeId = state.homeId;
    root.innerHTML = pageTitle('ThÃ nh viÃªn', `ThÃ nh viÃªn cá»§a nhÃ  <b>${esc(state.home?.name ?? '')}</b> â€” thÃªm, Ä‘á»•i vai trÃ², gia háº¡n hoáº·c xoÃ¡`,
      button('Táº£i láº¡i', { act: 'reload', tone: 'secondary', icon: 'refresh' })) + `
      <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6 items-start">
        <div id="member-list" class="lg:col-span-8"></div>
        <div class="lg:col-span-4">${panel({ title: 'Má»i thÃ nh viÃªn', icon: 'person_add', body: `
          <form id="add-member" class="space-y-3">
            ${field('Email *', `<input name="email" type="email" required class="${inputCls}" placeholder="nguoi.dung@email.com">`)}
            ${field('Vai trÃ²', `<select name="roleName" class="${inputCls}">${MEMBER_ROLES.map((r) => `<option>${r}</option>`).join('')}</select>`)}
            ${field('Háº¿t háº¡n', `<input name="expired" type="date" class="${inputCls}">`, 'Äá»ƒ trá»‘ng náº¿u khÃ´ng giá»›i háº¡n thá»i gian')}
            ${button('ThÃªm thÃ nh viÃªn', { type: 'submit', icon: 'send' })}
            <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Cáº§n quyá»n MEMBER_MANAGE. NgÆ°á»i Ä‘Æ°á»£c má»i pháº£i cÃ³ tÃ i khoáº£n vá»›i email nÃ y.</p>
          </form>` })}</div>
      </div>`;
    const listEl = root.querySelector('#member-list');

    const row = (m) => {
      const me = state.user && String(state.user.id) === String(m.userId);
      const expiredTs = toTs(m.expired);
      const expiredSoon = expiredTs && expiredTs < Date.now();
      if (String(editing) === String(m.userId)) {
        return `<tr class="bg-sky-50/40 border-b border-slate-100">
          <td class="py-3 pr-4"><div class="font-semibold text-slate-800">${esc(m.name || m.email)}</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-400">${esc(m.email)}</div></td>
          <td class="py-3 pr-4"><select name="roleName" form="edit-member" class="rounded-lg border-slate-200 text-sm">${MEMBER_ROLES.map((r) => `<option ${r === m.role ? 'selected' : ''}>${r}</option>`).join('')}</select></td>
          <td class="py-3 pr-4"><input name="expired" form="edit-member" type="date" value="${toDateInput(m.expired)}" class="rounded-lg border-slate-200 text-sm"></td>
          <td class="py-3 text-right whitespace-nowrap">${button('LÆ°u', { type: 'submit', size: 'sm', attrs: 'form="edit-member"' })} ${button('Huá»·', { act: 'cancel', tone: 'secondary', size: 'sm' })}</td>
        </tr>`;
      }
      return `<tr class="border-b border-slate-50">
        <td class="py-3 pr-4">
          <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-sky-100 text-sky-700 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs shrink-0">${esc(initials(m.name || m.email))}</div>
            <div class="min-w-0"><div class="font-semibold text-slate-800">${esc(m.name || 'â€”')}${me ? ' <span class="text-xs text-slate-400 font-normal">(báº¡n)</span>' : ''}</div>
              <div class="text-xs text-slate-400">${esc(m.email)} Â· ID ${esc(m.userId)}</div></div>
          </div>
        </td>
        <td class="py-3 pr-4">${m.role ? badge(m.role, roleTone(m.role)) : 'â€”'}${m.state ? ` ${badge(m.state, 'slate')}` : ''}</td>
        <td class="py-3 pr-4 text-sm ${expiredSoon ? 'text-rose-600 font-semibold' : 'text-slate-600'}">${m.expired ? fmtDate(m.expired) : 'KhÃ´ng giá»›i háº¡n'}</td>
        <td class="py-3 text-right whitespace-nowrap">${isOwner(m.role) ? '<span class="text-xs text-slate-400">Chá»§ nhÃ </span>' : `
          ${button('', { act: 'edit', val: m.userId, tone: 'ghost', icon: 'edit', size: 'sm', attrs: 'title="Äá»•i vai trÃ² / gia háº¡n"' })}
          ${button('', { act: 'delete', val: m.userId, tone: 'danger', icon: 'person_remove', size: 'sm', attrs: 'title="XoÃ¡ khá»i nhÃ "' })}`}
        </td>
      </tr>`;
    };

    const paint = () => {
      let body;
      if (members === null) body = loadingBlock();
      else if (error && !members.length) body = errorBlock(error);
      else if (!members.length) body = emptyState('group', 'ChÆ°a cÃ³ thÃ nh viÃªn');
      else {
        body = `<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-sm">
          <thead><tr class="text-left text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 border-b border-slate-100">
            <th class="py-2 pr-4 font-semibold">ThÃ nh viÃªn</th><th class="py-2 pr-4 font-semibold">Vai trÃ²</th><th class="py-2 pr-4 font-semibold">Háº¿t háº¡n</th><th></th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>${members.map(row).join('')}</tbody></table></div>
          <form id="edit-member" data-id="${esc(editing ?? '')}"></form>`;
      }
      listEl.innerHTML = panel({ title: `Danh sÃ¡ch thÃ nh viÃªn${members ? ` (${members.length})` : ''}`, icon: 'group', body });
    };

    const load = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.members.list(homeId);
        if (!alive) return;
        members = asList(res, 'members').map(normMember);
        error = null;
      } catch (e) {
        if (!alive) return;
        members = members ?? [];
        error = `KhÃ´ng táº£i Ä‘Æ°á»£c thÃ nh viÃªn: ${e.message}`;
      }
      paint();
    };

    root.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
      const b = e.target.closest('[data-act]');
      if (!b) return;
      const { act, val } = b.dataset;
      const m = members?.find((x) => String(x.userId) === String(val));
      if (act === 'reload') return busy(b, load);
      if (act === 'edit') { editing = val; return paint(); }
      if (act === 'cancel') { editing = null; return paint(); }
      if (act === 'delete' && m) {
        if (!confirm(`XoÃ¡ ${m.name || m.email} khá»i nhÃ ?`)) return;
        await busy(b, async () => {
          try { await api.members.remove(homeId, m.userId); toast('ÄÃ£ xoÃ¡ thÃ nh viÃªn', 'emerald'); await load(); }
          catch (err) { fail(err, 'KhÃ´ng xoÃ¡ Ä‘Æ°á»£c'); }
        });
      }
    });

    root.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const f = e.target;
      const v = formValues(f);
      const btn = f.id === 'edit-member' ? root.querySelector('[form="edit-member"][type=submit]') : f.querySelector('[type=submit]');
      if (f.id === 'add-member') {
        if (!/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(v.email)) return toast('Email khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡', 'rose');
        await busy(btn, async () => {
          try {
            const body = { email: v.email, roleName: v.roleName };
            if (v.expired) body.expired = v.expired;
            await api.members.add(homeId, body);
            f.reset();
            toast('ÄÃ£ thÃªm thÃ nh viÃªn', 'emerald');
            await load();
          } catch (err) { fail(err, 'KhÃ´ng thÃªm Ä‘Æ°á»£c thÃ nh viÃªn'); }
        });
      } else if (f.id === 'edit-member') {
        await busy(btn, async () => {
          try {
            await api.members.update(homeId, f.dataset.id, { roleName: v.roleName, expired: v.expired || null });
            toast('ÄÃ£ cáº­p nháº­t thÃ nh viÃªn', 'emerald');
            editing = null;
            await load();
          } catch (err) { fail(err, 'KhÃ´ng cáº­p nháº­t Ä‘Æ°á»£c'); }
        });
      }
    });

    paint();
    load();
    return () => { alive = false; };
  },
};

        el.innerHTML = infoBlock('NhÃ  nÃ y chÆ°a Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ cáº£m biáº¿n nÃ o. VÃ o <a href="#/settings" class="font-semibold underline">CÃ i Ä‘áº·t â†’ Cáº£m biáº¿n IoT</a> Ä‘á»ƒ thÃªm cáº£m biáº¿n nhiá»‡t Ä‘á»™, Ä‘á»™ áº©m, Ã¡nh sÃ¡ng, chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»™ng.');
      } else if (!state.loading && !state.socketConnected) {
        el.innerHTML = infoBlock('Äang máº¥t káº¿t ná»‘i Socket.IO â€” sá»‘ liá»‡u Ä‘Æ°á»£c lÃ m má»›i qua REST má»—i 30 giÃ¢y.', 'amber');
      } else el.innerHTML = '';
    };
    const paintLive = () => {
      renderMetricCards($('#metric-cards'), state.sensor, state.sensors);
      renderLcdPanel($('#lcd-panel'), state.sensor, state.socketConnected);
      renderStatusBar($('#status-bar'), state);
      paintNotice();
    };
    const paintChart = () => renderTrendChart($('#trend-chart'), state.history);

    paintLive();
    paintChart();
    const offs = [
      ...['sensor', 'sensors', 'devices', 'alerts', 'socket', 'loading'].map((e) => on(e, paintLive)),
      on('history', paintChart),
    ];
    return () => offs.forEach((off) => off());
  },
};



==================== FILE: C:\Users\Dat\OneDrive\Documents\YoloHome\Frontend\js\pages\notifications.js ====================

// ThÃ´ng bÃ¡o: GET /notifications?status=Unread|Read, PATCH /notifications/:id/read
import { state, refreshNotifications } from '../store.js';
import { api } from '../api.js';
import { asList, esc, fullTime } from '../utils/fields.js';
import { normNotification } from '../models.js';
import { panel, pageTitle, badge, segmented, button, emptyState, loadingBlock, errorBlock, toast, busy, fail } from '../ui.js';

let filter = 'Unread';

export const notificationsPage = {
  mount(root) {
    let items = null, error = null, alive = true;
    root.innerHTML = pageTitle('ThÃ´ng bÃ¡o', 'ThÃ´ng bÃ¡o gá»­i tá»›i tÃ i khoáº£n cá»§a báº¡n â€” báº¥m vÃ o thÃ´ng bÃ¡o Ä‘á»ƒ Ä‘Ã¡nh dáº¥u Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘á»c',
      button('Táº£i láº¡i', { act: 'reload', tone: 'secondary', icon: 'refresh' })) + '<div id="notif-box"></div>';
    const box = root.querySelector('#notif-box');

    const paint = () => {
      let body;
      if (items === null) body = loadingBlock();
      else if (error) body = errorBlock(error);
      else if (!items.length) body = emptyState('notifications_off', filter === 'Unread' ? 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ thÃ´ng bÃ¡o chÆ°a Ä‘á»c' : 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ thÃ´ng bÃ¡o');
      else {
        body = `<div class="divide-y divide-slate-100">${items.map((n) => `
          <div data-act="${n.read ? '' : 'read'}" data-val="${esc(n.id)}" class="flex items-start gap-3 py-3 px-2 -mx-2 rounded-lg ${n.read ? '' : 'cursor-pointer hover:bg-sky-50/60'}">
            <span class="mt-1.5 w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0 ${n.read ? 'bg-slate-200' : 'bg-sky-500'}"></span>
            <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                <span class="text-sm ${n.read ? 'text-slate-600' : 'font-semibold text-slate-900'}">${esc(n.title)}</span>
                ${badge(n.read ? 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘á»c' : 'ChÆ°a Ä‘á»c', n.read ? 'slate' : 'sky')}
              </div>
              ${n.content ? `<div class="text-sm text-slate-600 mt-0.5 break-words">${esc(n.content)}</div>` : ''}
              <div class="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400 mt-1">${fullTime(n.ts)}</div>
            </div>
          </div>`).join('')}</div>`;
      }
      const unreadHere = items?.filter((n) => !n.read).length ?? 0;
      box.innerHTML = panel({
        title: `Há»™p thÃ´ng bÃ¡o${items ? ` (${items.length})` : ''}`, icon: 'notifications',
        right: `<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
          ${unreadHere ? button('ÄÃ¡nh dáº¥u táº¥t cáº£ Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘á»c', { act: 'read-all', tone: 'secondary', size: 'sm', icon: 'done_all' }) : ''}
          ${segmented([['Unread', `ChÆ°a Ä‘á»c${state.unread ? ` (${state.unread})` : ''}`], ['Read', 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘á»c'], ['All', 'Táº¥t cáº£']], filter, 'filter')}</div>`,
        body,
      });
    };

    const load = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.notifications.list(filter === 'All' ? undefined : filter);
        if (!alive) return;
        items = asList(res, 'notifications').map(normNotification).sort((a, b) => (b.ts ?? 0) - (a.ts ?? 0));
        // PhÃ²ng khi BE bá» qua tham sá»‘ lá»c
        if (filter === 'Unread') items = items.filter((n) => !n.read);
        if (filter === 'Read') items = items.filter((n) => n.read);
        error = null;
      } catch (e) {
        if (!alive) return;
        items = [];
        error = `KhÃ´ng táº£i Ä‘Æ°á»£c thÃ´ng bÃ¡o: ${e.message}`;
      }
      paint();
    };

    const markRead = async (n) => {
      await api.notifications.markRead(n.id);
      n.read = true;
      n.status = 'Read';
    };

    root.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
      const b = e.target.closest('[data-act]');
      if (!b || !b.dataset.act) return;
      const { act, val } = b.dataset;
      if (act === 'filter') { filter = val; items = null; paint(); return load(); }
      if (act === 'reload') { items = null; paint(); await busy(b, load); return refreshNotifications(); }
      if (act === 'read') {
        const n = items?.find((x) => String(x.id) === String(val));
        if (!n) return;
        try {
          await markRead(n);
          if (filter === 'Unread') items = items.filter((x) => x !== n);
          paint();
          refreshNotifications();
        } catch (err) { fail(err, 'KhÃ´ng Ä‘Ã¡nh dáº¥u Ä‘Æ°á»£c'); }
      }
      if (act === 'read-all') {
        await busy(b, async () => {
          const unread = items.filter((n) => !n.read);
          const results = await Promise.allSettled(unread.map(markRead));
          const failed = results.filter((r) => r.status === 'rejected').length;
          if (failed) toast(`${failed} thÃ´ng bÃ¡o chÆ°a Ä‘Ã¡nh dáº¥u Ä‘Æ°á»£c`, 'amber'); else toast('ÄÃ£ Ä‘Ã¡nh dáº¥u táº¥t cáº£ lÃ  Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘á»c', 'emerald');
          if (filter === 'Unread') items = items.filter((n) => !n.read);
          paint();
          refreshNotifications();
        });
      }
    });

    paint();
    load();
    return () => { alive = false; };
  },
};

          return `<tr class="border-b border-slate-50">

    root.addEventListener('change', (e) => {

    root.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {

    root.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {

      if (f.id === 'thresholds') {

        for (const [k] of FIELDS) vals[k] = parseFloat(v[k]);
        if (Object.values(vals).some((n) => !Number.isFinite(n))) return toast('Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p sá»‘ há»£p lá»‡', 'rose');
        if (vals.HUMIDITY_MIN >= vals.HUMIDITY_MAX) return toast('Äá»™ áº©m tá»‘i thiá»ƒu pháº£i nhá» hÆ¡n tá»‘i Ä‘a', 'rose');
        if (vals.HUMIDITY_MIN < 0 || vals.HUMIDITY_MAX > 100) return toast('Äá»™ áº©m pháº£i trong khoáº£ng 0â€“100%', 'rose');

      } else if (f.id === 'add-sensor') {
        if (!v.deviceId) return toast('Cáº§n chá»n thiáº¿t bá»‹ â€” hÃ£y thÃªm thiáº¿t bá»‹ trÆ°á»›c', 'rose');
        await busy(btn, async () => {
          try {
            await api.iot.addSensor(state.homeId, { deviceId: idValue(v.deviceId), dataType: v.dataType, unit: v.unit });
            toast('ÄÃ£ Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ cáº£m biáº¿n', 'emerald');
            await refreshHomeData({ silent: true });
          } catch (err) { fail(err, 'KhÃ´ng Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ Ä‘Æ°á»£c cáº£m biáº¿n'); }
        });
      } else if (f.id === 'telemetry') {
        const value = parseFloat(v.value);
        if (!v.sensorId) return toast('ChÆ°a cÃ³ cáº£m biáº¿n', 'rose');
        if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return toast('GiÃ¡ trá»‹ pháº£i lÃ  sá»‘', 'rose');
        await busy(btn, async () => {
          try {
            await api.iot.pushTelemetry(state.homeId, idValue(v.sensorId), value, v.dataQuality);
            toast('ÄÃ£ gá»­i giÃ¡ trá»‹ â€” sensor_update sáº½ cáº­p nháº­t dashboard', 'emerald');
            f.querySelector('[name=value]').value = '';
            setTimeout(() => refreshHomeData({ silent: true }), 600);
          } catch (err) { fail(err, 'KhÃ´ng gá»­i Ä‘Æ°á»£c'); }
        });
      }
    });

    paintRules();
    paintSocket();
    paintSensorBox();
    const offs = [
      on('socket', paintSocket),
      on('sensor', paintSensorTable),
      onChange(['sensors', 'devices', 'loading'], () => JSON.stringify([state.sensors.map((s) => [s.id, s.dataType, s.unit]), state.devices.map((d) => [d.id, d.name]), state.errors.sensors]), () => {
        paintSensorTable();
        const dev = $('#sensor-device'), tele = $('#tele-sensor');
        if (dev) { const keep = dev.value; dev.innerHTML = deviceOptions(); if ([...dev.options].some((o) => o.value === keep)) dev.value = keep; }
        if (tele) { const keep = tele.value; tele.innerHTML = sensorOptions(); if ([...tele.options].some((o) => o.value === keep)) tele.value = keep; }
      }),
    ];
    return () => offs.forEach((off) => off());
  },
};



==================== FILE: C:\Users\Dat\OneDrive\Documents\YoloHome\Frontend\js\services\faceEngine.js ====================

// TrÃ­ch vector Ä‘áº·c trÆ°ng khuÃ´n máº·t 128 chiá»u tá»« webcam báº±ng face-api.js (@vladmandic/face-api).
// Backend so khá»›p báº±ng khoáº£ng cÃ¡ch Euclid (< 0.6) â€” Ä‘Ãºng chuáº©n descriptor cá»§a face-api.js.
// ThÆ° viá»‡n + mÃ´ hÃ¬nh (~8 MB) chá»‰ táº£i khi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng báº¥m "Láº¥y vector" láº§n Ä‘áº§u.
const VERSION = '1.7.15';
const BASE = `https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@vladmandic/face-api@${VERSION}`;

let loading = null;

export function loadFaceApi() {
  loading ??= (async () => {
    const faceapi = await import(`${BASE}/dist/face-api.esm.js`);
    await faceapi.tf?.ready?.();
    await Promise.all([
      faceapi.nets.tinyFaceDetector.loadFromUri(`${BASE}/model`),
      faceapi.nets.faceLandmark68Net.loadFromUri(`${BASE}/model`),
      faceapi.nets.faceRecognitionNet.loadFromUri(`${BASE}/model`),
    ]);
    return faceapi;
  })().catch((e) => { loading = null; throw e; });
  return loading;
}

// Tráº£ vá» máº£ng 128 sá»‘ thá»±c, hoáº·c null náº¿u khÃ´ng tháº¥y khuÃ´n máº·t.
export async function extractDescriptor(video) {
  const faceapi = await loadFaceApi();
  const result = await faceapi
    .detectSingleFace(video, new faceapi.TinyFaceDetectorOptions({ inputSize: 320, scoreThreshold: 0.5 }))
    .withFaceLandmarks()
    .withFaceDescriptor();
  if (!result) return null;
  return Array.from(result.descriptor, (v) => Math.round(v * 1e6) / 1e6);
}

export async function startCamera(video) {
  if (!navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) throw new Error('TrÃ¬nh duyá»‡t khÃ´ng há»— trá»£ camera (cáº§n HTTPS hoáº·c localhost)');
  const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ video: { width: 640, height: 480, facingMode: 'user' }, audio: false });
  video.srcObject = stream;
  await video.play().catch(() => {});
  return stream;
}

export function stopCamera(stream, video) {
  stream?.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
  if (video) video.srcObject = null;
}

// Äá»c vector ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng dÃ¡n vÃ o: JSON "[0.1, 0.2, ...]" hoáº·c "0.1, 0.2, ...".
export function parseVector(text) {
  const s = String(text ?? '').trim();
  if (!s) return null;
  let arr;
  try { arr = JSON.parse(s.startsWith('[') ? s : `[${s}]`); } catch { return null; }
  if (!Array.isArray(arr) || !arr.length || !arr.every((x) => typeof x === 'number' && Number.isFinite(x))) return null;
  return arr;
}



==================== FILE: C:\Users\Dat\OneDrive\Documents\YoloHome\Frontend\js\services\syncService.js ====================

import { TIMING } from '../config.js';

const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
export const formatTime = (d) => `${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}:${pad(d.getSeconds())}`;
export const formatDate = (d) => `${pad(d.getDate())}/${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}`;

// Cháº¡y toÃ n cá»¥c: Ä‘áº¿m ngÆ°á»£c 30s -> onSync (lÃ m má»›i qua REST), cáº­p nháº­t Ä‘á»“ng há»“ LCD náº¿u Ä‘ang hiá»ƒn thá»‹.
// Sá»‘ liá»‡u cáº£m biáº¿n Ä‘áº¿n tá»©c thá»i qua Socket.IO; chu ká»³ REST chá»‰ Ä‘á»ƒ bÃ¹ khi socket rá»›t.
export function startSyncService({ onSync } = {}) {
  let countdown = TIMING.SYNC_INTERVAL_S;
  const paint = () => {
    const el = document.getElementById('sync-timer');
    if (el) el.textContent = `${countdown}s`;
  };
  const sync = () => { countdown = TIMING.SYNC_INTERVAL_S; paint(); onSync?.(); };

  setInterval(() => {
    countdown--;
    if (countdown <= 0) sync(); else paint();
    const clock = document.getElementById('lcd-clock');
    if (clock) clock.textContent = formatTime(new Date());
  }, 1000);

  document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    const btn = e.target.closest('#manual-sync-btn');
    if (!btn) return;
    sync();
    btn.classList.add('opacity-70');
    setTimeout(() => btn.classList.remove('opacity-70'), 400);
  });
}



==================== FILE: C:\Users\Dat\OneDrive\Documents\YoloHome\Frontend\js\utils\fields.js ====================

// Äá»c dá»¯ liá»‡u Backend theo kiá»ƒu "chá»‹u lá»—i": khÃ´ng phá»¥ thuá»™c cÃ¡ch Ä‘áº·t tÃªn trÆ°á»ng
// (homeId / home_id / Home_ID Ä‘á»u khá»›p) vÃ  khÃ´ng phá»¥ thuá»™c cÃ¡ch bá»c pháº£n há»“i
// ([...], { data: [...] }, { homes: [...] }, { success, data: {...} }).

const normKey = (k) => String(k).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');

// Láº¥y giÃ¡ trá»‹ Ä‘áº§u tiÃªn khÃ¡c rá»—ng theo danh sÃ¡ch tÃªn trÆ°á»ng.
export function pick(obj, ...names) {
  if (!obj || typeof obj !== 'object') return undefined;
  const map = new Map();
  for (const k of Object.keys(obj)) {
    const n = normKey(k);
    if (!map.has(n)) map.set(n, obj[k]);
  }
  for (const name of names) {
    const v = map.get(normKey(name));
    if (v !== undefined && v !== null && v !== '') return v;
  }
  return undefined;
}

// TrÃ­ch máº£ng tá»« pháº£n há»“i.
export function asList(res, ...keys) {
  if (Array.isArray(res)) return res;
  if (!res || typeof res !== 'object') return [];
  for (const k of [...keys, 'data', 'items', 'results', 'rows', 'list']) {
    const v = pick(res, k);
    if (Array.isArray(v)) return v;
    if (v && typeof v === 'object') {
      const inner = asList(v, ...keys);
      if (inner.length) return inner;
    }
  }
  return Object.values(res).find(Array.isArray) ?? [];
}

// TrÃ­ch object tá»« pháº£n há»“i.
export function asObject(res, ...keys) {
  if (!res || typeof res !== 'object' || Array.isArray(res)) return null;
  for (const k of [...keys, 'data', 'result']) {
    const v = pick(res, k);
    if (v && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v)) return asObject(v, ...keys) ?? v;
  }
  return res;
}

// TÃ¬m giÃ¡ trá»‹ nguyÃªn thuá»· theo tÃªn á»Ÿ má»i táº§ng (vd. token náº±m trong data.token).
export function deepFind(obj, names, depth = 4) {
  if (!obj || typeof obj !== 'object' || depth < 0) return undefined;
  const direct = pick(obj, ...names);
  if (direct !== undefined && typeof direct !== 'object') return direct;
  for (const v of Object.values(obj)) {
    const found = deepFind(v, names, depth - 1);
    if (found !== undefined) return found;
  }
  return undefined;
}

// Chuyá»ƒn giÃ¡ trá»‹ (cÃ³ thá»ƒ lÃ  object lá»“ng, vd. role: { roleName }) thÃ nh chuá»—i hiá»ƒn thá»‹.
export function label(v) {
  if (v === undefined || v === null) return '';
  if (typeof v === 'object') {
    const inner = pick(v, 'name', 'displayName', 'roleName', 'typeCode', 'code', 'title', 'label', 'value');
    return inner !== undefined && typeof inner !== 'object' ? String(inner) : JSON.stringify(v);
  }
  return String(v);
}

export function toTs(v) {
  if (v === undefined || v === null || v === '') return null;
  if (v instanceof Date) return v.getTime();
  if (typeof v === 'number' || /^\d+$/.test(String(v))) {
    const n = Number(v);
    return n < 1e12 ? n * 1000 : n;          // giÃ¢y hoáº·c mili-giÃ¢y
  }
  const t = Date.parse(v);
  return Number.isFinite(t) ? t : null;
}

export const esc = (s) => String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => (
  { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));

export const clamp = (v, a, b) => Math.min(b, Math.max(a, v));
export const normText = (s) => String(s ?? '').normalize('NFD').replace(/[Ì€-Í¯]/g, '')
  .replace(/[Ä‘Ä]/g, 'd').toLowerCase().trim();

const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
export const hhmm = (ts) => { const d = new Date(ts); return `${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}`; };
export const fullTime = (ts) => {
  if (ts == null) return 'â€”';
  const d = new Date(ts);
  return `${pad(d.getDate())}/${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}/${d.getFullYear()} ${hhmm(ts)}:${pad(d.getSeconds())}`;
};
export const fmtDate = (v) => {
  const t = toTs(v);
  if (t == null) return v ? String(v) : 'â€”';
  const d = new Date(t);
  return `${pad(d.getDate())}/${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}/${d.getFullYear()}`;
};
export const toDateInput = (v) => {
  const t = toTs(v);
  if (t == null) return '';
  const d = new Date(t);
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}`;
};
// Chuá»—i sá»‘ â†’ number (giá»¯ nguyÃªn náº¿u khÃ´ng pháº£i sá»‘), Ä‘á»ƒ gá»­i id Ä‘Ãºng kiá»ƒu cho BE.
export const idValue = (v) => (v !== '' && v != null && /^\d+$/.test(String(v)) ? Number(v) : v);

// TÃ¬m trong pháº§n dá»¯ liá»‡u Ä‘Ã£ bÃ³c ({ data: {...} }) trÆ°á»›c, rá»“i tá»›i cáº¥p ngoÃ i cÃ¹ng cá»§a pháº£n há»“i.
export function pickAny(res, ...names) {
  const inner = asObject(res);
  const v = inner && inner !== res ? pick(inner, ...names) : undefined;
  return v !== undefined ? v : pick(res, ...names);
}

import { THRESHOLDS } from '../config.js';

// Khai bÃ¡o Ä‘á»§ class Tailwind Ä‘á»ƒ dá»… tra cá»©u vÃ  trÃ¡nh ná»‘i chuá»—i Ä‘á»™ng.
export const TONES = {
  emerald: { badge: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700', text: 'text-emerald-600' },
  sky:     { badge: 'bg-sky-50 text-sky-700',         text: 'text-sky-600' },
  amber:   { badge: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700',     text: 'text-amber-600' },
  rose:    { badge: 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700',       text: 'text-rose-600' },
  slate:   { badge: 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600',    text: 'text-slate-600' },

export function getTempStatus(t) {
  if (t == null) return NO_DATA;
  return t > THRESHOLDS.TEMP_MAX
    ? { label: 'VÆ°á»£t ngÆ°á»¡ng', tone: 'rose' }
    : { label: 'BÃ¬nh thÆ°á»ng', tone: 'emerald' };
}

export function getHumidityStatus(h) {
  if (h == null) return NO_DATA;
  const ok = h >= THRESHOLDS.HUMIDITY_MIN && h <= THRESHOLDS.HUMIDITY_MAX;
  return ok ? { label: 'LÃ½ tÆ°á»Ÿng', tone: 'sky' } : { label: 'NgoÃ i chuáº©n', tone: 'amber' };
}

export function getLightStatus(lux) {
  if (lux == null) return { ...NO_DATA, lamp: 'â€”' };
  return lux >= THRESHOLDS.LIGHT_MIN
    ? { label: 'Äá»§ sÃ¡ng', tone: 'amber', lamp: `ÄÃ¨n táº¯t (â‰¥${THRESHOLDS.LIGHT_MIN}L)` }
    : { label: 'Thiáº¿u sÃ¡ng', tone: 'slate', lamp: 'ÄÃ¨n báº­t' };
}

USE smart_home;

-- Quy Æ°á»›c giÃ¡ trá»‹ tráº¡ng thÃ¡i (chá»¯ thÆ°á»ng):
--   Account_state : active | locked
--   Home_Member.Status : active | expired
--   Connection_State : online | offline
--   Lock_Unlock_State : locked | unlocked
--   Access_Result : success | failed        Verify_Method : pin | face | remote
--   Excute_State  : pending | success | failed
--   Source_Type   : user | voice | ai | system
--   Notification.Level : info | warning | critical
-- Data_attached cá»§a Device_Command cÃ³ dáº¡ng JSON, vÃ­ dá»¥ {"state":"ON"}

DELIMITER $$

-- BÄƒm PIN cÃ³ "muá»‘i" theo cá»­a + ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng (PIN khÃ´ng lÆ°u dáº¡ng rÃµ)
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS fn_hash_pin$$
CREATE FUNCTION fn_hash_pin(p_door VARCHAR(100), p_user_id INT, p_pin VARCHAR(100))
RETURNS CHAR(64) DETERMINISTIC NO SQL
BEGIN
    RETURN SHA2(CONCAT(p_door, ':', p_user_id, ':', p_pin), 256);
END$$

DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS fn_is_valid_email$$
CREATE FUNCTION fn_is_valid_email(p_email VARCHAR(150))
RETURNS TINYINT(1) DETERMINISTIC NO SQL
BEGIN
    RETURN p_email REGEXP '^[^@ ]+@[^@ ]+[.][^@ ]+$';
END$$

-- NgÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng lÃ  thÃ nh viÃªn cÃ²n hiá»‡u lá»±c cá»§a nhÃ ?
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS fn_is_home_member$$
CREATE FUNCTION fn_is_home_member(p_user_id INT, p_home_id INT)
RETURNS TINYINT(1) READS SQL DATA
BEGIN
    RETURN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM Home_Member
                   WHERE User_ID = p_user_id AND Home_ID = p_home_id
                     AND LOWER(`Status`) = 'active'
                     AND (Expired IS NULL OR Expired > NOW()));
END$$

-- Vai trÃ² hiá»‡n táº¡i cá»§a ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng trong nhÃ  (NULL náº¿u khÃ´ng pháº£i thÃ nh viÃªn há»£p lá»‡)
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS fn_get_home_role$$
CREATE FUNCTION fn_get_home_role(p_user_id INT, p_home_id INT)
RETURNS VARCHAR(50) READS SQL DATA
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_role VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT NULL;
    SELECT r.Role_Name INTO v_role
    FROM Home_Member hm JOIN Role r ON r.Role_ID = hm.Role_ID
    WHERE hm.User_ID = p_user_id AND hm.Home_ID = p_home_id
      AND LOWER(hm.`Status`) = 'active'
      AND (hm.Expired IS NULL OR hm.Expired > NOW())
    LIMIT 1;
    RETURN v_role;
END$$

-- Kiá»ƒm tra quyá»n theo permission_code (Role -> Role_Permission -> Permission)
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS fn_has_permission$$
CREATE FUNCTION fn_has_permission(p_user_id INT, p_home_id INT, p_permission_code VARCHAR(50))
RETURNS TINYINT(1) READS SQL DATA
BEGIN
    RETURN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM Home_Member hm
        JOIN Role_Permission rp ON rp.Role_ID = hm.Role_ID
        JOIN Permission p       ON p.Permission_ID = rp.Permission_ID
        WHERE hm.User_ID = p_user_id AND hm.Home_ID = p_home_id
          AND LOWER(hm.`Status`) = 'active'
          AND (hm.Expired IS NULL OR hm.Expired > NOW())
          AND p.Permission_Code = p_permission_code);
END$$

-- XÃ¡c thá»±c PIN: tráº£ vá» User_ID sá»Ÿ há»¯u PIN há»£p lá»‡, NULL náº¿u sai/háº¿t háº¡n/bá»‹ thu há»“i
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS fn_verify_pin$$
CREATE FUNCTION fn_verify_pin(p_door VARCHAR(100), p_pin VARCHAR(100))
RETURNS INT READS SQL DATA
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_user INT DEFAULT NULL;
    SELECT User_ID INTO v_user
    FROM Access_Credential
    WHERE Smart_Door_Name = p_door
      AND LOWER(Credential_State) = 'active'
      AND (Valid_From IS NULL OR Valid_From <= NOW())
      AND (Valid_To   IS NULL OR Valid_To   >= NOW())
      AND Credential_Value = fn_hash_pin(p_door, User_ID, p_pin)
    LIMIT 1;
    RETURN v_user;
END$$

-- Sá»‘ láº§n truy cáº­p tháº¥t báº¡i gáº§n Ä‘Ã¢y (khÃ´ng tÃ­nh cÃ¡c láº§n bá»‹ khÃ³a táº¡m)
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS fn_count_failed_attempts$$
CREATE FUNCTION fn_count_failed_attempts(p_door VARCHAR(100), p_minutes INT)
RETURNS INT READS SQL DATA
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_cnt INT DEFAULT 0;
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_cnt
    FROM Access_Attempt
    WHERE Smart_Door_Name = p_door
      AND LOWER(Access_Result) = 'failed'
      AND (Failure_Reason IS NULL OR Failure_Reason <> 'locked_out')
      AND `Time` >= NOW() - INTERVAL p_minutes MINUTE;
    RETURN v_cnt;
END$$

-- GiÃ¡ trá»‹ Ä‘o má»›i nháº¥t cá»§a cáº£m biáº¿n
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS fn_latest_sensor_value$$
CREATE FUNCTION fn_latest_sensor_value(p_sensor_id INT)
RETURNS DOUBLE READS SQL DATA
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_val DOUBLE DEFAULT NULL;
    SELECT `Value` INTO v_val 
    FROM Sensor_Record
    WHERE Sensor_ID = p_sensor_id 
    ORDER BY Recorded_At DESC, Record_ID DESC 
    LIMIT 1;
    RETURN v_val;
END$$

DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS fn_unread_notifications$$
CREATE FUNCTION fn_unread_notifications(p_user_id INT)
RETURNS INT READS SQL DATA
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_cnt INT DEFAULT 0;
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_cnt FROM Notification
    WHERE User_ID = p_user_id AND Unread_Read = 'Unread';
    RETURN v_cnt;
END$$

-- XÃ¡c Ä‘á»‹nh ngÆ°á»i yÃªu cáº§u cá»§a má»™t lá»‡nh thiáº¿t bá»‹ (user -> Source_ID, voice -> chá»§ VoiceCommand)
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS fn_command_requester$$
CREATE FUNCTION fn_command_requester(p_source_type VARCHAR(50), p_source_id INT)
RETURNS INT READS SQL DATA
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_user INT DEFAULT NULL;
    IF LOWER(p_source_type) = 'user' THEN
        SET v_user = p_source_id;
    ELSEIF LOWER(p_source_type) = 'voice' THEN
        SELECT User_ID INTO v_user FROM Voice_Command WHERE Voice_Command_ID = p_source_id;
    END IF;
    RETURN v_user;
END$$

DELIMITER ;

-- Quy Æ°á»›c giÃ¡ trá»‹ tráº¡ng thÃ¡i (chá»¯ thÆ°á»ng):

DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_notify$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_notify(IN p_user_id INT, IN p_title VARCHAR(150), IN p_type VARCHAR(50),
                           IN p_content TEXT, IN p_level VARCHAR(30))
BEGIN
    IF p_user_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        INSERT INTO Notification (Title, `Type`, Content, `Level`, User_ID)
        VALUES (p_title, p_type, p_content, p_level, p_user_id);
    END IF;
END$$
 
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_notify_home_owner$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_notify_home_owner(IN p_home_id INT, IN p_title VARCHAR(150), IN p_type VARCHAR(50),
                                      IN p_content TEXT, IN p_level VARCHAR(30))
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_owner INT DEFAULT NULL;
    SELECT Owner_ID INTO v_owner FROM Home WHERE Home_ID = p_home_id;
    CALL sp_notify(v_owner, p_title, p_type, p_content, p_level);
END$$

DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_log_activity$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_log_activity(IN p_user_id INT, IN p_home_id INT, IN p_entity_type VARCHAR(50),
                                 IN p_action VARCHAR(100), IN p_source VARCHAR(50), IN p_result VARCHAR(50))
BEGIN
    INSERT INTO Activity_Log (Entity_Type, `Action`, `Source`, `Result`, User_ID, Home_ID)
    VALUES (p_entity_type, p_action, p_source, p_result, p_user_id, p_home_id);
END$$

-- Thu há»“i PIN vÃ  vÃ´ hiá»‡u hÃ³a khuÃ´n máº·t cá»§a ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng trong má»™t nhÃ 
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_revoke_member_access$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_revoke_member_access(IN p_user_id INT, IN p_home_id INT)
BEGIN
    UPDATE Access_Credential ac
    JOIN Smart_Door sd ON sd.Smart_Door_Name = ac.Smart_Door_Name
    SET ac.Credential_State = 'revoked'
    WHERE ac.User_ID = p_user_id AND sd.Home_ID = p_home_id
      AND LOWER(ac.Credential_State) = 'active';
 
    UPDATE Face_Profile
    SET Valid_To = NOW()
    WHERE User_ID = p_user_id AND Home_ID = p_home_id
      AND (Valid_From IS NULL OR Valid_From <= NOW())
      AND (Valid_To IS NULL OR Valid_To > NOW());
END$$

 
-- ===== TÃ i khoáº£n (6.1) =====
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_register_user$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_register_user(IN p_name VARCHAR(100), IN p_email VARCHAR(150),
                                  IN p_password_hash VARCHAR(255), IN p_display_name VARCHAR(100),
                                  OUT p_user_id INT)
BEGIN
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM `User` WHERE Email = LOWER(TRIM(p_email))) THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'Email Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½';
    END IF;
    INSERT INTO `User` (`Name`, Email, `Password`, Display_name, Account_state)
    VALUES (p_name, p_email, p_password_hash, p_display_name, 'active');
    SET p_user_id = LAST_INSERT_ID();
END$$
 
-- Chá»‰ so khá»›p mÃ£ bÄƒm do á»©ng dá»¥ng gá»­i lÃªn. Náº¿u dÃ¹ng bcrypt/argon2 thÃ¬ láº¥y hash theo email
-- rá»“i xÃ¡c thá»±c á»Ÿ táº§ng á»©ng dá»¥ng.
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_authenticate_user$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_authenticate_user(IN p_email VARCHAR(150), IN p_password_hash VARCHAR(255),
                                      OUT p_user_id INT, OUT p_result VARCHAR(20))
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_id INT DEFAULT NULL;
    DECLARE v_pw VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT NULL;
    DECLARE v_state VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT NULL;
 
    SELECT User_ID, `Password`, Account_state INTO v_id, v_pw, v_state
    FROM `User` WHERE Email = LOWER(TRIM(p_email));
 
    SET p_user_id = NULL;
    IF v_id IS NULL OR v_pw <> p_password_hash THEN
        SET p_result = 'invalid';
    ELSEIF LOWER(v_state) <> 'active' THEN
        SET p_result = 'locked';
    ELSE
        SET p_result = 'success';
        SET p_user_id = v_id;
    END IF;
END$$
 
-- ===== Home (6.2) =====
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_create_home$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_create_home(IN p_owner_id INT, IN p_home_name VARCHAR(100),
                                IN p_address VARCHAR(255), OUT p_home_id INT)
BEGIN
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM `User` WHERE User_ID = p_owner_id AND LOWER(Account_state) = 'active') THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'TÃ i khoáº£n khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i hoáº·c khÃ´ng hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng';
    END IF;
    -- trigger trg_home_ai tá»± thÃªm chá»§ nhÃ  vÃ o Home_Member vá»›i vai trÃ² Owner
    INSERT INTO Home (Home_Name, Address, `State`, Owner_ID)
    VALUES (TRIM(p_home_name), p_address, 'active', p_owner_id);
    SET p_home_id = LAST_INSERT_ID();
    CALL sp_log_activity(p_owner_id, p_home_id, 'home', 'create_home', 'web', 'success');
END$$
 
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_update_home$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_update_home(IN p_actor_id INT, IN p_home_id INT, IN p_home_name VARCHAR(100),
                                IN p_address VARCHAR(255), IN p_state VARCHAR(30))
BEGIN
    IF NOT fn_has_permission(p_actor_id, p_home_id, 'HOME_UPDATE') THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n cáº­p nháº­t nhÃ ';
    END IF;
    UPDATE Home
    SET Home_Name = COALESCE(p_home_name, Home_Name),
        Address   = COALESCE(p_address, Address),
        `State`   = COALESCE(p_state, `State`)
    WHERE Home_ID = p_home_id;
    CALL sp_log_activity(p_actor_id, p_home_id, 'home', 'update_home', 'web', 'success');
END$$
 
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_delete_home$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_delete_home(IN p_actor_id INT, IN p_home_id INT)
BEGIN
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM Home WHERE Home_ID = p_home_id AND Owner_ID = p_actor_id) THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'Chá»‰ chá»§ nhÃ  má»›i Ä‘Æ°á»£c xÃ³a nhÃ ';
    END IF;
    DELETE FROM Home WHERE Home_ID = p_home_id;
END$$
 
-- ===== ThÃ nh viÃªn (6.3) =====
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_add_member$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_add_member(IN p_actor_id INT, IN p_home_id INT, IN p_target_email VARCHAR(150),
                               IN p_role_name VARCHAR(50), IN p_expired DATETIME)
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_target INT DEFAULT NULL;
    DECLARE v_role   INT DEFAULT NULL;
    DECLARE v_home_name VARCHAR(100);
 
    IF NOT fn_has_permission(p_actor_id, p_home_id, 'MEMBER_MANAGE') THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n quáº£n lÃ½ thÃ nh viÃªn';
    END IF;
    SELECT User_ID INTO v_target FROM `User`
    WHERE Email = LOWER(TRIM(p_target_email)) AND LOWER(Account_state) = 'active';
    IF v_target IS NULL THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y tÃ i khoáº£n ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng';
    END IF;
    SELECT Role_ID INTO v_role FROM Role WHERE Role_Name = p_role_name;
    IF v_role IS NULL THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'Vai trÃ² khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i';
    END IF;
    IF p_role_name = 'Owner' THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ gÃ¡n vai trÃ² Owner';
    END IF;
 
    -- thÃªm má»›i, hoáº·c kÃ­ch hoáº¡t láº¡i thÃ nh viÃªn Ä‘Ã£ háº¿t háº¡n / bá»‹ vÃ´ hiá»‡u
    INSERT INTO Home_Member (`Status`, Expired, User_ID, Home_ID, Role_ID)
    VALUES ('active', p_expired, v_target, p_home_id, v_role)
    ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE `Status` = 'active', Expired = p_expired, Role_ID = v_role, Joined = NOW();
 
    SELECT Home_Name INTO v_home_name FROM Home WHERE Home_ID = p_home_id;
    CALL sp_notify(v_target, 'Báº¡n Ä‘Æ°á»£c thÃªm vÃ o nhÃ ', 'member',
        CONCAT('Báº¡n Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c thÃªm vÃ o nhÃ  "', v_home_name, '" vá»›i vai trÃ² ', p_role_name, '.'), 'info');
    CALL sp_log_activity(p_actor_id, p_home_id, 'home_member', 'add_member', 'web', 'success');
END$$
 
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_change_member_role$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_change_member_role(IN p_actor_id INT, IN p_home_id INT, IN p_target_user_id INT,
                                       IN p_role_name VARCHAR(50), IN p_expired DATETIME)
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_role INT DEFAULT NULL;
    IF NOT fn_has_permission(p_actor_id, p_home_id, 'MEMBER_MANAGE') THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n quáº£n lÃ½ thÃ nh viÃªn';
    END IF;
    SELECT Role_ID INTO v_role FROM Role WHERE Role_Name = p_role_name;
    IF v_role IS NULL THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'Vai trÃ² khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i';
    END IF;
    UPDATE Home_Member SET Role_ID = v_role, Expired = p_expired
    WHERE User_ID = p_target_user_id AND Home_ID = p_home_id;
    IF ROW_COUNT() = 0 THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'NgÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng khÃ´ng pháº£i thÃ nh viÃªn cá»§a nhÃ ';
    END IF;
    CALL sp_log_activity(p_actor_id, p_home_id, 'home_member', 'change_role', 'web', 'success');
END$$
 
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_remove_member$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_remove_member(IN p_actor_id INT, IN p_home_id INT, IN p_target_user_id INT)
BEGIN
    IF NOT fn_has_permission(p_actor_id, p_home_id, 'MEMBER_MANAGE') THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n quáº£n lÃ½ thÃ nh viÃªn';
    END IF;
    DELETE FROM Home_Member WHERE User_ID = p_target_user_id AND Home_ID = p_home_id;
    IF ROW_COUNT() = 0 THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'NgÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng khÃ´ng pháº£i thÃ nh viÃªn cá»§a nhÃ ';
    END IF;
    CALL sp_log_activity(p_actor_id, p_home_id, 'home_member', 'remove_member', 'web', 'success');
END$$
 
-- ÄÃ¡nh dáº¥u háº¿t háº¡n cho khÃ¡ch/thÃ nh viÃªn cÃ³ Expired Ä‘Ã£ qua (gá»i Ä‘á»‹nh ká»³ báº±ng EVENT)
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_expire_members$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_expire_members()
BEGIN
    UPDATE Home_Member SET `Status` = 'expired'
    WHERE LOWER(`Status`) = 'active' AND Expired IS NOT NULL AND Expired <= NOW();
END$$
 
-- ===== Thiáº¿t bá»‹ (6.5) =====
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_add_device$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_add_device(IN p_actor_id INT, IN p_home_id INT, IN p_type_code VARCHAR(50),
                               IN p_device_name VARCHAR(100), IN p_hardware_id VARCHAR(100),
                               IN p_serial_no VARCHAR(100), IN p_controller_id INT, OUT p_device_id INT)
BEGIN
    IF NOT fn_has_permission(p_actor_id, p_home_id, 'DEVICE_MANAGE') THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n thÃªm thiáº¿t bá»‹';
    END IF;
    INSERT INTO Device (Device_Name, Hardware_ID, Serial_No, Available_working_State,
                        Home_ID, Type_Code, Controller_ID)
    VALUES (p_device_name, p_hardware_id, p_serial_no, 'enabled', p_home_id, p_type_code, p_controller_id);
    SET p_device_id = LAST_INSERT_ID();
    CALL sp_log_activity(p_actor_id, p_home_id, 'device', 'add_device', 'web', 'success');
END$$
 
-- Gá»­i lá»‡nh Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn (tá»« Web/App, giá»ng nÃ³i, AI...). Thiáº¿t bá»‹ offline -> lá»‡nh tháº¥t báº¡i ngay.
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_send_device_command$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_send_device_command(IN p_actor_id INT, IN p_device_id INT, IN p_source_type VARCHAR(50),
                                        IN p_source_id INT, IN p_data JSON, OUT p_command_id INT)
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_home INT DEFAULT NULL;
    DECLARE v_conn VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT NULL;
    DECLARE v_state VARCHAR(30);
 
    SELECT Home_ID, Connection_State INTO v_home, v_conn FROM Device WHERE Device_ID = p_device_id;
    IF v_home IS NULL THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'Thiáº¿t bá»‹ khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i';
    END IF;
    IF NOT fn_has_permission(p_actor_id, v_home, 'DEVICE_CONTROL') THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn thiáº¿t bá»‹';
    END IF;
 
    SET v_state = IF(LOWER(COALESCE(v_conn, 'offline')) = 'online', 'pending', 'failed');
    INSERT INTO Device_Command (Source_ID, Source_Type, Data_attached, Excute_State, Device_ID)
    VALUES (COALESCE(p_source_id, p_actor_id), p_source_type, p_data, v_state, p_device_id);
    SET p_command_id = LAST_INSERT_ID();
    CALL sp_log_activity(p_actor_id, v_home, 'device', 'send_command', p_source_type, v_state);
END$$
 
-- IoT Server / Yolo pháº£n há»“i káº¿t quáº£ thá»±c thi lá»‡nh (success | failed)
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_update_command_result$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_update_command_result(IN p_command_id INT, IN p_result VARCHAR(20))
BEGIN
    IF LOWER(p_result) NOT IN ('success', 'failed') THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'Káº¿t quáº£ pháº£i lÃ  success hoáº·c failed';
    END IF;
    UPDATE Device_Command SET Excute_State = LOWER(p_result) WHERE Command_ID = p_command_id;
    IF ROW_COUNT() = 0 THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y lá»‡nh';
    END IF;
END$$
 
-- ===== GiÃ¡m sÃ¡t mÃ´i trÆ°á»ng (6.4) =====
-- Yolo gá»­i dá»¯ liá»‡u: backend xÃ¡c Ä‘á»‹nh thiáº¿t bá»‹/cáº£m biáº¿n theo Hardware_ID + loáº¡i dá»¯ liá»‡u
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_ingest_sensor_data$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_ingest_sensor_data(IN p_hardware_id VARCHAR(100), IN p_data_type VARCHAR(30),
                                       IN p_value DOUBLE, IN p_quality VARCHAR(30))
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_device INT DEFAULT NULL;
    DECLARE v_sensor INT DEFAULT NULL;
 
    SELECT Device_ID INTO v_device FROM Device WHERE Hardware_ID = p_hardware_id;
    IF v_device IS NULL THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'KhÃ´ng xÃ¡c Ä‘á»‹nh Ä‘Æ°á»£c thiáº¿t bá»‹ theo Hardware_ID';
    END IF;
    SELECT Sensor_ID INTO v_sensor FROM Sensor
    WHERE Device_ID = v_device AND Data_Type = p_data_type LIMIT 1;
    IF v_sensor IS NULL THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'Thiáº¿t bá»‹ khÃ´ng cÃ³ cáº£m biáº¿n loáº¡i dá»¯ liá»‡u nÃ y';
    END IF;
    INSERT INTO Sensor_Record (`Value`, Data_quality, Sensor_ID) VALUES (p_value, p_quality, v_sensor);
END$$

DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_yolo_heartbeat$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_yolo_heartbeat(IN p_controller_id INT, IN p_firmware VARCHAR(30))
BEGIN
    UPDATE Yolo_Controller
    SET Lastest_working_time = NOW(), Connection_State = 'online',
        Firmware_version = COALESCE(p_firmware, Firmware_version)
    WHERE Controller_ID = p_controller_id;
END$$
 
-- Thiáº¿t bá»‹/bá»™ Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn khÃ´ng gá»­i tÃ­n hiá»‡u quÃ¡ p_timeout phÃºt -> offline
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_check_offline_devices$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_check_offline_devices(IN p_timeout_minutes INT)
BEGIN
    UPDATE Device SET Connection_State = 'offline'
    WHERE LOWER(Connection_State) = 'online' AND Last_Connection_Time IS NOT NULL
      AND Last_Connection_Time < NOW() - INTERVAL p_timeout_minutes MINUTE;
 
    UPDATE Yolo_Controller SET Connection_State = 'offline'
    WHERE LOWER(Connection_State) = 'online' AND Lastest_working_time IS NOT NULL
      AND Lastest_working_time < NOW() - INTERVAL p_timeout_minutes MINUTE;
END$$
 
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_get_home_dashboard$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_get_home_dashboard(IN p_actor_id INT, IN p_home_id INT)
BEGIN
    IF NOT (fn_has_permission(p_actor_id, p_home_id, 'DEVICE_VIEW')
            AND fn_has_permission(p_actor_id, p_home_id, 'SENSOR_VIEW')) THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n xem thÃ´ng tin nhÃ ';
    END IF;
 
    SELECT d.Device_ID, d.Device_Name, dt.Type_Name, d.Connection_State,
           d.Last_Connection_Time, ds.Current_State
    FROM Device d
    JOIN Device_Type dt ON dt.Type_Code = d.Type_Code
    LEFT JOIN Device_State ds ON ds.Device_ID = d.Device_ID
    WHERE d.Home_ID = p_home_id;
 
    SELECT s.Sensor_ID, d.Device_Name, s.Data_Type, s.Unit,
           fn_latest_sensor_value(s.Sensor_ID) AS Latest_Value
    FROM Sensor s JOIN Device d ON d.Device_ID = s.Device_ID
    WHERE d.Home_ID = p_home_id;
END$$
 
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_get_sensor_history$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_get_sensor_history(IN p_actor_id INT, IN p_sensor_id INT, IN p_limit INT)
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_home INT DEFAULT NULL;
    SELECT d.Home_ID INTO v_home FROM Sensor s JOIN Device d ON d.Device_ID = s.Device_ID
    WHERE s.Sensor_ID = p_sensor_id;
    IF v_home IS NULL OR NOT fn_has_permission(p_actor_id, v_home, 'SENSOR_VIEW') THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n xem dá»¯ liá»‡u cáº£m biáº¿n';
    END IF;
    SELECT Record_ID, `Value`, Data_quality FROM Sensor_Record
    WHERE Sensor_ID = p_sensor_id ORDER BY Record_ID DESC LIMIT p_limit;
END$$
 
-- ===== Cá»­a máº­t mÃ£ (6.6, 6.7) =====
-- Táº¡o PIN (chá»§ nhÃ  cáº¥p cho mÃ¬nh, thÃ nh viÃªn hoáº·c khÃ¡ch; khÃ¡ch nÃªn cÃ³ Valid_To)
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_create_access_credential$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_create_access_credential(IN p_actor_id INT, IN p_door VARCHAR(100), IN p_target_user_id INT,
                                             IN p_pin VARCHAR(100), IN p_valid_from DATETIME,
                                             IN p_valid_to DATETIME, OUT p_credential_id INT)
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_home INT DEFAULT NULL;
    SELECT Home_ID INTO v_home FROM Smart_Door WHERE Smart_Door_Name = p_door;
    IF v_home IS NULL THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'Cá»­a khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i';
    END IF;
    IF NOT fn_has_permission(p_actor_id, v_home, 'DOOR_MANAGE') THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n quáº£n lÃ½ cá»­a';
    END IF;
    IF p_pin NOT REGEXP '^[0-9]{4,8}$' THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'PIN pháº£i gá»“m 4-8 chá»¯ sá»‘';
    END IF;
    -- trigger trg_access_credential_bi kiá»ƒm tra thÃ nh viÃªn há»£p lá»‡ vÃ  khoáº£ng thá»i gian
    INSERT INTO Access_Credential (Credential_Value, Credential_State, Valid_From, Valid_To, User_ID, Smart_Door_Name)
    VALUES (fn_hash_pin(p_door, p_target_user_id, p_pin), 'active', p_valid_from, p_valid_to,
            p_target_user_id, p_door);
    SET p_credential_id = LAST_INSERT_ID();
    CALL sp_log_activity(p_actor_id, v_home, 'access_credential', 'create_pin', 'web', 'success');
END$$
 
-- Nháº­p PIN táº¡i cá»­a. p_result: success | failed | locked_out
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_verify_door_pin$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_verify_door_pin(IN p_door VARCHAR(100), IN p_pin VARCHAR(100),
                                    OUT p_result VARCHAR(20), OUT p_user_id INT)
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_home INT DEFAULT NULL;
    DECLARE v_user INT DEFAULT NULL;
 
    SELECT Home_ID INTO v_home FROM Smart_Door WHERE Smart_Door_Name = p_door;
    IF v_home IS NULL THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'Cá»­a khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i';
    END IF;
    SET p_user_id = NULL;
 
    IF fn_count_failed_attempts(p_door, 10) >= 5 THEN
        -- khÃ³a táº¡m: sai tá»« 5 láº§n trong 10 phÃºt
        INSERT INTO Access_Attempt (Verify_Method, Access_Result, Failure_Reason, Smart_Door_Name)
        VALUES ('pin', 'failed', 'locked_out', p_door);
        SET p_result = 'locked_out';
    ELSE
        SET v_user = fn_verify_pin(p_door, p_pin);
        IF v_user IS NULL THEN
            INSERT INTO Access_Attempt (Verify_Method, Access_Result, Failure_Reason, Smart_Door_Name)
            VALUES ('pin', 'failed', 'wrong_or_expired_pin', p_door);
            SET p_result = 'failed';
        ELSEIF NOT fn_has_permission(v_user, v_home, 'DOOR_UNLOCK') THEN
            INSERT INTO Access_Attempt (Verify_Method, Access_Result, Failure_Reason, User_ID, Smart_Door_Name)
            VALUES ('pin', 'failed', 'no_permission', v_user, p_door);
            SET p_result = 'failed';
        ELSE
            INSERT INTO Access_Attempt (Verify_Method, Access_Result, User_ID, Smart_Door_Name)
            VALUES ('pin', 'success', v_user, p_door);
            UPDATE Smart_Door SET Lock_Unlock_State = 'unlocked' WHERE Smart_Door_Name = p_door;
            CALL sp_log_activity(v_user, v_home, 'smart_door', 'unlock_by_pin', 'pin', 'success');
            SET p_result = 'success';
            SET p_user_id = v_user;
        END IF;
    END IF;
END$$
 
-- Chá»§ nhÃ  Ä‘á»•i máº­t mÃ£: xÃ¡c thá»±c mÃ£ hiá»‡n táº¡i -> nháº­p má»›i 2 láº§n -> cáº­p nháº­t -> ghi lá»‹ch sá»­
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_change_door_pin$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_change_door_pin(IN p_actor_id INT, IN p_credential_id INT, IN p_current_pin VARCHAR(100),
                                    IN p_new_pin VARCHAR(100), IN p_confirm_pin VARCHAR(100))
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_door VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT NULL;
    DECLARE v_user INT DEFAULT NULL;
    DECLARE v_hash CHAR(64) DEFAULT NULL;
    DECLARE v_home INT DEFAULT NULL;
 
    SELECT Smart_Door_Name, User_ID, Credential_Value INTO v_door, v_user, v_hash
    FROM Access_Credential WHERE Credential_ID = p_credential_id;
    IF v_door IS NULL THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'Máº­t mÃ£ khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i';
    END IF;
    SELECT Home_ID INTO v_home FROM Smart_Door WHERE Smart_Door_Name = v_door;
 
    IF NOT fn_has_permission(p_actor_id, v_home, 'DOOR_MANAGE') THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'Chá»‰ chá»§ nhÃ  má»›i Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘á»•i máº­t mÃ£';
    END IF;
    IF v_hash <> fn_hash_pin(v_door, v_user, p_current_pin) THEN
        CALL sp_log_activity(p_actor_id, v_home, 'access_credential', 'change_pin', 'web', 'failed');
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'Máº­t mÃ£ hiá»‡n táº¡i khÃ´ng Ä‘Ãºng';
    END IF;
    IF p_new_pin <> p_confirm_pin THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'Hai láº§n nháº­p máº­t mÃ£ má»›i khÃ´ng khá»›p';
    END IF;
    IF p_new_pin NOT REGEXP '^[0-9]{4,8}$' THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'PIN pháº£i gá»“m 4-8 chá»¯ sá»‘';
    END IF;
    IF p_new_pin = p_current_pin THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'Máº­t mÃ£ má»›i pháº£i khÃ¡c máº­t mÃ£ hiá»‡n táº¡i';
    END IF;
 
    UPDATE Access_Credential SET Credential_Value = fn_hash_pin(v_door, v_user, p_new_pin)
    WHERE Credential_ID = p_credential_id;
    CALL sp_log_activity(p_actor_id, v_home, 'access_credential', 'change_pin', 'web', 'success');
END$$
 
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_revoke_credential$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_revoke_credential(IN p_actor_id INT, IN p_credential_id INT)
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_home INT DEFAULT NULL;
    SELECT sd.Home_ID INTO v_home
    FROM Access_Credential ac JOIN Smart_Door sd ON sd.Smart_Door_Name = ac.Smart_Door_Name
    WHERE ac.Credential_ID = p_credential_id;
    IF v_home IS NULL THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'Máº­t mÃ£ khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i';
    END IF;
    IF NOT fn_has_permission(p_actor_id, v_home, 'DOOR_MANAGE') THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n quáº£n lÃ½ cá»­a';
    END IF;
    UPDATE Access_Credential SET Credential_State = 'revoked' WHERE Credential_ID = p_credential_id;
    CALL sp_log_activity(p_actor_id, v_home, 'access_credential', 'revoke_pin', 'web', 'success');
END$$
 
-- Chá»§ nhÃ  má»Ÿ/khÃ³a cá»­a tá»« xa
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_set_door_state$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_set_door_state(IN p_actor_id INT, IN p_door VARCHAR(100), IN p_state VARCHAR(20))
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_home INT DEFAULT NULL;
    SELECT Home_ID INTO v_home FROM Smart_Door WHERE Smart_Door_Name = p_door;
    IF v_home IS NULL THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'Cá»­a khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i';
    END IF;
    IF LOWER(p_state) NOT IN ('locked', 'unlocked') THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i pháº£i lÃ  locked hoáº·c unlocked';
    END IF;
    IF NOT fn_has_permission(p_actor_id, v_home, 'DOOR_MANAGE') THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn cá»­a tá»« xa';
    END IF;
 
    UPDATE Smart_Door SET Lock_Unlock_State = LOWER(p_state) WHERE Smart_Door_Name = p_door;
    IF LOWER(p_state) = 'unlocked' THEN
        INSERT INTO Access_Attempt (Verify_Method, Access_Result, User_ID, Smart_Door_Name)
        VALUES ('remote', 'success', p_actor_id, p_door);
    END IF;
    CALL sp_log_activity(p_actor_id, v_home, 'smart_door', CONCAT('set_', LOWER(p_state)), 'remote', 'success');
END$$
 
-- ===== FaceAI (6.9) =====
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_register_face_profile$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_register_face_profile(IN p_actor_id INT, IN p_home_id INT, IN p_target_user_id INT,
                                          IN p_valid_from DATETIME, IN p_valid_to DATETIME,
                                          OUT p_profile_id INT)
BEGIN
    IF NOT fn_has_permission(p_actor_id, p_home_id, 'FACE_MANAGE') THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n quáº£n lÃ½ FaceAI';
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM Face_Profile WHERE User_ID = p_target_user_id) THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'NgÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng Ä‘Ã£ cÃ³ FaceProfile';
    END IF;
    INSERT INTO Face_Profile (Valid_From, Valid_To, User_ID, Home_ID)
    VALUES (p_valid_from, p_valid_to, p_target_user_id, p_home_id);
    SET p_profile_id = LAST_INSERT_ID();
    CALL sp_log_activity(p_actor_id, p_home_id, 'face_profile', 'register_face', 'web', 'success');
END$$
 
-- KÃ­ch hoáº¡t / vÃ´ hiá»‡u hÃ³a khuÃ´n máº·t báº±ng cÃ¡ch Ä‘áº·t láº¡i khoáº£ng hiá»‡u lá»±c
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_set_face_profile_validity$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_set_face_profile_validity(IN p_actor_id INT, IN p_profile_id INT,
                                              IN p_valid_from DATETIME, IN p_valid_to DATETIME)
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_home INT DEFAULT NULL;
    SELECT Home_ID INTO v_home FROM Face_Profile WHERE Face_Profile_ID = p_profile_id;
    IF v_home IS NULL THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'FaceProfile khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i';
    END IF;
    IF NOT fn_has_permission(p_actor_id, v_home, 'FACE_MANAGE') THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n quáº£n lÃ½ FaceAI';
    END IF;
    UPDATE Face_Profile SET Valid_From = p_valid_from, Valid_To = p_valid_to
    WHERE Face_Profile_ID = p_profile_id;
    CALL sp_log_activity(p_actor_id, v_home, 'face_profile', 'update_validity', 'web', 'success');
END$$
 
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_delete_face_profile$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_delete_face_profile(IN p_actor_id INT, IN p_profile_id INT)
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_home INT DEFAULT NULL;
    SELECT Home_ID INTO v_home FROM Face_Profile WHERE Face_Profile_ID = p_profile_id;
    IF v_home IS NULL THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'FaceProfile khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i';
    END IF;
    IF NOT fn_has_permission(p_actor_id, v_home, 'FACE_MANAGE') THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n quáº£n lÃ½ FaceAI';
    END IF;
    DELETE FROM Face_Profile WHERE Face_Profile_ID = p_profile_id;
    CALL sp_log_activity(p_actor_id, v_home, 'face_profile', 'delete_face', 'web', 'success');
END$$
 
-- AI service tráº£ káº¿t quáº£ nháº­n diá»‡n táº¡i cá»­a. p_reliability: 0..100. p_decision: allowed | denied
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_record_face_recognition$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_record_face_recognition(IN p_door VARCHAR(100), IN p_face_profile_id INT,
                                            IN p_label VARCHAR(100), IN p_reliability DECIMAL(5,2),
                                            IN p_threshold DECIMAL(5,2), OUT p_decision VARCHAR(10))
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_home INT DEFAULT NULL;
    DECLARE v_prof_home INT DEFAULT NULL;
    DECLARE v_user INT DEFAULT NULL;
    DECLARE v_from DATETIME DEFAULT NULL;
    DECLARE v_to DATETIME DEFAULT NULL;
    DECLARE v_result VARCHAR(50);
    DECLARE v_reason VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT NULL;
    DECLARE v_profile INT DEFAULT p_face_profile_id;
    DECLARE EXIT HANDLER FOR SQLEXCEPTION
    BEGIN
        ROLLBACK;
        RESIGNAL;
    END;
 
    SELECT Home_ID INTO v_home FROM Smart_Door WHERE Smart_Door_Name = p_door;
    IF v_home IS NULL THEN
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'Cá»­a khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i';
    END IF;
 
    IF v_profile IS NOT NULL THEN
        SELECT User_ID, Valid_From, Valid_To, Home_ID INTO v_user, v_from, v_to, v_prof_home
        FROM Face_Profile WHERE Face_Profile_ID = v_profile;
        IF v_prof_home IS NULL OR v_prof_home <> v_home THEN
            SET v_profile = NULL;      -- khuÃ´n máº·t khÃ´ng thuá»™c nhÃ  nÃ y -> coi nhÆ° ngÆ°á»i láº¡
            SET v_user = NULL;
        END IF;
    END IF;
 
    IF v_profile IS NULL THEN
        SET v_result = 'unknown';         SET v_reason = 'unknown_face';
    ELSEIF (v_from IS NOT NULL AND v_from > NOW()) OR (v_to IS NOT NULL AND v_to < NOW()) THEN
        SET v_result = 'expired';         SET v_reason = 'profile_expired';
    ELSEIF p_reliability < p_threshold THEN
        SET v_result = 'low_confidence';  SET v_reason = 'low_confidence';
    ELSEIF NOT fn_has_permission(v_user, v_home, 'DOOR_UNLOCK') THEN
        SET v_result = 'no_permission';   SET v_reason = 'no_permission';
    ELSE
        SET v_result = 'recognized';
    END IF;
 
    START TRANSACTION;
    INSERT INTO Face_Recognition_Event (Reliability, Label, Recognization_Result,
                                        Face_Profile_ID, Smart_Door_Name, Home_ID)
    VALUES (p_reliability, p_label, v_result, v_profile, p_door, v_home);
 
    IF v_reason IS NULL THEN
        INSERT INTO Access_Attempt (Verify_Method, Access_Result, User_ID, Smart_Door_Name)
        VALUES ('face', 'success', v_user, p_door);
        UPDATE Smart_Door SET Lock_Unlock_State = 'unlocked' WHERE Smart_Door_Name = p_door;
        CALL sp_log_activity(v_user, v_home, 'smart_door', 'unlock_by_face', 'face', 'success');
        SET p_decision = 'allowed';
    ELSE
        INSERT INTO Access_Attempt (Verify_Method, Access_Result, Failure_Reason, User_ID, Smart_Door_Name)
        VALUES ('face', 'failed', v_reason, v_user, p_door);
        SET p_decision = 'denied';
    END IF;
    COMMIT;
END$$
 
-- ===== Giá»ng nÃ³i (6.8) =====
-- ÄÃ£ cÃ³ vÄƒn báº£n + thiáº¿t bá»‹ + dá»¯ liá»‡u lá»‡nh sau bÆ°á»›c speech-to-text/phÃ¢n tÃ­ch tá»« khÃ³a.
-- KhÃ´ng xÃ¡c Ä‘á»‹nh Ä‘Æ°á»£c thiáº¿t bá»‹ hoáº·c khÃ´ng Ä‘á»§ quyá»n -> 'unsupported' (trigger sáº½ cáº£nh bÃ¡o).
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_process_voice_command$$
DELIMITER $$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_process_voice_command(
    IN p_user_id INT,
    IN p_home_id INT,
    IN p_command_detail TEXT,
    IN p_device_name VARCHAR(255),
    IN p_command_data JSON,
    IN p_language VARCHAR(20),
    OUT p_voice_id INT,
    OUT p_state VARCHAR(30)
)
BEGIN
    DECLARE v_device_id INT DEFAULT NULL;
    DECLARE v_has_permission BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE;

    -- 1. TÃ¬m Device_ID dá»±a theo tÃªn thiáº¿t bá»‹ trong nhÃ 
    SELECT Device_ID INTO v_device_id 
    FROM Device 
    WHERE Home_ID = p_home_id AND Device_Name = p_device_name 
    LIMIT 1;

    -- 2. Kiá»ƒm tra quyá»n VOICE_USE cá»§a ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng
    SET v_has_permission = fn_has_permission(p_user_id, p_home_id, 'VOICE_USE');

    IF v_device_id IS NULL OR NOT v_has_permission THEN
        SET p_state = 'unsupported';
    ELSE
        SET p_state = 'sent';
    END IF;

    -- 3. LÆ°u vÃ o Voice_Command vá»›i Ä‘Ãºng cá»™t Working_State
    INSERT INTO Voice_Command (
        Command_Detail, 
        Working_State, 
        Device, 
        User_ID, 
        Home_ID, 
        Command_Data, 
        `Language`
    ) VALUES (
        p_command_detail, 
        p_state, 
        v_device_id, 
        p_user_id, 
        p_home_id, 
        p_command_data, 
        p_language
    );

    -- 4. Láº¥y ID vá»«a sinh ra
    SET p_voice_id = LAST_INSERT_ID();
END$$

-- ===== ThÃ´ng bÃ¡o & lá»‹ch sá»­ (6.10) =====
-- p_notification_id = NULL -> Ä‘Ã¡nh dáº¥u Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘á»c táº¥t cáº£
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_mark_notifications_read$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_mark_notifications_read(IN p_user_id INT, IN p_notification_id INT)
BEGIN
    UPDATE Notification SET Unread_Read = 'Read'
    WHERE User_ID = p_user_id AND Unread_Read = 'Unread'
      AND (p_notification_id IS NULL OR Notification_ID = p_notification_id);
END$$
 
-- Chá»§ nhÃ  xem toÃ n bá»™; thÃ nh viÃªn chá»‰ xem lá»‹ch sá»­ cá»§a chÃ­nh mÃ¬nh
DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS sp_get_activity_history$$
CREATE PROCEDURE sp_get_activity_history(IN p_actor_id INT, IN p_home_id INT, IN p_limit INT)
BEGIN
    IF fn_has_permission(p_actor_id, p_home_id, 'LOG_VIEW_ALL') THEN
        SELECT Activity_Log_ID, User_ID, Entity_Type, `Action`, `Source`, `Result`, `Time`
        FROM Activity_Log WHERE Home_ID = p_home_id
        ORDER BY Activity_Log_ID DESC LIMIT p_limit;
    ELSEIF fn_has_permission(p_actor_id, p_home_id, 'LOG_VIEW_OWN') THEN
        SELECT Activity_Log_ID, User_ID, Entity_Type, `Action`, `Source`, `Result`, `Time`
        FROM Activity_Log WHERE Home_ID = p_home_id AND User_ID = p_actor_id
        ORDER BY Activity_Log_ID DESC LIMIT p_limit;
    ELSE
        SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = 'KhÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n xem lá»‹ch sá»­';
    END IF;
END$$

DELIMITER ;

SELECT r.Role_ID, p.Permission_ID FROM Role r CROSS JOIN Permission p
WHERE r.Role_Name = 'Owner';

-- Member: giÃ¡m sÃ¡t, Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn thiáº¿t bá»‹, giá»ng nÃ³i, thÃ´ng bÃ¡o, lá»‹ch sá»­ cá»§a mÃ¬nh
INSERT IGNORE INTO Role_Permission (Role_ID, Permission_ID)
SELECT r.Role_ID, p.Permission_ID FROM Role r CROSS JOIN Permission p
WHERE r.Role_Name = 'Member'
  AND p.Permission_Code IN ('DEVICE_VIEW','DEVICE_CONTROL','SENSOR_VIEW','DOOR_UNLOCK',
                            'VOICE_USE','LOG_VIEW_OWN','NOTIFICATION_VIEW');
                            
-- Guest: chá»‰ Ä‘Æ°á»£c má»Ÿ cá»­a (PIN/Face táº¡m thá»i), khÃ´ng Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn thiáº¿t bá»‹, khÃ´ng xem thÃ´ng tin nhÃ 
INSERT IGNORE INTO Role_Permission (Role_ID, Permission_ID)
SELECT r.Role_ID, p.Permission_ID FROM Role r CROSS JOIN Permission p
WHERE r.Role_Name = 'Guest' AND p.Permission_Code IN ('DOOR_UNLOCK');



==================== FILE: C:\Users\Dat\OneDrive\Documents\YoloHome\Database\smart_home.sql ====================

DROP DATABASE IF EXISTS smart_home;
CREATE DATABASE smart_home CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci;
USE smart_home;

CREATE TABLE `User` (
	User_ID INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    `Name` VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    Email VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    `Password` VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,       -- SAVE HASH
    Display_name VARCHAR(100),
    Account_state VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Active',
    Time_account_created DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE Home (
	Home_ID INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    Home_Name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    Address VARCHAR(255),
    `State` VARCHAR(30),
    Time_Home_Created DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    Owner_ID INT NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_home_owner FOREIGN KEY (Owner_ID)
		REFERENCES `User`(User_ID) ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE Home_Updated_Time (
	Home_ID INT NOT NULL,
    Updated_Time DATETIME NOT NULL,
    PRIMARY KEY (Home_ID, Updated_Time),
    CONSTRAINT fk_hut_home FOREIGN KEY (Home_ID)
		REFERENCES Home(Home_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE Role (
	Role_ID INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    Role_Name VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE
);

CREATE TABLE Permission (
	Permission_ID INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    Permission_Code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    Permission_Name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    Permission_Module VARCHAR(50),
    Permission_Description VARCHAR(255)
);

CREATE TABLE Role_Permission (
    Role_ID INT NOT NULL,
    Permission_ID INT NOT NULL,
    PRIMARY KEY (Role_ID, Permission_ID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_rp_role FOREIGN KEY (Role_ID)
        REFERENCES Role(Role_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT fk_rp_permission FOREIGN KEY (Permission_ID)
        REFERENCES Permission(Permission_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE Home_Member (
    Home_Member_ID INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    `Status` VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
    Joined DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    Expired DATETIME NULL,
    User_ID INT NOT NULL,
    Home_ID INT NOT NULL,
    Role_ID INT NOT NULL,
    UNIQUE KEY uq_member (User_ID, Home_ID),
    CONSTRAINT fk_hm_user FOREIGN KEY (User_ID)
        REFERENCES `User`(User_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT fk_hm_home FOREIGN KEY (Home_ID)
        REFERENCES Home(Home_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT fk_hm_role FOREIGN KEY (Role_ID)
        REFERENCES Role(Role_ID) ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE Notification (
    Notification_ID INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    Title VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
    `Type` VARCHAR(50),
    Content TEXT,
    `Level` VARCHAR(30),
    Unread_Read ENUM('Unread','Read') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Unread',
    User_ID INT NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_noti_user FOREIGN KEY (User_ID)
        REFERENCES `User`(User_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE Activity_Log (
    Activity_Log_ID INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    Entity_Type VARCHAR(50),
    `Action` VARCHAR(100),
    `Source` VARCHAR(50),
    `Result` VARCHAR(50),
    `Time` DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    User_ID INT NOT NULL,
    Home_ID INT NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_al_user FOREIGN KEY (User_ID)
        REFERENCES `User`(User_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT fk_al_home FOREIGN KEY (Home_ID)
        REFERENCES Home(Home_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE Smart_Door (
    Smart_Door_Name VARCHAR(100) PRIMARY KEY,
    Working_State VARCHAR(30),
    Lock_Unlock_State ENUM('Locked','Unlocked') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Locked',
    Home_ID INT NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_door_home FOREIGN KEY (Home_ID)
        REFERENCES Home(Home_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE Face_Profile (
    Face_Profile_ID INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    Face_Name VARCHAR(100) NULL COMMENT 'TÃªn nháº­n dáº¡ng khuÃ´n máº·t',
    Face_Data JSON NULL COMMENT 'Vector 128D/512D khuÃ´n máº·t',
    Valid_From DATETIME NULL,
    Valid_To DATETIME NULL,
    Created_At DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    User_ID INT NOT NULL,
    Home_ID INT NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_fp_user FOREIGN KEY (User_ID)
        REFERENCES `User`(User_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT fk_fp_home FOREIGN KEY (Home_ID)
        REFERENCES Home(Home_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT uq_user_home UNIQUE (User_ID, Home_ID)
) CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

CREATE TABLE Face_Recognition_Event (
    FaceRegEvent_ID INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    Reliability DECIMAL(5,2),
    Label VARCHAR(100),
    Recognization_Result VARCHAR(50),
    `Time` DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    Face_Profile_ID INT NULL,                        -- cÃ³ thá»ƒ lÃ  ngÆ°á»i láº¡
    Smart_Door_Name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    Home_ID INT NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_fre_profile FOREIGN KEY (Face_Profile_ID)
        REFERENCES Face_Profile(Face_Profile_ID) ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT fk_fre_door FOREIGN KEY (Smart_Door_Name)
        REFERENCES Smart_Door(Smart_Door_Name) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE,
	CONSTRAINT fk_fre_home FOREIGN KEY (Home_ID)
        REFERENCES Home(Home_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE Access_Credential (
    Credential_ID INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    Credential_Value VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    Credential_State VARCHAR(30)  NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
    Valid_From DATETIME,
    Valid_To DATETIME,
    User_ID INT NOT NULL,
    Smart_Door_Name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_ac_user FOREIGN KEY (User_ID)
        REFERENCES `User`(User_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT fk_ac_door FOREIGN KEY (Smart_Door_Name)
        REFERENCES Smart_Door(Smart_Door_Name) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE Access_Attempt (
    Access_ID INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    Verify_Method VARCHAR(50),
    Access_Result VARCHAR(30),
    `Time` DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    Failure_Reason VARCHAR(255),
    User_ID INT NULL,
    Smart_Door_Name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_aa_user FOREIGN KEY (User_ID)
        REFERENCES `User`(User_ID) ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT fk_aa_door FOREIGN KEY (Smart_Door_Name)
        REFERENCES Smart_Door(Smart_Door_Name) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE Device_Type (
    Type_Code VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,
    Type_Name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    Type_Group VARCHAR(50),
    `Description` VARCHAR(255),
    Supported BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    Manufacturer VARCHAR(100)
);

CREATE TABLE Yolo_Controller (
    Controller_ID INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    Controller_Name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    Lastest_working_time DATETIME,
    Connection_State VARCHAR(30),
    Firmware_version VARCHAR(30)
);

CREATE TABLE Device (
    Device_ID INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    Device_Name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    Hardware_ID VARCHAR(100) UNIQUE,
    Serial_No VARCHAR(100),
    Connection_State VARCHAR(30),
    Last_Connection_Time DATETIME,
    Available_working_State VARCHAR(30),
    Home_ID INT NOT NULL,
    Type_Code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    Controller_ID INT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_dev_home FOREIGN KEY (Home_ID)
        REFERENCES Home(Home_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT fk_dev_type FOREIGN KEY (Type_Code)
        REFERENCES Device_Type(Type_Code) ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT fk_dev_ctrl FOREIGN KEY (Controller_ID)
        REFERENCES Yolo_Controller(Controller_ID) ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE Device_State (
    Device_State_ID INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    Current_State VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    Changed_At DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    Device_ID INT NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_ds_dev FOREIGN KEY (Device_ID)
        REFERENCES Device(Device_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE Device_Command (
    Command_ID INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    Source_ID INT,
    Source_Type ENUM('USER','VOICE_COMMAND','SCHEDULE','SYSTEM') NOT NULL,
    Data_attached JSON,
    Excute_Time DATETIME,
    Excute_State VARCHAR(30),
    Device_ID INT NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_dc_dev FOREIGN KEY (Device_ID)
        REFERENCES Device(Device_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE Voice_Command (
    Voice_Command_ID INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    Command_Detail VARCHAR(255),
    Device INT NULL,                        
    Command_Data JSON,
    Working_State VARCHAR(30),
    `Language` VARCHAR(20),
    User_ID INT NOT NULL,
    Home_ID INT NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_vc_user FOREIGN KEY (User_ID)
        REFERENCES `User`(User_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT fk_vc_home FOREIGN KEY (Home_ID)
        REFERENCES Home(Home_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE Sensor (
    Sensor_ID INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    Sampling_Cycle INT COMMENT 'giÃ¢y',
    Working_State VARCHAR(30),
    Data_Type VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    Unit VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    Device_ID INT NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_sensor_dev FOREIGN KEY (Device_ID)
        REFERENCES Device(Device_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE
);
 
CREATE TABLE Sensor_Record (
    Record_ID BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    `Value` DOUBLE NOT NULL,
    Data_quality VARCHAR(30),
    Recorded_At DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    Sensor_ID INT NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_sr_sensor FOREIGN KEY (Sensor_ID)
        REFERENCES Sensor(Sensor_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE System_Event (
    Event_ID INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    Connection_State VARCHAR(30),
    Description VARCHAR(255),
    Server_State VARCHAR(30),
    `Time` DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);
 
CREATE INDEX idx_activity_time ON Activity_Log(`Time`);
CREATE INDEX idx_access_time ON Access_Attempt(`Time`);
CREATE INDEX idx_fre_time ON Face_Recognition_Event(`Time`);
CREATE INDEX idx_device_home ON Device(Home_ID);
CREATE INDEX idx_sensor_record ON Sensor_Record(Sensor_ID, Recorded_At);
CREATE INDEX idx_device_state ON Device_State(Device_ID, Changed_At);
CREATE INDEX idx_hm_home ON Home_Member(Home_ID);
CREATE INDEX idx_notification_unread ON Notification(User_ID, Unread_Read);
CREATE INDEX idx_device_command_history ON Device_Command(Device_ID, Excute_Time);
CREATE INDEX idx_access_credential ON Access_Credential(Smart_Door_Name, Credential_State);
